Living reference. Paste the relevant sections at the top of every spec so the agent does not re-derive them.

`BOLO_GPT_AUDIO_VOICE` "shimmer" is untouched, the two constants must stay separate because gpt-audio and gpt-4o-mini-tts have different voice sets; audited: chat replies never read or write `tts_cache` (only `ttsPrewarm.ts` and `routes/openai.ts` touch it, and the /openai/tts VOICES allowlist never included sage, so no cache entry can pin sage); api-server suite 628 tests, 614 pass, 14 fail, exactly the documented #782 baseline in section 8), and Task 900 (free-taste phrases on the journey taste station, July 31, 2026: server-only fix in `artifacts/api-server/src/routes/learning.ts`; `GET /lesson-groups/:id/phrases` now carries the same M1 teaser exception as the category-phrases route, so a teaser-state caller requesting the designated taste group gets the teaser rows instead of a 402; see the section 4 paragraph under the D1b showroom entry; api-server suite 632 tests, 618 pass, 14 fail, exactly the documented #782 baseline in section 8), and Task 899 (web train size bump + boarding-pass stub tear, July 31, 2026: the redrawn train renders ~16-18% larger through new :root tuning vars in `gujarati-coach/src/index.css` (`--train-ticket-h` 47px / `--train-ticket-h-lg` 65px on the home pass, was h-10/lg:h-14; `--train-marker-w` 37px inside the journey rail-marker pill, whose pill grew w-10 h-7 to w-[46px] h-8); measured before/after via `qa/task899-tear-probe.mjs`: home mobile 61x40 to 72x47, desktop 87x57 to 102x67, marker drawing 32x21 to 37x24, text-block gap unchanged at 12px, no marker overflow, no detail smearing at 2x. Activating the home pass now plays a both-halves tear: `handlePassActivate` in home.tsx preventDefaults, applies `.animate-stub-tear` to the fare-zone stub and `.animate-body-tear` to the ticket body, then `navigate("/journey")` after `--tear-nav-delay` parsed from CSS in ms (JS fallback 500ms). Navigation is never blocked: reduced motion returns early for instant native Link navigation, any animation-path error falls through to an immediate navigate(), and keyboard Enter rides the same click path so it gets the tear too. User-tuned tear look over three feedback rounds (final): both halves pull away from each other along the perforation, each carrying its own accent stock, rounded outer corners, a STATIC jagged clip-path rip edge, and a STATIC drop-shadow filter (box-shadow is useless there: clip-path clips it; the animation itself stays transforms/opacity only); during the tear the Link container goes transparent and drops overflow-hidden/shadow while TicketStripes, shimmer, blur-circle, perforation, and pass-glow are all hidden, so the widening gap shows the ACTUAL page behind the pass and the ticket reads as floating pieces (round 2's translucent-black recess and round 1's page-colored underlay strip were both rejected on the way here). `--tear-nav-delay` is deliberately 500ms, above the original ~250ms plan budget, per two explicit user requests for a longer visible tear. All tear timing/rotation/distance/drop vars live in the :root tuning constants block. The journey header ticket is untouched (tear classes only ever applied in home.tsx). Web suite 252 to 255 tests, all green; 3 new pins in `home-boarding-pass.test.tsx` (tear-plus-navigation, reduced-motion instant nav, getComputedStyle-failure fallback); mid-tear screenshot captured at `qa/shots/task899/mid-tear.png`), and Task 917 (directional pulse on the web journey map's active rail segment, July 31, 2026: `journey.tsx` samples `PULSE_DOTS_PER_SEG` (8) accent dots per segment directly on the rail beziers (same control points as the `segs` `d` strings) along the run from the current stop to the NEXT STATION in path order; a zone-boundary run spans two segments (station to postcard, postcard to station) with one continuous dot order, and no next station (final stop before the terminus, journey complete, showroom with no boardable stop) yields zero dots, so nothing ever pulses toward the terminus and a LOCKED next stop still pulses (test-out invitation). Mechanism is the tie-mark opacity wave, NOT mobile's dash-offset loop (web constraint: transforms/opacity only): each dot carries an inline `--rail-pulse-delay` fraction growing toward the next stop, and the `.rail-pulse-dot` CSS animation (base opacity 0, no fill mode, keyframes `rail-pulse`) phases on it, so the wave direction is correct on every serpentine orientation by construction and the delays are assertable in jsdom. Constants `--rail-pulse-cycle` 3.4s / `--rail-pulse-min` 0 / `--rail-pulse-peak` 0.85 joined the `:root` tuning block; reduced motion renders no dots at all (component gate via `useReducedMotion`) with the CSS reset as backstop. Web suite 310 to 315 tests via new `journey-rail-pulse.test.tsx` (5 pins: active-segment-only count, locked next stop, two-segment zone-boundary run with strictly increasing delays, no-next-station zero dots, journey-complete zero dots, reduced-motion zero dots); live frames at `qa/shots/task917/`; see the RESOLVED web-rail-pulse row in section 8).

**Maintenance rule:** after every completed task, append what changed. Anything in here that turns out to be wrong is worth more than the correction itself, because it means a spec was written against it.

Last updated after: Free-tier content policy + pronunciation evaluation gate (Aug 2, 2026, owner-ruled): Free serves the FULL Hindi Fare Zone 1 (every hi Greetings lesson group, phrase AND sentence stage, premium flipped false) and every language's FIRST stop (lowest-position Greetings group, non-premium phrase rows) is fully playable free — serve, practice, and evaluate — whatever the teaser state; everything past Stop 1 keeps byte-identical 402s. Data path is dual: reversible dev migration `lib/db/drizzle/0035_free_tier_content_policy.sql` (journal idx 35, documented inverse in comments) plus idempotent startup reconciliation `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/freeTierContentPolicy.ts` (runs from startupSeed under the advisory lock; authoritative for prod since publish syncs schema not data; excludes `__`-prefixed test languages so dev boots never flip live test fixtures; invariants pinned in `freeTierContentPolicy.test.ts`). Server: `getFirstStopGroup`/`__resetTeaserCacheForTests` in `lib/teaser.ts`; `denyLockedLanguage` gained `firstStopPhraseId`/`firstStopGroupId` grant opts; six regions of `routes/learning.ts` (category-phrases + group-phrases first-stop branches, phrase-list premium filter, per-plan sentence-row filtering with 402 feature_locked only when zero rows remain, `/phrases/:id`, attempts meter counts teaser consumption ONLY when state is teaser AND the phrase is in the teaser set — first-stop attempts insert plainly, and journey listing marks teaserStation for BOTH teaser and exhausted states). SECURITY (architect-review catch): `POST /openai/pronunciation` previously accepted ANY catalog phraseId, leaking stored text into the response and signed evaluation token past every serving gate; it now runs the exact `/phrases/:id` gate (denyLockedLanguage with both id opts, then premium → `denyLockedFeature("extendedLibrary", …)`) BEFORE stored text is touched. Byte-identical-denial + first-stop carve-out + post-exhaustion pins live in `openai.pronunciation.gating.test.ts`; the fast-path and language-hint suites' stub apps now set `resolvedPlan={plan:"plus", chosenLanguage:null}` so the gate short-circuits "allowed" before any teaser DB lookup (gating has its own suite). In the real app the openai router already mounts behind requireAuth + loadEntitlements (`routes/index.ts`), so the gate reads a resolved plan in production. Clients: web + mobile journey gate sentence stops on server `planLocked` ONLY (stage-based Plus gating and useEntitlements/isPlus removed from journey.tsx + useJourneyProgress.ts on BOTH platforms); a non-planLocked sentence stop boards free (mobile pin in `__tests__/journey-map.test.tsx`). one_language gained NO new logic anywhere — it rides `featuresForPlan` exactly like Free at every new decision point (sentence filtering, per-row premium filter, first-stop carve-out, pronunciation gate); family resolves to plan "plus" in `lib/entitlements.ts`, so All-Access treatment is inherited unchanged. Visual leftover: sentence stops still render the ALL-ACCESS diamond chip even where their rows now serve free. Gate per brief: typecheck clean, targeted suites only (teaser/showroom/sentences/policy/plan-visible/lesson-groups/attempts batch green after the showroom sentence deep-link repin; pronunciation gating 4/4; fast-path + language-hint green; mobile journey-map 17/17; the pilot-capture unlisted-user failure is the documented PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS env baseline), no full suite.

Last updated after: WebKit element-blessing autoplay fix (Aug 2, 2026): on-device trace proved the WebAudio primer was a NO-OP for coach playback: WebKit (iPhone Safari / WKWebView) blesses playback PER MECHANISM and PER ELEMENT, so a silent-WebAudio-buffer gesture unlock blesses only WebAudio, and the coach HTMLAudioElement's play() still rejected NotAllowedError 132ms after the gesture with userActivation already consumed. The mechanism that works, and the standing contract: ALL FOUR programmatic voice surfaces (coach phrase, meaning segment, band call-out, spoken feedback) route through persistent module-scope singleton elements in `src/lib/iosAudio.ts` (`getCoachAudioElement` / `getMeaningAudioElement` / `getBandAudioElement` / `getFeedbackAudioElement`, src swapped per clip; band-audio.ts swaps src per band), and every session entry gesture calls `blessAudioPlayback()`, which plays a ~1ms silent WAV through EACH singleton inside the gesture so WebKit remembers the blessing per element. Do NOT refactor playback back to per-phrase `new Audio(...)` EVER (a fresh element carries no blessing and first-phrase autoplay regresses on iOS), and do not reintroduce a WebAudio primer or a bless-once flag. `primeAudioUnlock` is retired; nothing needs a WebAudio gesture unlock (tear SFX is WebAudio but plays inside its own gesture). Element hygiene: session cleanup nulls `onended`, and blessing nulls `onended` AND `onerror`, so stale handlers never fire on a later silent blessing play; blessing skips an element that is actually playing. Exemptions from the never-per-play rule: preload-only elements that never call play() (band clip cache warming) and elements played directly inside their own gesture (own-recording playback on the compare screen). Pins: `src/test/ios-audio-bless.test.ts` (four distinct singletons, ALL blessed on every call, stale-handler clearing, busy-skip) and the meaning replay test asserts NO new Audio instances ever appear. Test infra: the singletons reset per test via `__resetBlessedAudioElementsForTests()` in `src/test/setup.ts` beforeEach, or instance counts leak across a file's Audio mocks. Gate per brief: typecheck clean, targeted tests only, no full suite.

Last updated after: back-navigation first-phrase autoplay fix (Aug 2, 2026) [SUPERSEDED same day by the element-blessing entry above; the re-entrant WebAudio primer proved a no-op for coach playback on device]: `primeAudioUnlock` in `src/lib/iosAudio.ts` is now INTENTIONALLY RE-ENTRANT — the once-per-page-load `audioUnlocked` flag was the spec's own flaw and was removed, because WKWebView can deactivate the page's audio session between SPA sessions (leave a lesson, come back), so a re-entry gesture must replay the one-sample silent buffer or the first coach autoplay rejects and the phrase stays unspoken until the replay tap; the AudioContext is closed right after the buffer, so per-gesture calls stay ~free — do NOT "optimize" a once-flag back in. Every entry path into practice now primes on its triggering gesture: journey station rows/polish/lock-modal, home hero pass and links, all four category-detail links, and (newly primed, was the one gap) the progress-page Retake link. The play()-rejection degrade path is unchanged and was already visible (speaker-button ring pulse + "The phrase audio didn't play" status line). Pin: `src/test/ios-audio-prime.test.ts` asserts a fresh buffer starts on EVERY call (3 calls = 3 buffers) and a missing AudioContext never throws. Caveat: diagnosis and fix are code-read + unit-pinned; the WKWebView repro itself is device-only. Gate per brief: typecheck clean, targeted tests only (primer pin + progress-retake-link, 7/7), no full suite.

Last updated after: #1090 (paywall Terms/Privacy links pinned to exact URLs, Aug 13, 2026): both paywalls now link Apple's Standard EULA (`https://www.apple.com/legal/internet-services/itunes/dev/stdeula/`) and `https://bolo-india.app/privacy` as hardcoded absolute literals — mobile in `lib/legal.ts` (no longer built from `EXPO_PUBLIC_DOMAIN`), web in `pages/upgrade.tsx` as external `target="_blank"` anchors instead of wouter routes. Both tests pin the URLs whole-string. See section 10ar (it supersedes the URL half of 10aq).

Last updated after: #1067 (undecodable recordings degrade to soft nocatch, Aug 12, 2026): `POST /openai/pronunciation`'s STT catch now branches on `err instanceof UndecodableAudioError` FIRST (same inline house pattern as `parrotChat.ts`) — the corrupted/unsupported-format rejection that `speechToText` raises degrades to the existing empty-transcript nocatch response (signed token, zero score, `nocatch` band, feedback prewarm) with a new distinct cause label `undecodable_audio` (added to the `NocatchCause` union; `attempts.nocatch_cause` is free-form text, no migration) and no-fault copy ("That recording didn't come through on our end. That's on us, not you."). Every other STT failure keeps the loud 502; `ensureCompatibleFormat`/`convertToWav` never raise this error type, so converter outages stay loud even though conversion sits inside the same try. The route's warn log for this case records the format and byte length the error carries. Chat and pronunciation now handle undecodable audio symmetrically. Pins in `openai.pronunciation.undecodable.test.ts` (soft nocatch + cause + arrival log; ordinary STT error still 502 — the over-widening guard).

Last updated after: #1046 (English-meaning audio segment on mobile review, Aug 12, 2026): mobile review was the last practice surface with no meaning segment; it now runs the identical post-phrase chain as mobile practice (400ms beat, English voice, shared `meaningSpeechText`, lazy pref ref, per-session cache, overlapping prewarm with reset-on-failure, fail-silent to phrase-only), behind the same `bolo.meaningAudio` preference and coach-voice master gate, on the same playback token so barge-in and iOS routing are covered for free. Review's settings menu is three items now — the two-item ruling in section 10ai is superseded. See section 10ak.

Last updated after: #1044 (lesson header settings menu + language chip, Aug 12, 2026): the three practice-header audio pills moved behind a settings gear on BOTH platforms (web = the previously-unimported shadcn `dropdown-menu.tsx`, `menuitemcheckbox` + `aria-checked` + `data-disabled`; mobile = the house Modal-as-bottom-sheet, `onRequestClose` for the Android back gesture), all three carrying the owner-approved text labels `Autoplay phrase` / `Spoken feedback` / `Speak meaning` plus an explicit On/Off word. Mobile's Feedback item and the result-card mute share ONE `spokenEnabled` state. A display-only uppercase language chip sits immediately left of the gear in a fixed three-character slot (never truncated: `sat` clipped to `SA` is Sanskrit), with no handler, no role and no focus. See section 10aj.

Last updated after: #1003 follow-ups (header pill fit + meaning beat prewarm, Aug 2, 2026): practice header pill labels (PHRASE/FEEDBACK/MEANING) are now `sr-only sm:not-sr-only` — icon-only below the sm breakpoint with accessible names, titles, and aria-pressed preserved; the three pills are grouped into one right-aligned `ml-auto` unit inside a `flex-wrap` header (`gap-x-2 gap-y-1 sm:gap-3`) so at large accessibility text sizes the group wraps to a second header row as a unit instead of clipping under the page wrapper's overflow-hidden (probe caught the Meaning pill at 332-377px on a 320px viewport at 125% root text before this); verified at 320px light/dark/normal/large-text and 375px via committed `qa/header-pills-320-probe.mjs` (real signed-in Clerk session, all PASS, worst case right edge 304/320). Meaning segment beat: `MEANING_SEGMENT_PAUSE_MS` stays 400 (owner-accepted deviation from the 450 brief — the constant was never the cause); the long felt gap came from cold-cache synthesis starting only after the phrase clip ended (felt gap = max(network synth, beat)), so `playCoach` now PRE-WARMS `synthMeaning()` (hoisted out of `playMeaning` to the effect scope) while the phrase clip plays — felt gap = the beat constant on cold and warm caches alike; a failed prewarm resets its handle so `playMeaning` retries fresh and keeps owning the fail-silent fallback; the meaning toggle still applies to the very next play (preference read fresh at clip end), but toggling off mid-clip can leave one cache-filling synthesis call — the toggle-off test pin in `practice-meaning-audio.test.tsx` was adjusted (meaningCalls 0 -> 1, nothing plays, still 1 audio element). Gate per brief: typecheck clean, three practice audio suites 14/14 targeted only, no full suite, no restart (client-only).

Last updated after: Perfect band threshold 93 -> 91 (Aug 2, 2026, owner-ruled): `BAND_THRESHOLDS.perfect` 91 in `scoreBands.ts` plus both client mirrors (`band-pill.tsx`, `bolo-mobile/lib/ui.ts` — mobile devices see it at the next iOS build); FSRS Easy threshold in `fsrsScheduler.ts` moved 93 -> 91 with it by explicit ruling (stays a separate literal, moves only by owner ruling — an Easy that could not fire under the honesty cap 92 left the scheduler without its top rating); verify-time clamp in `evaluationToken.ts` now re-derives the band via `bandFromScore(safeScore)` instead of hardcoding `great`; ONE copy change ("Your pronunciation was spot on." -> "Every sound was spot on." in `FAST_PASS_RESPONSES`); three sharedConstants contract suites + `scoreBands.test.ts` + cap-pinning tests in evaluationToken/fast-path/honesty/attempts updated to 91-threshold expectations; `backfillScoringV2.ts` keeps its historical `>= 93` (one-time backfill already executed, documents how ratings were seeded); browser-verified: capped 92 renders band perfect, "Perfect!" flash, and confetti (`qa/perfect-band-92-probe.mjs`, coach-clip-into-mic pattern). History display inconsistency for pre-change stored five-band rows accepted as-is (see section 3).

Last updated after: Build 35 owner rulings (August 5, 2026): Two follow-on fixes to the audio-toggle split. (1) Tear-SFX gate: `playTearSfx()` is now gated by `soundPref` on both platforms. Web: `home.tsx` imports `loadSoundPref` and wraps the call (`if (loadSoundPref()) { playTearSfx(); }`). Mobile: `JourneyPassCard.tsx` imports `loadSoundPref` and uses the async squawk-SFX pattern (`loadSoundPref().then(on => { if (on) playTearSfx(); })`). (2) Meaning-toggle disabled: when Coach voice is off, the practice-page Meaning toggle is visibly disabled using the screen's existing pattern. Web: `practice.tsx` meaning button gains `disabled={!coachVoiceRef.current}` + `disabled:opacity-40 disabled:pointer-events-none`. Mobile: `PracticeHeader` gains `meaningAudioDisabled?: boolean` prop; call site passes `meaningAudioDisabled={!coachVoiceEnabled}`; Pressable gains `disabled={meaningAudioDisabled}` + `opacity: meaningAudioDisabled ? 0.4 : 1` in style. Tests: `journey-pass-motion.test.tsx` gains soundPref mock + async SFX teardown + off-case test; `home-boarding-pass.test.tsx` gains Sound-effects-off suppression test; `practice-meaning-audio.test.tsx` gains coachVoicePref module mock + 2 meaning-toggle disabled tests; `practice-band-audio.test.tsx` gains 2 meaning-toggle disabled tests. Mobile suite: 518/518 (baseline 515 + 3). Web suite: 507/507 (baseline 504 + 3). Typecheck: zero errors.

New files: `artifacts/bolo-mobile/lib/coachVoicePref.ts` (async AsyncStorage) and `artifacts/gujarati-coach/src/lib/coachVoicePref.ts` (sync localStorage), both exporting `loadCoachVoicePref/saveCoachVoicePref/COACH_VOICE_PREF_KEY`. no code changes; key findings: (1) consumable NON_SUBSCRIPTION_PURCHASE events would accidentally grant Plus via the existing concernedEntitlement null-fallback in revenuecatSync.ts — must add to IGNORED_EVENT_TYPES before any token SKUs go live; (2) daily lesson limit is dead code since July 30 and recommended for removal in Build 32; (3) advisory lock pattern for token spend = pg_advisory_xact_lock consistent with teaser and phraseReports patterns; (4) token balance = SUM(token_ledger.amount) - SUM(token_spend_ledger.cost), never a stored counter; Build 32 slice: ledger tables + earn hooks + Station Pause + Express Multiplier + RevenueCat NON_SUBSCRIPTION_PURCHASE handler + tokenBalance on /progress/summary + remove dead cap code; Build 33 slice: test-out retries + Streak Repair + All-Access monthly grant + web/mobile surfaces), and Task 940 (account deletion purge, July 31, 2026: DELETE /account must explicitly delete every user-keyed table before the `users` row or the FK delete fails; covered tables now: family_seats/plans, chat_turns, friend_invites, attempts, badges, lesson_generations, friendships, xp_ledger, user_ability, user_item_memory, phrase_reports, daily_quiz_completions, game_sessions, lesson_group_progress, lesson_group_testouts, script_trace_progress, contact_submissions; `token_ledger`/`token_spend_ledger` TODO for build-32; rule: audit `lib/db/src/schema/` for `references.*usersTable` whenever adding a schema to keep the handler complete; deletion test seeds all 14 user-keyed tables to catch any future omission)

**Section numbering is stable.** Specs reference section 9 for working rules. Do not renumber.

## 1. Repository layout

Monorepo, pnpm workspace, root at `/home/runner/workspace`.

| Path | What it is |
|---|---|
| `artifacts/api-server` | Node/Express API |
| `artifacts/gujarati-coach` | React web app |
| `artifacts/bolo-mobile` | Expo React Native app, expo-router |
| `artifacts/bolo-launch-video` | Marketing asset: programmatic launch video (React/Framer Motion, video-js scaffold). Not product code; nothing in the main apps depends on it |
| `artifacts/bolo-social-clips` | Marketing asset: short social clips, same video-js scaffold. Not product code; no main-app dependencies |
| `artifacts/mockup-sandbox` | Internal design tooling: vite preview server for canvas component mockups. Never shipped; no main-app dependencies |
| `lib/db/src` | Drizzle schema |
| `lib/db/drizzle/` | Migrations and `_journal.json` |
| `lib/api-spec/openapi.yaml` | Schema source of truth |
| `lib/api-client-react/src/generated/api.schemas.ts` | Generated client types |
| `lib/api-zod/src/generated/types/` | Generated Zod types |
| `lib/integrations-openai-ai-react/src/audio/` | Web audio recording hook |

**Stack:** Postgres, Drizzle ORM with drizzle-kit migrate, Clerk auth (self-managed; dev instance `free-bedbug-6.clerk.accounts.dev`, production instance on `clerk.bolo-india.app` with CNAME DNS verified and Apple + Google SSO custom credentials), OpenAI direct key (Whisper STT, chat, TTS), ts-fsrs v5, expo-audio on mobile.

**Clerk key locations (dev and prod never share instances):** dev = workspace secrets `CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` / `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` / `CLERK_SECRET_KEY` (pk_test/sk_test, free-bedbug-6). Production = Replit production environment vars `CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` and `VITE_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` (both `pk_live_Y2xlcmsuYm9sby1pbmRpYS5hcHAk`, set) plus `CLERK_SECRET_KEY` sk_live as a deployment secret (owner sets it in the Publishing tool's deployment settings, then republishes). Mobile = EAS production env `EXPO_PUBLIC_CLERK_PUBLISHABLE_KEY` (same pk_live, set). No proxy in production: CNAME custom domain and Clerk proxy are mutually exclusive; `VITE_CLERK_PROXY_URL` stays unset and the stale `EXPO_PUBLIC_CLERK_PROXY_URL` was deleted from EAS. `clerkProxyMiddleware` remains mounted but dormant (nothing routes to `/api/__clerk` when no client sets a proxyUrl). Note `publishableKeyFromHost` returns a dev fallback key when one is present, so the production env must carry live keys or the site would run against the dev instance.

**Never hand-edit** anything under `generated/` or `dist/`. Change `openapi.yaml` and regenerate.

## 2. Database schema

### Pre-existing

**users:** `id` text PK, `timezone` text nullable (IANA), `daily_goal` int default 50 (was 10 until migration 0025, July 28, 2026; existing rows untouched), `tz_grace_used_on` date nullable, `has_chosen_language` bool NOT NULL default false (Spec B1, migration `0032_brown_patch.sql`, July 30, 2026: backfill grandfathered rows with a non-null `active_language` to true; see the section 4 "Language choice signal" entry for the write rules)

**attempts:** `id` serial PK, `user_id`, `language_code`, `phrase_id` nullable, `native_script`, `romanized`, `english`, `transcript`, `score` int 0-100, `passed` bool, `feedback`, `created_at`, plus added in Task 1: `latency_ms`, `audio_duration_ms`, `band`, `fsrs_rating`, `theta_delta`, `beta_delta`, `xp_awarded`, `flags` jsonb

**phrases** (`lib/db/src/schema/phrases.ts`): `language_code`, `category_id`, `difficulty` int, `premium` bool, `stage` text (`phrase` or `sentence`), `hint`, `sort_order`, plus Task 1 additions `accepted_answers` jsonb, `elo_difficulty`, `elo_difficulty_rd`, `exposure_count`, plus Spec D2's `register` text nullable (`formal` / `colloquial` / `code_switched`, migration `0021_spec_d2_register.sql`) with index `phrases_language_register_idx` on `(language_code, register)`. All rows currently NULL; no authoring or filtering yet. Plus C1's `source` text nullable (migration `0028_messy_arachne.sql`, July 29, 2026): provenance copied from the seed entry's `origin` field by the seeder (`curated` or `generated_c1`); runtime-inserted rows (lesson generation, replenisher) and rows that predate the column stay NULL. QA passes target `source='generated_c1'` precisely.

**game_sessions:** `id`, `user_id`, `language_code`, `game`, `correct_count`, `total_count`, `xp_awarded`, `context`, `created_at`. Game values: `word-match`, `speed-round`, `listen-and-pick`, `phrase-builder`, `daily-quiz`, `script-trace`

**daily_quiz_completions:** `id`, `user_id`, `language_code`, `quiz_date` date, `score` 0-5, `xp_awarded`, `completed_at`

**script_trace_progress:** `id`, `user_id`, `chapter`, `character_id`, `passed`, `best_score`, `attempt_count`, `updated_at`

**badges:** `id`, `user_id`, `language_code`, `badge_key`, `earned_at`

**languages:** `code` text PK, `name`, `native_name`, `script`, `font_family`, `rtl` bool, `sort_order` int, plus `speech_capability` text default `'supported'` (migration `0022_speech_capability.sql`; see section 4 "Speech capability")

**phrase_reports** (Spec B2, migration `0031_groovy_sebastian_shaw.sql`, July 30, 2026): `id` serial PK, `user_id` FK users, `phrase_id` int FK phrases, `reason` text (`translation_wrong`/`transliteration_wrong`/`audio_wrong`/`other` — text, not a pg enum, so new reasons are a code change), `note` text nullable (≤280 enforced in the route), `language_code` FK languages + `stage` — both DERIVED server-side from the phrase row at write time (never client-supplied), `created_at` timestamptz. Indexes: `(user_id, created_at)` serves the rolling-hour throttle; `(phrase_id)` serves review-time joins (incl. against `phrases.source` provenance). Append-only; duplicate (user, phrase) reports allowed by design

### Added in Task 1 (migration `0020_scoring_core_v2.sql`)

**user_item_memory:** `user_id`, `phrase_id`, `stability`, `difficulty`, `state`, `reps`, `lapses`, `last_reviewed_at`, `due_at`. PK (user_id, phrase_id), index (user_id, due_at)

**user_ability:** `user_id`, `language_code`, `skill`, `theta`, `theta_rd`, `attempt_count`, `updated_at`. PK (user_id, language_code, skill)

**xp_ledger:** `id`, `user_id`, `attempt_id`, `game_session_id`, `source`, **`xp`**, `multiplier_reason`, `language_code`, `created_at`. Unique on `(user_id, source, ref_id)`

> The XP column is named **`xp`**, not `amount`.

## 3. Business rules and invariants


### Band thresholds (Spec 0 rule 40; five-band since Task 901, July 31, 2026)

Five bands, top to bottom: `perfect` (score >= 91), `great` (80-90), `good` (68-79), `almost` (55-67), `retry` (< 55). `nocatch` is set separately when audio is under 400 ms or transcription fails. The SINGLE config block is `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/scoreBands.ts` (`BAND_THRESHOLDS`, `BAND_LADDER`, `bandFromScore`, credit-group helpers, `normalizeBand`); clients mirror it in `gujarati-coach/src/components/ui/band-pill.tsx` and `bolo-mobile/lib/ui.ts`, pinned by the three sharedConstants contract suites. The perfect/great split moved 93 -> 91 by owner ruling (Aug 2, 2026) so `perfect` is reachable under the honesty cap (92); the FSRS Easy threshold moved with it by the same ruling and stays deliberately coincident (separate literal in `fsrsScheduler.ts`, moves only by owner ruling). The 68 split remains display-only TUNING PENDING; **80 and 55 are FROZEN** — they are the legacy nailed/close boundaries every behavioral consumer keys on. History display note (accepted as-is by owner ruling): attempts stored with five-band names before Aug 2, 2026 keep their stored band, so an old 91-92 row shows `great` while a new identical score shows `perfect`; no migration, no re-derivation of stored bands.

**Band is derived from score only.** It was briefly derived from `passed`, which allowed an LLM-returned boolean to inflate it. Fixed. Do not reintroduce.

`passed` is `score >= 80` server-side, so `passed` and "full-credit band" (`perfect`|`great`) coincide, but they are computed independently. Mastery-related rules that read "band nailed / score >= 80" are, in five-band terms, "at least Great" — behavior unchanged.

**Credit groups (behavioral, frozen):** full credit = `perfect`|`great` (== legacy `nailed`): full XP, Elo 1.0, FSRS Good/Easy, test-out counts it. Half credit = `good`|`almost` (== legacy `close`): half XP factor, Elo 0.5, FSRS Hard. Behavioral code must use `isFullCreditBand`/`isHalfCreditBand`/`isPassingBand`, never string-compare individual band names.

**Legacy stored rows:** attempts written before Task 901 still hold `nailed`/`close` in the DB — there was NO data migration. Read paths normalize via `normalizeBand(band, score)` (exact re-derivation: legacy bands came from the same score field with the same frozen edges); `SPEAKING_STREAK_QUALIFYING_BANDS` also accepts the legacy names directly.

### Band treatment (must stay consistent across practice and review)

| Band | Mascot pose | Haptic | Sound cue | XP arc | Shake | Confetti |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| perfect | `cheer` | Success | `correct` | yes | no | yes (only band) |
| great | `cheer` | Success | `correct` | yes | no | no |
| good | `thumbsup` | Light impact | none | yes | no | no |
| almost | `thumbsup` | Light impact | none | yes | no | no |
| retry | `tryagain` | Warning | `wrong` | no | yes | no |
| nocatch | `thinking` | none | none | no | no | no |

The result card on both platforms renders the five-band **ladder** (`BandLadder` — web `ui/band-ladder.tsx`, mobile `components/BandLadder.tsx`): all five labels (Perfect/Great/Good/Almost/Try again) with the achieved band filled in its brand color (perfect=success, great=accent, good=primary, almost=mutedForeground, retry=destructive) and the rest muted; labels only, never a numeric score. `nocatch` never shows the ladder — it keeps its neutral `BandPill` ("Didn't catch that"), now muted instead of destructive (rule 16 alignment, deliberate Task 901 change; the mobile review flash is likewise neutral gray for nocatch).

`good`/`almost` are partial successes, not failures (worth 0.5 in Elo, Hard in FSRS, 0.6 XP band factor).

`nocatch` is a **system failure to hear**, not a learner error. It gets no negative treatment of any kind. This supersedes the earlier treatment where it shared `retry`'s handling.

Build 30 batch 1 (July 30, 2026) extended nocatch neutrality in both directions: the server skips badge awarding entirely for nocatch attempts (`learning.ts` POST /attempts wraps the award block in `if (!isNocatch)`; the attempt row still persists, `newlyEarnedBadges` is always an array), and the mobile practice full-bleed score flash skips nocatch (no color assigned, no opacity pulse). Nothing negative AND no rewards. Also from that batch: on a **retry-band** result card (mobile) the CTA layout flips, "Try again" becomes the primary chunky button (`try-again-button`) and "Next phrase"/"Finish" a bordered secondary (`next-secondary-button`); all other bands keep the original icon-retry + primary-next layout. The ladder's retry rung (and formerly the band pill) also reads "Try again", so tests must address the button by testID (mobile) or role (web).

### Session end

Session confetti and the `session_complete` cue share one gate: `good * 2 >= total`, where good counts phrases ending at a passing band (`isPassingBand`, legacy nailed|close). A weak session gets no confetti and no celebratory sound. "PERFECT SESSION" = every phrase ended full-credit (unchanged math, legacy all-nailed).

Summary confetti renders language glyphs, capped at 40 on web and 25 on mobile. Per-phrase confetti stays shapes at 70 web / 44 mobile.

### Scoring

- **FSRS:** ts-fsrs v5, default parameters, desired retention 0.90. State materialized in `user_item_memory`.
- **Mastery:** `stability >= 21 days AND last attempt correct`. Reversible. No permanent graduation.
- **Elo:** Rasch logit scale. `K = 0.4 / (1 + 0.05 * n)`. Observed score 1.0 full-credit (perfect|great), 0.5 half-credit (good|almost), 0.0 retry.
- **XP:** `10 * band_factor * difficulty_multiplier * review_multiplier * decay_multiplier`. Band factor 1.0/0.6/0.0. Difficulty `clamp(1 + 0.5*(beta - theta), 0.25, 2.0)`. Review 1.5 when due. Decay 0.1 when stability > 60 days and not due.
- **Game XP:** word-match 15, listen-and-pick 15, phrase-builder 20, speed-round 25, plus 10 when accuracy >= 80%.
- **Daily quiz XP:** 10 per correct, plus 20 for a perfect 5/5. Max 70.
- **Script Trace XP:** flat 30 per chapter.
- **XP total** is always the sum of `xp_ledger.xp`. Never a stored counter.

### Streaks

All streaks use `localDayKey()` and `previousDayKey()` from `progressMetrics.ts`, in the user's IANA timezone. General and quiz streaks share one implementation and cannot drift.

A day with no activity yet falls back to yesterday, so a streak does not appear broken mid-day. This is correct; do not "fix" it.

**The day streak has ONE definition and ONE implementation as of Task #1081 (August 12, 2026):** a streak day is a day on which the learner **completed a lesson OR played a mini-game, in ANY language**. Bare attempts no longer earn a day. The single source is `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/streakDays.ts` — `loadStreakLadder(userId, timeZone?, now?)` returns `{ earnedDayKeys, coveredDayKeys, currentStreakDays }`, and every surface reads it: `GET /progress/summary` (the banner), `GET /tokens/streak-repair` (the offer's eligibility and promised number), `POST /tokens/repair-streak` (which re-derives rather than trusting the offer), and `loadExtendedMetrics` in `badgeAward.ts` (the six streak badges). `computeProgressMetrics` no longer derives a streak at all — it **takes `currentStreakDays` as an argument**, which is the structural guarantee that the banner and the repair offer cannot disagree. Do not add a second expression; two of them promising different numbers on a 25 Chai spend surface is exactly the defect this replaced.

**Speaking streak (Spec D2):** derived at query time by `computeSpeakingStreakDays()` in `progressMetrics.ts` — filters attempts to passing bands (`SPEAKING_STREAK_QUALIFYING_BANDS`: perfect/great/good/almost plus legacy stored nailed/close) and delegates to `computeStreakDays`, so bucketing and the mid-day fallback can never drift. Surfaced as optional `speakingStreakDays` on `GET /progress/summary`. No new table or column backs it. **It has NO display surface as of Task #1057 (August 12, 2026)** — tracking, derivation, its unit tests and the response field are all retained, but the Mic stat was removed from both the web home stat bar and the mobile Progress-tab grid (owner ruling: not worth a permanent tile). Any future surface reads the same field; do not re-derive it.

### Architectural conventions

- **Derive, do not store.** Three deliberate exceptions: FSRS state (path dependent), Elo item difficulty (cross-user), and `xp_ledger` (depends on ability and difficulty at time of attempt).
- **One date-bucketing implementation.** `localDayKey()`. Never write a second.
- **One reduced-motion check.** `prefersReducedMotion()` from `motionPrefs.ts`. Never write a second.
- **`evaluationToken` is never decoded on the client.** Signed server authorization material, passed through opaquely. `band` and `xpAwarded` are top-level response fields for display.
- **Generated Zod schemas are non-strict.** Adding response fields is safe for older clients.
- **New API response fields are always optional**, for Expo back-compat with installed builds.
- **No em dashes in any output.**

## 4. API server

`artifacts/api-server/src/`

| File | Contains |
|---|---|
| `routes/openai.ts` | `POST /openai/pronunciation`. Line 595 latency guard, 969 LLM `passed` fallback, 993 band derivation |
| `routes/learning.ts` | `POST /attempts`, `GET /review/phrases`, `GET /progress/summary` (1273-1326), `GET /categories`, `POST /game-sessions`, `/attempts/recent` |
| `lib/lessonGroupAccess.ts` | Shared sequential-unlock guard (July 30, 2026): `getUnlockedGroupIds(userId, categoryId, languageCode, {stats?})` = load context + derive + completion latch (idempotent write-through, so phrase routes latch too). Call sites: category phrases GET, category sentences GET (cached path; freshly generated rows are NULL-group = servable), `/lesson-groups/:id/phrases` (locked → 403 `{error:"lesson_group_locked",groupId,status:"locked"}`, never 402), and the journey listing (via `loadGroupUnlockContext` + `deriveAndLatchUnlock`; showroom branch loads context but never derives/latches). Per-phrase rule `isPhraseServable`: unlocked ∨ NULL group ∨ prior attempt (retake exemption — Retake deep-links resolve against the category list). CALLER CONTRACT: entitlement gates run BEFORE the guard. Deliberately UNFILTERED: test-out routes (they exist to sample locked groups — the skip-ahead mechanism), `/review/phrases` (attempted-only by construction), chat seed words, and daily-quiz sampling (approved decision: not practice surfaces). Tests: `routes/learning.unlock-filtering.test.ts` (incl. #854, July 30, 2026: concurrent-derive race → exactly one latch row via the `(user_id, lesson_group_id)` PK upsert, and a non-vacuous teaser/showroom no-latch test that builds a real Greetings teaser group for the test language — reset `__resetTeaserCacheForTests()` when creating throwaway teaser sets) |
| `routes/phraseReports.ts` | `POST /phrases/:id/report` (Spec B2, July 30, 2026): authenticated (mounted behind requireAuth in `routes/index.ts`) phrase-incorrectness reports → `phrase_reports`. Client sends ONLY `reason` + optional `note` (≤280); `language_code`/`stage` derived from the phrase row. Cap: 20 stored reports per user per rolling hour, counted from `phrase_reports` itself (DB-backed like the test-out throttle) — over-limit returns a SILENT `200 {success:true}` and stores nothing (unlike test-out's 429). 400 invalid reason/note/id, 404 unknown phrase. Clients are fire-and-forget with an optimistic "Thanks, we'll check it" toast, failures silent, no practice-flow changes: web `components/phrase-report.tsx` (popover on the practice card, quiet flag next to the play/text row), mobile `components/PhraseReportButton.tsx` (modal sheet next to the "Hear it" button; ships with build 27, no dedicated build). Tests: `routes/phraseReports.test.ts` |
| `routes/games.ts` | Daily quiz, script trace, `computeXp`, `computeQuizStreak` |
| `lib/progressMetrics.ts` | `computeProgressMetrics`, `buildPhraseStats`, `computeStreakDays`, `computeDailyQuizStreak`, `localDayKey`, `previousDayKey` |
| `lib/pronunciationGuards.ts` | `applyScoreGuards`, `normalizeLatin`, `normalizeNative`, `simToScore`. Lines 230/245/274 `passed >= 80`, 266 similarity guard |
| `lib/fsrsScheduler.ts` | `scoreAndBandToRating`, `applyFsrsRating`, `rowToCard` |
| `lib/xpEngine.ts` | XP math, `readLedgerXp` |
| `lib/evaluationToken.ts` | `EvaluationClaims`, `signEvaluation`. Line 116 |
| `lib/feedbackTts.ts` | Eval-time feedback TTS prewarm (Task 903, July 31, 2026): every `/openai/pronunciation` response path fires `prewarmFeedbackTts(feedback, tip)` fire-and-forget — synthesizes the exact spoken-feedback string clients build (`[feedback, tip].filter(Boolean).join(" ")`, pinned by `feedbackSpokenText`) into ttsCache under the default phrase-audio identity (empty language key). An in-flight pending map (`registerPendingFeedbackSynthesis`/`getPendingFeedbackSynthesis`, 60s TTL) closes the race: `/openai/tts` on cache miss JOINS a pending prewarm instead of synthesizing twice (logs `hit: "pending-prewarm"`; falls through to fresh synthesis if the pending rejects). The cache row is written BEFORE the pending resolves. Prewarm is SKIPPED when `TTS_PROVIDER === "elevenlabs"` (per-user voice prefs make the client's cache key unpredictable — see §8). Tests: `lib/feedbackTts.test.ts` (unit + a real-router pending-join integration test) |
| `lib/backfillScoringV2.ts` | Idempotent startup backfill. Line 306 gate throw. `SCORING_V2_GATE_OVERRIDE=1` bypasses |
| `lib/pricingCatalog.ts` | Live plan prices read from the configured Stripe price ids (`STRIPE_{PLUS,FAMILY}_{MONTHLY,ANNUAL}_PRICE_ID`), shaped as `{plus,family} x {monthly,annual} -> {amountCents, currency}`. Injectable `PriceFetcher` + clock for tests; 5-minute in-memory cache with in-flight dedupe; ONLY successes are cached; throws when no price id is configured or a configured price has a null `unit_amount` (never substitutes a guess). Tests: `lib/pricingCatalog.test.ts` (8, no DB) |
| `routes/pricing.ts` | `GET /pricing`, PUBLIC (mounted before `requireAuth`, next to languages) because the signed-out landing page renders the pricing ladder. Answers 503 on catalog failure so clients render a price-free surface |

`index.ts` opens the port FIRST, then runs the startup pipeline (content seed, `runBackfillScoringV2()`, scope triggers, lesson-group backfill) behind `listen` (changed July 29, 2026: the C1 rollout seed blew the publish promote step's ~60 second port-open window when the pipeline ran before listen). Pipeline order is unchanged and load-bearing; a pipeline failure still exits the process. Startup probe path `/api/healthz` (in `artifact.toml`) returns 200 with no auth and no content dependency.

### Language access gating, three-state model (M1 teaser, July 29, 2026)

`lib/gating.ts` resolves locked-language access via `getLanguageAccess(req, lang)` into four states: `allowed` (plan covers the language), `teaser` (locked, but fewer than 3 distinct teaser phrases attempted), `exhausted` (locked, 3 distinct teaser phrases attempted, lifetime), and `locked` (locked language with no teaser set, for example test fixtures; behaves exactly like pre-M1). The teaser set is the first 3 phrase-stage phrases of the first Greetings lesson group per language, resolved and cached in `lib/teaser.ts` (`TEASER_LIMIT`, `getTeaserPhraseIds`, `countTeaserConsumed`; consumption is DERIVED from the attempts table, no new table). `denyLockedLanguage` is now async and takes `opts.teaserPhraseId` for id-aware exceptions on `GET /phrases/:id` and `POST /attempts`. Denials go through `sendLockedLanguageDenial`: `exhausted` sends reason `teaser_exhausted`; `teaser` and `exhausted` denials carry a `teaser: {consumed, limit}` object; `locked` payloads are byte-identical to pre-M1. Successful teaser responses carry the same `teaser` object on each phrase row and on the attempt result (all optional fields in openapi.yaml for mobile back-compat). Attempts consume a slot per DISTINCT phrase regardless of score. The web app shows a post-result UpgradeCard in practice when `consumed >= limit` and reason-keyed UpgradeScreen copy.

### D1b showroom exception on the lesson-groups listing (July 29, 2026)

`GET /categories/:id/lesson-groups/:lang` is a deliberate product exception to M1's 402-on-all-locked rule, decided in the D1b journey-map build (decision 3): for `teaser` and `exhausted` callers, and ONLY those two states, this one read-only route returns the full group structure instead of a 402, so the journey map can render as the paywall's showroom. The showroom response carries zone and station counts and statuses only, zero phrase content: every group is forced `status: "locked"` except, in teaser state only, the single group hosting the teaser set (marked `teaserStation: true`, `status: "unlocked"`). The envelope adds `access: "teaser" | "exhausted"` and `teaser: {consumed, limit}`. No completion-latch rows are written in showroom mode (no `lesson_group_progress` writes for a language the plan does not own). The `locked` state (language with no teaser set) still sends the pre-M1 402 byte-identically, and a test asserts that exact body, so the exception provably applies to teaser and exhausted callers only. This SUPERSEDES the M1 spec's original behavior for this one route; every other locked-language surface keeps the M1 402 contract, so the M1 write-up above and this entry do not contradict each other. Mobile reuses this contract when its map lands (build 26).

Sentence-stage gate on group phrases (July 29, 2026, code-review follow-up): `GET /lesson-groups/:id/phrases` now denies sentence-stage groups for callers without the `sentences` feature with the same 402 `feature_locked` payload as `/categories/:id/sentences/:lang`. The journey UI dialog-gates sentence stations client-side, but that is convenience, not authority: a deep link (`/practice/:id?group=<sentence group>`) hits the route directly, and before this gate a Free caller on an allowed language got a 200 (premium-filtered) instead of an authoritative denial. Boundary pinned in `learning.lesson-groups-showroom.test.ts` (402 for non-Plus with zero sentence text, 200 for Plus).

Taste-station phrases on the group route (Task 900, July 31, 2026): `GET /lesson-groups/:id/phrases` carries the same M1 teaser exception the category-phrases route implements. A teaser-state caller requesting the designated taste group (the group holding the teaser phrase set, the one the journey listing marks `teaserStation: true`) receives exactly the teaser phrase rows, each with the `teaser: {consumed, limit}` object, in the same per-phrase shape as the category-phrases teaser branch, instead of a 402. Before this fix the journey listing advertised the taste station as unlocked but this route applied a blanket `denyLockedLanguage`, so the advertised station 402d into the paywall. Exhausted callers and non-taste groups on a locked language keep the 402 byte-identical via `sendLockedLanguageDenial`, and entitled callers are byte-identical to before. The teaser branch returns BEFORE the sequential-unlock guard (`getUnlockedGroupIds` / `deriveAndLatchUnlock`), so no `lesson_group_progress` latch rows are ever written for a language the caller's plan does not own (the `lib/lessonGroupAccess.ts` CALLER CONTRACT). Boundary pinned in `routes/teaserGating.test.ts`, including a non-vacuous no-latch test that puts the taste group at the exact COMPLETION_RATIO threshold before the denied call, and a verification that `POST /attempts` accepts teaser phrases reached via this flow.

### Pronunciation pipeline (do not modify)

Whisper `speechToText` → retry at high quality if transcript empty or similarity <= 0.40 → fast path if phonetic similarity >= 0.93 using `simToScore` (0.90-1.00 maps to 80-100) → otherwise LLM rubric via `PRONUNCIATION_RUBRIC_PROMPT` → `applyScoreGuards`.

Guards include normalization, a wrong-phrase cap at 40 when a sibling phrase (up to 400 in the same language) beats the target at >= 0.80 similarity, and a Levenshtein floor/cap.

### Speech capability + script-mismatch guard (July 2026)

- `languages.speech_capability` (`supported` | `degraded` | `unsupported`, default `supported`, migration 0022) is server-authoritative, seeded from probe verdicts in `seedData.ts`, exposed as optional `speechCapability` on `GET /languages`. Verdicts: ks + sat degraded, mni + brx unsupported, rest supported.
- **Probe limitation:** verdicts come from `artifacts/api-server/scripts/probeSttLanguages.ts` using TTS-generated audio, so `supported` means "best case with clean synthetic speech". Re-probing with real human recordings is pending and likely infeasible in this environment; if human audio shows a language materially worse, downgrade its seed value.
- `/openai/pronunciation` short-circuits for `unsupported` languages BEFORE any STT call: returns band `nocatch`, xp 0, listen-record-compare copy. Clients never send evaluations for these languages, this is the backstop.
- `applyScoreGuards` guard ladder changed: the old `cross-script-cap` (cap 85) is replaced by universal `script-mismatch-nocatch` — a transcript in the wrong Unicode script (or a Latin transcript with romanized sim < 0.45 against a non-Latin target) proves the recognizer failed, and resolves to band `nocatch` (score 0, no XP, no streak break) in ALL languages. Latin transcripts with sim >= 0.45 stay scoreable; wrong-phrase-cap takes precedence.
- The STT language-code-rejected retry in `lib/integrations-openai-ai-server` is unchanged but now logs `[stt] language_code_rejected_retrying_without_hint` with the language code.
- `POST /attempts` treats band `nocatch` as a system miss end to end: the attempt row is inserted for analytics, but Elo theta is untouched (`thetaDelta` 0, no ability upsert), no FSRS rating/memory write, and no phrase exposure bump. This is the server-authoritative safeguard; clients in unsupported-language compare mode never submit attempts at all.
- Mobile auth offers Sign in with Apple (`components/AppleAuthButton.tsx`, Clerk `oauth_apple` via `useSSO`, same flow shape as `GoogleAuthButton.tsx`) on both `(auth)/sign-in.tsx` and `(auth)/sign-up.tsx`. iOS-only render (`Platform.OS !== 'ios'` returns null) — App Review 4.8 requirement, not needed on Android. Apple may supply a `privaterelay.appleid.com` email and supplies the user's name only on FIRST authorization; all name usages fall back (`user?.firstName ?? 'friend'` etc.), never assume email is the user's real mailbox. Web has no Apple button yet (intentional).
- Clients: web `practice.tsx` (also serves review mode) and mobile `practice/[id].tsx` + `review.tsx` read the active language's `speechCapability`. Degraded shows a one-time dismissible notice (key `bolo.approxNoticeSeen.<code>`, localStorage/AsyncStorage). Unsupported switches to a listen-record-compare stage (play target, hear yourself, no evaluation request, no band/XP).

### Language choice signal + provenance flag (Spec B1, July 30, 2026)

- `users.has_chosen_language` distinguishes an EXPLICIT language pick from the LanguageProvider's first-reconcile seed (which pushes the local default "hi" server-side and must never count as a choice). Exposed as optional `hasChosenLanguage` on the GET /account learning-prefs object. PATCH /account/preferences accepts ONLY `hasChosenLanguage: true`; `false` is a 400 ("a choice can't be unmade") — the flag is one-way. Writers that send true: the web `/choose-language` step, the language picker (both locked and unlocked branches — picker picks now persist server-side, previously local-only), and the account settings language control. A plain `activeLanguage` write without the flag leaves it untouched (that is the seed path).
- `GET /languages` gained optional `communityReviewed: boolean`, derived server-side in `routes/languages.ts` from a module-level Set built by `c1RolloutLanguageCodes()` (`lib/db/src/seedData.ts`, keys of the C1 rollout JSON). **PINNED SEMANTICS (July 30, 2026): `true` means "this language's extended sentence content is C1-LLM-generated and its community review is ONGOING" — it is the transparency-footer-note trigger, NOT a quality seal.** The name reads inverted; clients branching on it must treat `true` as "AI-generated, flaggable, under review" (the server-side Set is literally named `generatedContentLanguages`). `false` means the language did NOT ship C1-generated sentence top-ups, for either reason: hand-curated pilot (gu) or C1 rollout withdrawn (kok, ks, sat, brx, mni — these stay at the curated 8-sentence base). Live values verified against the running server July 30, 2026: exactly 16 of 22 true (as bn doi hi kn mai ml mr ne or pa sa sd ta te ur); false = brx gu kok ks mni sat. Not stored on the languages table — recompute follows the rollout file automatically. If a client ever needs a literal "vetted content" flag, add a NEW field; do not reinterpret this one.
- Tests: `routes/languageChoice.test.ts` (fresh-account default, seed-write leaves false, one-way 400, explicit pick sticks, communityReviewed derivation).

### normalizeNative

Strips ZWJ/ZWNJ, NFC normalizes, removes Devanagari nasal-plus-virama `[\u0919\u091E\u0923\u0928\u092E]\u094D`, drops anusvara and chandrabindu, strips non-letters, lowercases.

Known tradeoff: nasalized and non-nasalized forms collapse, so `हंस` and `हस` normalize identically. Accepted for a fuzzy pronunciation layer.

Devanagari only. Other scripts are #776, gated on native speaker review.

**Contact form is public (Aug 2, 2026, tiny UI batch item 5).** `contactRouter` is mounted BEFORE the barrel-wide `requireAuth` in `routes/index.ts` (it previously sat behind it, so signed-out visitors 401'd). The POST `/contact` handler now derives the caller itself: `getAuth(req)?.userId ?? null` (clerkMiddleware runs app-wide) plus `ensureLocalUser` when present so the `contact_submissions.user_id` FK stays satisfiable; attribution is best-effort inside a try/catch — a Clerk hiccup never blocks a message. Rate limit (3 per user/IP per 10 min) unchanged and works for anonymous callers.

**Fonts (self-hosted since the fonts pass, July 28, 2026):** `src/fonts/fonts.css` declares all `@font-face` rules (Inter + 13 Noto script families incl. Ol Chiki and Meetei Mayek) from local woff2 subsets in `src/fonts/` (~2.0 MB total, `font-display: swap`, OFL.txt alongside). `index.css` imports it; there are **no** runtime Google Fonts CDN requests (index.html preconnect/link tags removed too). Regenerate via the css2-API fetch approach if families change.

**Web visual polish pass (Task 812, July 29, 2026):** `AppShell` wraps its children in `PageTransition` keyed by wouter location — the shell persists across /app, /progress, /friends, /games because Switch reconciles it in place, so the location key is what re-triggers the entrance; DesktopNav sits outside it and never re-animates. `index.css` gained two utilities: `.card-lift` (hover lift + press compress; applied to game cards, category-detail phrase/sentence cards, leaderboard rows, friend rows) and `.animate-content-enter` (0.25s skeleton→content fade; applied to home and progress roots, category-detail `<main>`, and friends' leaderboard/requests/friends sections). Both are pure CSS, so the global reduced-motion rule collapses them automatically (spinners exempt via `:not(.animate-spin)`). `EmptyState` copy now staggers in under the mascot's existing pop-in entrance, gated on `useReducedMotion()`.

**Journey map (Spec D1b v2, July 29, 2026):** `/journey` (route in App.tsx, Guard + AppShell) renders the full Bolo Rail map for the active language: sticky boarding-pass header (line name, done/total stations, teaser consumed/limit chip in showroom mode), serpentine railway track (see the July 29 serpentine paragraph below) with six fare-zone postcards (`ZonePostcard`, grayscale when a showroom zone is fully locked), station rows ("Stop N of M", express stamp for `tested_out`, rotated-diamond marker + Plus chip for sentence stage, "Free taste" chip on the teaser station, mascot at the current station). Stations sort phrase-stage before sentence-stage, then by position. Three lock-dialog kinds: progression, sentence (Plus), and language (teaser vs exhausted). Plain-locked 402 renders the UpgradeScreen. Zone/category mismatch is a hard-stop error screen, no remap. Sentence stations are ALWAYS dialog-gated for non-Plus users client-side, and the server is separately authoritative (see the section 4 sentence-stage gate). Line/zone naming data lives in `src/lib/journeyLines.ts` (`JOURNEY_ZONES`, `JOURNEY_LINES` for all 22 language codes, `getJourneyLine()` falls back to a generic "Bolo Line"). `practice.tsx` gained a `?group=` mode (`useListLessonGroupPhrases` with the enabled+queryKey pair, back target `/journey`, post-attempt invalidation of the group phrases and category lesson-group lists). `home.tsx` fronts the topic grid with a "Ride the {line}" card (rebuilt into the boarding-pass hero by Task 823 — see the P1 v2 paragraph). Reachability (resolved July 29, 2026): the language picker routes locked-language taps into the showroom (sets the locked language active, navigates to `/journey`), and `language-context.tsx` no longer auto-reverts plan-locked active languages; see the resolved section 8 row.

**Journey serpentine + full-ticket pass (Task 3 of the asset/visual batch, July 29, 2026; web only, presentation only):** User-picked mockup winners ("pronounced" serpentine curve + "full ticket") applied to production. (1) `journey.tsx` map: the straight rail was replaced by a serpentine railway — stations alternate left/right (`stationX`: even global index → x=92, odd → mapWidth−94), cubic-bezier twin-rail segments with sleeper ties (`RailSegment`: 3 SVG strokes — dashed ties at 0.3 opacity, 8.5px rail, 4px `hsl(var(--background))` center split; locked segments GRAY + 0.5 opacity + dashed). Fixed vertical rhythm: station rows 100px, postcards 122px, terminus 92px. The map column is `max-w-[390px]` centered (mobile-first on desktop too); geometry adapts to narrower phones via a ResizeObserver width hook (`useMapWidth`). The interchange diamond is pinned where the track meets each postcard's top border. `StationRow` became `StationCard` (card is the Link/button tap target with the same aria-labels; the marker is a separate non-interactive absolute element). All behaviors unchanged: lock dialogs, showroom graying (GRAY zoneColor), sentence gating, teaser chip, `?group=` hrefs. (2) `journey.tsx` header + `home.tsx` hero: full-ticket treatment via shared `src/components/ticket.tsx` (`TicketStripes` diagonal CSS-gradient stock, `ZoneStamp` rotated dashed fare-zone ring, `TicketPerforationV` with bg-background notch cutouts). Header pass gains a w-[76px] tear-off stub (stamp of the current station's zone, hidden when there is no current stop); hero gains a w-16 stub (stamp from `useJourneyProgress().current.zoneIndex`, vertical-writing-mode line name) and drops the title truncate for 2-line wrap (`text-lg lg:text-2xl`). Stripes/stamps use `line.accent`; no artwork generated. Verified via `qa/demo-polish-shots.mjs` (all checks pass, mobile + desktop) with Nix chromium; web suite at documented 8-failure baseline; typecheck clean. **Cutout-ruling port (July 30, 2026, post build-28):** the mobile cutout ruling now applies on web too — cutout circles may ONLY straddle card edges as bites; the hero's floating `PunchHole` (component deleted from `ticket.tsx`) and the journey header's floating notch dot + 🎫 emoji (tofu without an emoji font) were removed. Measured light+dark via `qa/task879-shots.mjs` (Clerk ticket sign-in; probes every bg-colored circle in both cards → all STRADDLES-EDGE, none FULLY-INSIDE, no emoji).

**Journey scenery/color pass (July 29, 2026; web only, presentation only):** `src/components/journey-scenery.tsx` adds hand-coded inline-SVG scenery (brand palette + line accent, no raster/generated art). (1) `ZoneVista`: each fare-zone postcard gained a picture side — an accent-tinted sky band with a landmark scene keyed by ZONE INDEX (fixed across all languages): gateway arch (Z1 Greetings), family homes (Z2), clock tower (Z3 Numbers), chai stall (Z4 Food), bazaar + pennants (Z5), festival palace + fireworks (Z6 finale). The vista svg must use `preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMax meet"` — `slice` crops the top of tall landmarks because the band is wider than the 240-unit scene. `ZonePostcard` was restructured (vista on top, address row below, stamp+postmark now side-by-side; rotated italic caption removed) and `PC_H` grew 122→152; harness-checked texts ("Fare zone N ·", stamp "Zone"/number) unchanged. Showroom graying is free: the vista sits inside the postcard's `grayscale` wrapper. (2) `TracksideDoodad`: small scenes (tree, cloud+birds, railway signal, bushes+milestone, hut, telegraph pole) cycle by global station index in the free strip opposite each stop card, rendered inside the map SVG; grayed via CSS `filter: grayscale(1)` when the zone is showroom-locked. (3) `Bunting`: festival flag string above the terminus. Also: the current stop's status line renders in the line accent. Verified via demo-polish harness (all pass) + per-postcard element screenshots; suite still at the 8-failure baseline; typecheck clean.

**Boarding pass + journey CTA animation (July 30, 2026; web only, presentation only):** High-energy idle-motion and CTA-copy pass on the home hero pass and journey map; the pass is the app's single continue mechanism and reads like a game's PLAY button. index.css gains a TUNING-CONSTANTS `:root` block (all amplitudes/cycle times as CSS vars, one place to adjust after seeing it live) plus seven keyframe pairs after the reduced-motion block, all whole-element transform/opacity, short rests, identity start/end: `.animate-ticket-breathe` (3.2s heartbeat, scale via `--pass-breathe-scale`), `.animate-ticket-shimmer` (skewed light band sweeping the ticket face once per heartbeat), `.animate-pass-glow` (opacity-pulsing accent shadow overlay lifting the pass off the page), `.animate-cta-arrow-nudge` (2.4s double-pump slide, `--arrow-slide`), `.animate-train-drive` (home train drives forward then settles with a small overshoot), `.animate-stop-glow-pulse` (2.6s strong border+glow on the Now-boarding card), `.animate-train-bob` (2.2s double station bounce). `home.tsx`: press feedback is a dedicated framer `motion.div` (whileTap scale 0.94 + low-damping spring = overshoot on release; `PASS_PRESS_*` constants at top of file); the breathe class lives on a wrapper div because a CSS transform animation on a framer-driven element would fight its inline transform; shimmer overlay sits inside the Link (overflow-hidden clips it), the glow overlay sits OUTSIDE the Link (so its outward shadow is not clipped) but inside the press div. CTA copy is progress-aware via `useJourneyProgress`: "Start your journey" (no progress), "Resume at Stop N · X phrase(s) to go" (current stop, remaining = phraseCount - masteredCount), "Continue your journey" (progress but no boardable stop). `journey.tsx`: current-stop `TrainEngine` bounces; current `StationCard` glow uses solid ring + 16px bloom; the current-station `Mascot` gets `idle="cheer"` (whole-image hop on the canonical PNG, reduced-motion safe inside the component). Every CSS class is gated on `useReducedMotion()` in JS (the global CSS reduce block also covers the keyframes). Pinned by `src/test/home-boarding-pass.test.tsx` (5 tests: three CTA copy states incl. singular, reduced-motion static render asserting all five home classes absent, hero analytics press). Live-verified via `qa/boarding-pass-cta-shots.mjs` (asserts all seven classes in the authed DOM + captures home/journey shots). Web suite: 35 files / 252 tests. Same-day follow-up (train + station card visual upgrade, web only): `train-svg.tsx` redrawn in brand tokens (indigo body, teal trim, slate chassis, `currentColor` headlamp only) with animatable parts as SVG groups: `.train-wheel` spoked groups (need `transform-box: fill-box`) and `.train-steam-1/2/3` puffs resting at opacity 0. Wheel spin + steam puff keyframes fire ONLY via descendant selectors under `.animate-train-drive`/`.animate-train-bob`, so they inherit the parents' reduced-motion JS gating; svg root is `overflow: visible` so puffs rise above the viewBox. NOTE: `station.masteredCount/phraseCount` are optional on the journey `Station` type — template literals hid this; arithmetic needs `?? 0`. Journey `StationCard` is now a station signboard: active card gets a full-width line-color top bar (`rounded-t-lg`, NOT overflow-hidden — that would clip the glow overlay's bloom), warm `--station-surface` background (dark-mode override exists), `StationSignGlyph`, and a filled progress track replacing the text-only mastered fraction (label kept); non-current cards get a short quiet top tick (line color when accessible, border gray when locked) and keep hover. `--steam-rise` and `--station-surface` joined the :root constants block. Probe extended: wheel/steam group counts (4/3) asserted on both surfaces.

**Chat voice iOS/WebKit audio unlock (July 29, 2026; web `chat.tsx`):** every iPhone browser (Safari, Chrome, Firefox — all WebKit) only allows `.play()` on an `<audio>` element previously started inside a real user gesture; Bolo's reply audio starts seconds after the tap, so the old per-turn `new Audio(...)` was silently blocked (captions showed, no voice — rejection swallowed to `setPhase("idle")`). Fix: two pooled elements (`voicePoolRef` voice/greeting, `sfxPoolRef` squawk) "blessed" with a 50 ms silent WAV data URI by `unlockAudioPlayback()` called synchronously in `handleMicPointerDown`, `sendTextTurn`, and `handleRetry`; all buffered/greeting/SFX playback goes through `acquireAudio(pool, src)` (pause + clear handlers + swap src). The MSE streaming path keeps its own fresh element (WebKit never takes it; desktop doesn't need blessing). The word-reveal duration `probe` only calls `.load()`, never plays — left as `new Audio`. Play-rejection catches now log `[audio] play blocked path=…` with the error name. Test impact: chat test files must stub `Audio` with a constructible class (like the practice tests' `FakeAudio`) because the pointer-down handler now constructs synchronously — `vi.fn(() => ({...}))` stubs throw "not a constructor" (fixed in chat-bilingual-hint, chat-mic-permission-guard, chat-error-banner). Suite back at the 8-failure baseline; typecheck clean. Real-iPhone confirmation pending (needs republish — the report came from the production app).

**Web monetization surfaces (July 29, 2026):** `/upgrade` sells exactly two plans: All-Access (internal id `plus`, $12.99/mo or $89.99/yr, 7-day trial) and Family ($19.99/mo or $139.99/yr). One Language is retired on web; legacy `?plan=one_language` deep links land on the All-Access card, and server internals for mobile One Language holders (requiredPlan values, gating.ts, chatLimits.ts, dev-override endpoint) are unchanged. Customer-facing name is "All-Access" on every web sale surface (the "Save 42%" badges match both annual prices); `subscription.tsx`/`account.tsx` keep "One Language" only as a display label for held mobile tiers. `upgradeHref` takes `{plan, reason}` (the lang param is gone). `beginFamilyCheckout(interval, queryClient)` sends the billing interval; the server's `getFamilyPriceId(interval)` reads `STRIPE_FAMILY_MONTHLY_PRICE_ID`/`STRIPE_FAMILY_ANNUAL_PRICE_ID`. All four Stripe live price IDs live in `.replit` `[userenv.shared]`; the old $9.99/$71.99 Plus prices are archived in Stripe so no new checkout can use them. The in-place Plus-to-Family upgrade on `subscription.tsx` uses the monthly Family price. The Free context card on `/upgrade` reads two lines since Task 824 (July 29, 2026): "Hindi + a free taste of all 22 languages" plus a muted "3 lessons a day" line that keeps the server-daily-cap mirror comment; a full web sweep found no other user-facing "Hindi only" framing (account.tsx's "Hindi + {chosenLangName}" is the accurate legacy One-Language label, left alone). NO REAL SUBSCRIBERS exist as of July 29, 2026: the only paid row ever was the owner's $9.99 test subscription, canceled in Stripe on July 29, 2026; the prod users row reads tier=free, status=canceled with the canceled subscription id and Stripe customer id retained as the audit trail. No grandfathering machinery is needed for the price change.

**Games hub preview vignettes (Task 840, July 29, 2026; web only, presentation only):** the `/games` hub cards' static lucide icon tiles were replaced with miniature looping preview vignettes that pantomime each game's mechanic (Word Match pair slide+light-up, Listen & Pick wave+chip pick, Phrase Builder tile shuffle, Speed Round timer ring + answer flashes, Bolo Quiz choice cycle + sparkle). Implementation: `src/pages/games/game-previews.tsx` + colocated `game-previews.css` (kept out of index.css deliberately), pure CSS keyframes on design tokens, no framer-motion. Two load-bearing conventions: (1) each vignette's BASE styles are authored as its settled frame and keyframes carry all motion states, so the global prefers-reduced-motion rule in index.css collapses every loop to a meaningful static frame for free; (2) no `animation-fill-mode` anywhere (the forwards/both containing-block trap — see the P1 v2 bottom-nav paragraph). Loop phases stagger via a `--gv-delay` custom property (negative delays, set per-card in `index.tsx`), each cycle holds a long settled pause so the grid stays calm. `GamePreview` falls back to the game's lucide icon for unknown ids; locked cards keep the dimmed tile + lock treatment but still play their preview. Card layout, links, pills, and difficulty badges unchanged (`src/test/games-hub.test.tsx` pins titles, hrefs for free/Plus, and vignette presence). Verified via `qa/games-preview-shots.mjs` (see the QA harness row); suite at the 8-failure baseline; typecheck clean.

**Landing page refresh (Task 839, July 29, 2026; `src/pages/landing.tsx`, presentation only):** the signed-out landing now reflects the full product. Header logo swapped from the static `mascot-wave.png` to the rigged `<Mascot pose="wave" size={36} idle="none" />` (hero already used the rig). Sections: hero + SpeakingDemo (unchanged centerpiece, expanded copy), language chips, "The loop that makes it stick" (4 cards: Hear it → Say it out loud → Get coached → It comes back — spaced review is part of the pitch), "Way more than flashcards" (Chat with Bolo, journey map, games arcade w/ Script Trace call-out, retention loop) plus anchored feature chips, updated comparison block, and an honest no-hard-sell Free vs Plus/Family note. All sections reuse the local `Reveal` + `glass-card` language and a shared `FeatureCard`; hero secondary CTA and language chips moved from `bg-white` to `bg-card` for dark mode. `src/test/landing.test.tsx` pins the new headings (chip assertions use anchored regexes because labels recur in card body copy).

**Public homepage South Asian repositioning (Task 997, August 2, 2026; web public surface only):** the pre-login marketing surface repositions from "Indian languages" to "South Asian languages" everywhere (landing hero "Actually speak your family's language.", index.html title/description/OG/Twitter, choose-language, upgrade benefit lists, privacy/terms positioning prose; `bolo-india.app` domain/email strings and the authed-only `india-facts.ts` deliberately untouched). Landing rebuilt: hero (two CTAs, "Talk, don't tap" tagline, SpeakingDemo), diaspora-ordered language showcase (leaders Hindi/Punjabi/Urdu/Bengali/Tamil/Telugu/Gujarati/Marathi first via `diasporaOrdered()`; `FALLBACK_LANGS` is those eight incl. RTL Urdu; every chip deep-links to `/languages/<slug>`), five-step "how it works" with fresh product screenshots (`public/screens/*.webp`, ~102 KB total, `loading="lazy"`, band labels only, no raw scores), comparison block, families section (Family seat count from `FAMILY_SEATS`), and a pricing preview fed by the shared `src/lib/pricing.ts` (`TIER_PRICING` + `FAMILY_SEATS`; `upgrade.tsx` now imports it, so paywall and preview can never drift; NO daily-limit copy — the Free daily lesson cap was retired July 2026, `dailyNewLessonLimit()` returns null for all plans). 22 public per-language SEO routes at `/languages/:slug` (`src/pages/learn-language.tsx`, registered via `lazyRoute` + `PREFETCH_ORDER`; the `/learn/*` prefix stays with authed CategoryDetail). Page content, including 2-3 free-starter sample phrases with romanization per language, comes from the committed build-time data module `src/lib/languagePages.ts` (generated from `lib/db` seedData LANGUAGES + curatedLessons.json; regenerate on catalog/greetings changes — see its header). Head metadata mechanism: `src/lib/seo.ts` (`useDocumentHead` sets title/description/canonical/OG/Twitter per page at runtime — client-rendered SPA, no SSR; `SITE_ORIGIN=https://bolo-india.app` is a functional URL constant; `useHomepageStructuredData` injects Organization + SoftwareApplication JSON-LD, homepage-only, removed on unmount). `public/sitemap.xml` lists the homepage + all 22 language URLs; `public/robots.txt` references it. Public-surface analytics (the only pre-auth events; autocapture/pageviews remain off): `homepage_view`, `section_in_viewport` (IntersectionObserver, once per named section), `language_entry_click`, `per_language_page_view`, `cta_click` (placements hero-primary/hero-secondary/bottom-cta/pricing-free/pricing-allaccess/pricing-family/per-language-cta), and `signup_started` (attributed by source placement; distinct from `sign_up_completed`, which App.tsx still fires post-account-creation) — all in `analyticsEvents.ts` with no mobile mirror (mobile has no public marketing surface). QA: `qa/task997-howitworks-shots.mjs` (authed screenshot capture, strips the dev-preview banner) and `qa/task997-gate.mjs` (320/768/1280 responsive shots, horizontal-overflow probe, JSON-LD + per-page head validation). `qa/home-load-timing.mjs` after the rebuild shows no regression vs the pre-change baseline (heroVisible ~1.1-1.2s, no resource >800ms). `src/test/landing.test.tsx` covers the new sections, diaspora ordering, chip links, lazy screenshots with alt text, pricing from the shared config, the stale-strings guard, the per-language page (incl. RTL Urdu and not-found fallthrough), and every new analytics event name.

**SEO batch shell defaults (August 4, 2026, web only):** `index.html` static defaults carry the owner-approved positioning: title "Bolo! : Learn 22 South Asian Languages", the "first app built to teach 22 South Asian languages" description, a canonical link, og:url, og:image `https://bolo-india.app/og-image.png` (1200x630, with og:image:width/height) and twitter:image. A static SoftwareApplication JSON-LD block (no offers, no aggregateRating) ships in the shell under the id `bolo-structured-data`, deliberately shared with `useHomepageStructuredData` in `lib/seo.ts` so the runtime hook replaces its content on the homepage instead of adding a second block; runtime `applyDocumentHead` still owns all head tags per public page once JS runs. `public/og-image.png` is composed with ImageMagick from the canonical cheer mascot, the committed Bricolage Grotesque store fonts (bolo-mobile assets/store/fonts), and the brand gradient (#4F46E5 to #0D9488); no wordmark raster exists in the repo, so the wordmark is rendered text in the brand font. `sitemap.xml` adds `/contact`, `/privacy`, `/terms` (the homepage lastmod bumped to 2026-08-04, language pages unchanged); `robots.txt` was already compliant (allow all + sitemap reference) and is untouched.

**iOS App Store discovery (August 4, 2026, web only):** the shell carries Apple's Smart App Banner meta (`apple-itunes-app`, app-id 6790907772, sourced from `ascAppId` in `bolo-mobile/eas.json`), which only Safari on iOS renders. The landing hero shows the official Apple "Download on the App Store" badge (`public/appstore-badge.svg`, Apple's US-UK RGB black SVG) linking to `https://apps.apple.com/app/id6790907772`, rendered only when `isIosSafariWeb()` (`lib/iosAudio.ts`, reuse-first) reports an iOS user agent; Android and desktop render unchanged. Pre-release state: `APP_STORE_LIVE` (const beside `APP_STORE_URL` in `landing.tsx`, default false) keeps the badge muted (reduced opacity), unlinked, and untracked with a "Coming soon to the App Store" caption in the same slot; flip it to true at launch for the linked badge whose clicks track `cta_click` with placement `hero-appstore-badge`. The Landing component takes an optional `appStoreLive` prop defaulting to the const (a same-module const cannot be vi.mocked); tests drive both states through it. Covered in `landing.test.tsx` (pre-release muted + captioned + untracked, live linked + tracked, non-iOS renders neither).

**Home-screen shortcut invitation (August 7, 2026, web only):** the App Store badge markup moved out of the landing hero into `src/components/app-store-badge.tsx` (`AppStoreBadge`, exporting `APP_STORE_URL` and `APP_STORE_LIVE`) so the signed-in home page shows the same badge rather than a second copy. The extraction is behavior-preserving and DOM-identical: the component returns a fragment (linked `<a>` when live, muted `<img>` plus "Coming soon to the App Store" caption when not), the caller keeps the wrapper, its layout classes, its platform gate and any entrance animation, and `placement` is a required prop (`hero-appstore-badge` on landing, `home-appstore-badge` on home) so analytics stay per-surface. `landing.tsx` imports `APP_STORE_LIVE` for its `appStoreLive` prop default and is otherwise unchanged; `landing.test.tsx` passes unmodified. Bolo is NOT a PWA: no manifest, no service worker, nothing installable, so Android gets no install affordance and the copy calls a home-screen bookmark a shortcut, never an installed app. `src/components/add-to-home-screen.tsx` (`AddToHomeScreen`) renders at the bottom of `pages/home.tsx` inside `<main>` as secondary chrome (border-top, small muted type): platform-specific steps plus an honest "still opens in Safari/Chrome/your browser" note, with the badge beside it for iOS only. Platform comes from the NEW `src/lib/platform.ts` (`detectShortcutPlatform` returning `ios | android | unknown`, plus `isIpadOsDesktopUa`), which recognizes iPadOS 13+ via `navigator.platform === 'MacIntel' && maxTouchPoints > 1` because Safari there sends a Macintosh UA by default; `unknown` (desktop, unrecognized) gets neutral wording rather than a guess. `isIosSafariWeb()` in `lib/iosAudio.ts` and its two callers are deliberately untouched, so the signed-out hero's iPhone-only gate is unchanged. Covered in `home-add-to-home.test.tsx` (detection incl. iPad-vs-Mac, per-platform steps with no cross-contamination, badge coming-soon state on home, no promise of an installed app).
