The nav Link's `aria-label="Chat with Bolo"` carries the accessible name (the Mascot wrapper is `aria-hidden`); `bottom-nav.test.tsx` pins img-present with `mascot-wave.png` src inside the chat link.

**QA visual verification harness (maintained tooling, July 29, 2026):** `qa/home-overflow-probe.mjs` (horizontal-overflow detector: measures scrollWidth vs viewport at 390px, lists unclipped offenders, and hide-bisects down to the culprit element; `E2E_PATHS` picks routes — used to find the home boarding-pass overflow, where the grid track honored the ticket's nowrap min-content until `min-w-0` was added to both grid children), `qa/upgrade-paywall-shots.mjs` (paywall pricing, layout, and card-overlap checks at mobile and desktop widths, monthly and annual) and `qa/journey-map-e2e.mjs` (journey map scenarios: `teaser`, `plus`, `free`, and `picker`; `picker` is the real locked-picker-to-showroom navigation plus home/chat blast-radius checks). Both drive real chromium: `CHROME_BIN=$(which chromium) E2E_USER_ID=<clerk user id> E2E_SCENARIO=<name> NODE_PATH=/tmp/pw/node_modules node qa/<script>`. `qa/games-preview-shots.mjs` (Task 840) joins the set: /games hub card vignettes — running animations, staggered `--gv-delay` phases, reduced-motion settle check, light/dark + mobile/desktop shots (when `which chromium` is empty, the playwright-browsers Nix store chromium binary works as `CHROME_BIN`). `qa/nav-bolo-shots.mjs` (Task 838): bottom-nav rigged Bolo — svg-not-img in the chat button, ambient transforms actually changing over time, light/dark + /games shots, reduced-motion still-frame visibility (note /chat renders outside AppShell so it has no bottom nav — that skip is expected). Prereqs: chromium via system deps (not committed to replit.nix), playwright installed at `/tmp/pw` (a workspace restart wipes `/tmp`; reinstall with `npm i playwright`), a throwaway Clerk QA user signed in via the sign_in_tokens API, and `users.has_completed_tour=true` on that user or the first-run tour hijacks navigation. Screenshots land in `qa/shots/` (gitignored). Upcoming visual work should rerun these rather than building new harnesses.

Deck source of truth is the owner's copy deck paste; do not edit these strings ad hoc. (2) iOS web audio helpers at `src/lib/iosAudio.ts` (integrated as-is): `primeAudioUnlock()` is wired into session entry gestures (journey StationCard link, polish link, test-out confirm, home review/recent-plays/boarding-pass, category-detail practice + sentence links); chat needs no priming (its audio is blessed by the mic gesture). `playCoach` in practice.tsx now separates synthesis failure (deck card + "Play phrase" button) from `audio.play()` autoplay rejection (NotAllowedError/NotSupportedError -> pulsing ring on the existing speaker button + tap-to-hear caption) via `coachAudioFailed` state, cleared at the top of every playing_coach effect run. One-time iOS Safari silent-switch hint ("Hearing nothing?") renders under the phrase card on the first playing_coach per mount, gated on `isIosSafariWeb() && shouldShowIosAudioHint()`; "Got it" calls `markIosAudioHintShown()`. (3) Mascot `motion.img` swallows `onContextMenu` (long-press save-sheet suppression, iOS-device-only verification pending). (4) Privacy policy: retention section now has the voice-contribution paragraphs (bold inline "Voice recordings." lead-in), "contacting us"/"contact form" both link to `/contact`, `privacy@bolo-india.app` removed from the page, the "Your rights" access-and-deletion bullet points at "the contact form linked below" (no email exists on the page), the Sharing-and-third-parties speech-provider bullet carries the ruled dual-path qualifier ("In normal use... Recordings made through our invited voice contribution mode are retained as described in Data retention below."), and `LAST_UPDATED` = August 2, 2026. Canonical web test baseline as of this batch: **391 tests / 55 files** (was 384/54; the delta predates the batch).

**Demo-day polish pass (P1 v2, Task 823, July 29, 2026; web only, presentation only):** Six items. (1) Bottom nav: root cause of the nav scrolling away was `.animate-content-enter`'s `animation-fill-mode: both` leaving a persistent identity transform on page wrappers, which made them containing blocks so the nav's `position: fixed` became container-relative; fixed by switching the fill mode to `backwards` (visually identical end state). `BottomNav` now mounts once in `AppShell` OUTSIDE the `PageTransition` subtree (per-page mounts removed from home/progress/friends/games hub; the six game subpages keep theirs); `.pb-safe` was an undefined utility computing 0px — now a real `env(safe-area-inset-bottom)` utility in index.css, and index.html gained `viewport-fit=cover`. `/journey` main got `pb-28 lg:pb-14` to clear the nav. (2) Home hero: `home.tsx` boarding-pass hero in the line accent (station name, "Stop N of M", per-stop mastered bar, Begin/Continue action verb, streak + daily-goal chips on the ticket stub); live state comes from `src/lib/useJourneyProgress.ts`, which reuses the journey page's six `useListCategoryLessonGroups` queries (shared react-query cache) and degrades to `current: null` on loading/402/error. Topic grid header is "Browse by topic". New allowlisted analytics event `journey_entered_via_hero` (web `analyticsEvents.ts` only, payload = language code). Tests mocking `@workspace/api-client-react` as a module factory and rendering Home MUST now stub `useListCategoryLessonGroups` (entitlements-gating.test.tsx does). (3) Chat meter: presentation-only rework into a labeled free allowance ("2 free chat minutes each week", time-left counter, amber low state under 25% with an inline "Go unlimited" link, and a zero state that replaces the bar with an inline Go All-Access prompt). Expiry BEHAVIOR untouched: `disabled={capExhausted}` on both mic surfaces, the `startRecording` upgrade redirect, and `weekly_cap_exceeded` handling are byte-identical. The meter still only renders once the server reports `secondsRemaining` (unchanged condition). (4) `src/components/train-svg.tsx`: interim `currentColor` side-profile engine SVG, faces right; used in the journey current-station marker (replacing the 🚂 emoji) and the home hero. The bespoke artwork-batch train replaces it later. (5) `ZonePostcard` furniture: zone-number stamp corner, circular postmark, divided-back vertical rule, italic slightly-rotated geo-name caption (bundled fonts only); all tinted via the existing `color` variable so the D1b grayscale locked treatment inherits unchanged; "Fare zone N" copy preserved for the harness. (6) Hold-to-speak affordance: practice idle copy is now "Hold Bolo to speak" (aria-labels unchanged: belly zone still `Hold to speak`/`Release to submit`), idle pulsing ring on practice and chat mascots (omitted under reduced motion), pressed state via a plain CSS `scale` transform on an inner div — deliberately NOT a stacked framer `motion.div`, which would fight the amplitude MotionValue on the practice mascot. One-time first-practice hint bubble via localStorage `bolo.practice.hint.v1` (hook lives with the unconditional hooks ABOVE practice.tsx's loading/error early returns — placing hooks below them crashes with "Rendered more hooks"). Practice tests asserting the visible idle copy were updated to the new string. Recording logic untouched. New harness `qa/demo-polish-shots.mjs` (same env/pattern as the others; Clerk sign-in tokens are single-use, so it mints one per browser context) captures the matrix: home hero, journey top+mid-scroll, chat, practice idle+pressed at 430x900 and 1280x900. Web suite baseline unchanged (account.test.tsx x6 + chat-error-banner x2).

**Bottom-nav Bolo hero button (Task 837, July 29, 2026; web only, presentation only):** The centre Bolo circle in `bottom-nav.tsx` grew 56→80px (`h-20 w-20`, parrot img 36→56px) and its offset moved `-top-5`→`-top-11`. Geometry invariant: the circle's BOTTOM edge stays at row top + 36px — all extra size protrudes UPWARD, past the XP strip and ~13px above the nav's top border — so the row height, label position, and pb-safe interplay are untouched. The active (`/chat`) tint is now an opaque treatment (`bg-card` always on, tint via a `from-primary/15 to-primary/5` gradient overlay) so the full-width XP bar the circle overlaps never shows through; note `/chat` renders without a BottomNav (own layout), so the active state is only reachable if that changes. The "Bolo" label gained `leading-none` — without it the 15px line box clipped ~3px below the viewport when `pb-safe` is 0. Pages that mount the nav bumped mobile bottom padding pb-24→pb-28 (progress, friends, games hub + six game subpages; home/journey were already pb-28) to clear the taller protrusion. (5) Customer-facing copy: "Plus" → "All-Access" across the web app, and sell copy no longer promises "unlimited lessons"/daily-limit framing (pitches languages + features instead); internal identifiers (isPlus, PlusInterval, cancelPlus, plan codes, lucide Plus/UserPlus) intentionally untouched. Screenshot harness: `qa/task870-shots.mjs`. Mobile half of the copy sweep is a separate task.

**Build 30 batch 3, games + home cleanup, web half (July 30, 2026).** (1) All five games (Word Match, Phrase Builder, Speed Round, Listen and Pick, Bolo Quiz) speak target-language audio via the existing `useSynthesizeSpeech` + per-card base64 cache pattern: Word Match speaks the Indian card's nativeScript on flip (never the English card), Phrase Builder speaks a tile's nativeScript when placed (reorder taps stay silent), Speed Round speaks the prompt word; Bolo Quiz gained a per-question `audioCacheRef`. (2) One shared mute control: `src/lib/gameAudioPref.ts` (localStorage key `bolo.gameAudio`, `off` = muted, default unmuted) + `src/components/game-mute-button.tsx`, wired into all five play surfaces; muted skips synthesis calls entirely, not just playback. (3) Every game's active-play state has a persistent exit control; timed games (Speed Round, Bolo Quiz) confirm via `window.confirm` before discarding a run. (4) Listen and Pick wrong answers hold the reveal (red pick, green correct, replay) until a tap-to-continue; correct answers keep auto-advance. (5) Phrase Builder supports tap-based reorder of placed tiles (select, then tap another placed tile to swap; tap the selected tile again to return it to the tray). (6) Games hub Plus tiles fail closed: `plusReady = isPlus === true && !isLoading`, pinned in `games-hub.test.tsx` (loading and undefined cases). Note: the web home has NO Daily Quiz card and never had a ContinueCard equivalent (confirmed by search), so the quiz-rename and ContinueCard-deletion items were mobile-only. (7) One-time name prompt on home: `src/components/name-prompt-card.tsx`, shown only when Clerk `firstName` is null, saves via the PATCH /account/profile dual-write then `user.reload()`, dismissal persisted in localStorage (`bolo.namePromptDismissed`); web tests that render home and do not care about it stub it to null (needs a react-query provider + profile hooks otherwise). (8) Shared `lib/api-client-react/src/custom-fetch.ts` now has a 30s default AbortSignal timeout with a per-call `timeoutMs` override and a `RequestTimeoutError` (both re-exported from the package index). Chat SSE streaming bypasses customFetch entirely, so the timeout cannot collide with batch 2's audio streaming.

**Phrasebook door + tour removal (Task 906, July 31, 2026; web half).** (1) Home's topic grid is gone; in its place a Phrasebook door: `<section aria-label="Phrasebook">` in `src/pages/home.tsx` with a stretched Link to the new `/phrasebook` route, subtitle "Browse and practice any topic", a chip row of the first 3 categories deep-linking `/learn/:id` (mastered/total shown only when masteredCount > 0), and a dashed "+N more" chip when more than 3 exist. (2) New `src/pages/phrasebook.tsx`: the full topic library (grid moved from home, mastered/total line per card, back link to /app), fires the new allowlisted `phrasebook_opened` analytics event exactly once on mount with `{ language }` (added to `analyticsEvents.ts` on BOTH platforms; home does NOT fire it). Route registered lazily in App.tsx behind Guard+AppShell. (3) The guided tour is REMOVED from the web app entirely: deleted `src/lib/tour-context.tsx`, `src/components/guided-tour-overlay.tsx`, `src/components/tour-auto-launcher.tsx`, `src/test/tour-auto-launcher.test.tsx`; App.tsx/account.tsx/bottom-nav/desktop-nav stripped. The server-side `users.has_completed_tour` column and account-preferences `hasCompletedTour` API field remain (api-server was out of scope; see section 8). (4) Journey zone-title dedup: `src/pages/journey.tsx` resolves zone display titles from the `/categories` listing by id, with `JOURNEY_ZONES` hardcoded titles as a loading-state fallback only; the title-mismatch hard-stop guard is removed (a server-side rename now just renders). (5) Analytics: alongside `phrasebook_opened`, a new `TOPIC_OPENED` event fires from the door chips (source `home_chip`) and from phrasebook cards (source `phrasebook`); no pre-existing topic-open events existed. Door/page carry stable testids (`phrasebook-door`, `link-phrasebook-door`, `phrasebook-chip-*`, `phrasebook-topic-*`). (6) Express test-out shipped on web (closes the deferred task-819 gap): the progression lock dialog offers "Test out of this stop" (`link-test-out`) linking to `/practice/:zoneId?group=<id>&mode=testout`; practice.tsx in testout mode loads GET `/lesson-groups/:id/test-out` (envelope: phrases + sampleSize + requiredCorrect), saves NO per-phrase attempts and awards no XP (collects `evaluationToken`s in a ref), hides all retry controls (one take per phrase, banner `testout-banner`), and submits one POST batch at the end; verdict screen (`testout-summary`) shows pending/pass (Express stamp + confetti, invalidates the group listings)/fail (encouraging copy)/error (resubmit unless 429). Fail path returns to `/journey` (no in-place session reset, deliberate). (7) Progression dialog defect fixes: the manual duplicate X close button was removed (shadcn `DialogContent` renders its own) and the copy reads "finish the stop before this one to board here". Tests: `phrasebook-door.test.tsx` (home door, main variant), `home-phrasebook-door.test.tsx` (door testids + chip analytics), `phrasebook-page.test.tsx` (library page + analytics), `journey-zone-titles.test.tsx` (dedup), `journey-testout-dialog.test.tsx`, `practice-testout.test.tsx`. NOTE: any new hook import in practice/journey must be added to every full-replacement `vi.mock("@workspace/api-client-react")` factory in tests importing those pages (8 files patched for the testout hooks).

**Home upsell card reflow + badge-card return pin (Task 919, July 31, 2026; web only).** (1) `UpgradeCard` in `src/components/plus.tsx`: the title/badge row is now `flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1` and the h3 gained `min-w-0`. Rationale: `PlusPill` is deliberately `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap` (the 360px language picker relies on that never-wrap rule), so at narrow widths the pill must yield to the next line instead of squeezing the title into one-word-per-line wrapping or clipping the card edge; PlusPill itself is unchanged at all call sites. (2) Badge-card navigation: the home latest-badge card is a wouter push to `/progress`, which renders under Guard+AppShell, so the always-mounted nav (BottomNav below `lg`, DesktopNav at `lg`+) is the return path by convention (Progress is a primary tab; back buttons are reserved for non-tab pages like Friends). No code change; pinned instead. (3) Home card audit at 360/768/1280: page never overflows; `innerOverflow` flagged on the stats banner, boarding pass, and badge card is their absolutely-positioned decorative blobs clipped by each card's own `overflow-hidden` (by design, not the defect class); phrasebook door, chat shortcut, recent plays clean. Tests: `src/test/home-badge-card.test.tsx` (4: badge card lands on /progress + BottomNav Home returns, no-badge renders no card, UpgradeCard wrap structure + pill contract + href, PlusPill global shape). Live probe: `qa/task919-shots.mjs` (three-width measurement pass incl. pill-within-card / pill-overlaps-title rect checks, plus an authed badge-card click-through to /progress and back; seed a `badges` row with a REAL badge key like `first_phrase` for the QA account if it has none, then delete it). Web suite 42 files / 295 tests.

**Practice coach speaks the English meaning after each phrase (Task 1003, August 2, 2026; web only).** After the phrase clip's `ended` event in the practice playCoach chain, a second English-voiced segment plays: "means <translation>", or the translation alone when it reads as a sentence (`meaningSpeechText` in `src/lib/meaning-audio.ts`: caller sentence flag from sentence-stage sessions, sentence-final punctuation, or 6+ words). Two separate audio elements, never one mixed synthesis; the English segment synthesizes through the existing `/openai/tts` mutation with `languageName: "English"` / `languageCode: "en"`, so it lands under its own server cache key with no server changes. Synthesis (on a client session-cache miss, `meaningAudioCacheRef`) runs in parallel with a `MEANING_SEGMENT_PAUSE_MS` (400ms) sleep so cache hits keep the beat tight. State stays `playing_coach` through the meaning segment and its `onended` (or any failure or disabled path) sets idle, which is what makes barge-in / hold-to-record cleanup work through the existing effect teardown plus the explicit `meaningAudioElRef` pause in `startRecording`. Best-effort by contract: any meaning synthesis or playback failure falls back silently to phrase-only and NEVER touches `coachAudioFailed`. Controlled by a "Meaning" pill (Languages icon, aria-pressed) beside the Phrase and Feedback pills, default ON, persisted per device at `bolo.meaningAudio` ("on"/"off", `src/lib/meaning-audio.ts`), read fresh at play time via `meaningAudioPrefRef`. TEST TRAP: `src/test/setup.ts` now pins the preference OFF suite-wide in a `beforeEach` because the legacy practice tests drive a phrase-only chain; any practice test file that calls `localStorage.clear()` in its own beforeEach wipes that pin and MUST re-set `bolo.meaningAudio = "off"` after the clear (all current files do). Tests: `src/test/meaning-audio.test.ts` (pref + speech text), `src/test/practice-meaning-audio.test.tsx` (chain, toggle, fail-silent both paths, barge-in, session cache). Live probe: `qa/task1003-meaning-probe.mjs` (all checks green August 2, 2026). Mobile parity deliberately NOT shipped (later pass).

**Unresolved:** one inventory reported `src/pages/review.tsx` exists but does not call the eval endpoint. A later one reported the web app has no review screen. Resolve before writing any spec touching web review.

**Tiny UI batch, web half (Aug 2, 2026; items 1/3/5).** (1) Journey All-Access chip (`pages/journey.tsx`) now renders only when `station.stage === "sentence" && station.planLocked === true` — the server's serving verdict, not stage alone. Owner-confirmed consequence: Plus/Family callers see no chips anywhere (nothing is planLocked for them); the chip is purely an upsell marker on stops the caller cannot ride. The stage-based diamond rail marker is untouched. (2) `components/badge-unlock.tsx`: the celebration overlay gained a secondary "See progress" wouter Link to `/progress` (stopPropagation + onDismiss on click) under the primary "Awesome!" dismiss; the overlay is no longer a dead-end. (3) `/contact` route in `App.tsx` is now public (`<Route path="/contact" component={Contact} />`, Guard removed) — /privacy links signed-out readers there and Guard was bouncing them to the homepage; pairs with the API-side mount move (section 4). Browser-verified via `qa/tiny-ui-batch-probe.mjs` (chips keyed to planLocked on a fresh Free Hindi account: 5 Zone 1 sentence stops chipless, 25 plan-locked chipped; badge overlay "See progress" lands on /progress; both privacy links land on the rendered form signed-out).

**Plan-locked stops omit the phrase count (Aug 2, 2026, tiny UI batch 2 item 1; web only).** In `pages/journey.tsx` StationCard, the locked status line's count segment is now additionally gated on `station.planLocked !== true`. Plan-locked stations serve `phraseCount: 0` by design (S2 map honesty in `learning.ts`: counts reflect only plan-visible phrases), so they previously read "Locked · 0 phrases"; they now read just "Locked" with the lock icon. Progression-locked stops keep real counts ("Locked · 8 phrases"). Rendering change only; no entitlement, server, or gating logic touched. Mobile intentionally NOT changed (out of that batch's scope); its plan-locked stops still show the count line.

## 6. Mobile app (`artifacts/bolo-mobile`)

Expo, expo-router. `app/(app)/_layout.tsx` sets `headerShown: false` for Stack screens. Practice and review are Stack screens **outside** the `(tabs)` group, so both are full-screen with no tab bar and no system header.

| Path | Notes |
|---|---|
| `app/(app)/(tabs)/_layout.tsx` | Five slots: Home, Games, Bolo (center bubble), Progress, Language switcher (opens picker; Profile is NOT a tab — reachable from Home's top-right gear). Floating pill bar since the pre-28 polish pass |
| `app/(app)/(tabs)/index.tsx` | Home. Line 306 XpCounter chrome, 543/556 score badges |
| `app/(app)/(tabs)/progress.tsx` | `Stat` component, general streak with Feather `zap` labeled "Day streak" |
| `app/(app)/(tabs)/chat.tsx` | ~487/529 recorder start/stop |

Line numbers drifted in Task 901 — search by symbol |
| `app/(app)/review.tsx` | Line 244 `ReviewHeader` (settings gear + language chip on the phrase-card variant only, section 10ai), 257 XpCounter session, ~731/751 recorder start/stop, 764 token passthrough, 769 optimistic, 784 setResult, 842/852 header render, 924 session summary, 1060 title ternary. Line numbers have drifted — search by symbol |

| `components/TalkingMascot.tsx` | Chat wrapper, listening/talking/thinking overlays |
| `components/Confetti.tsx` | Hand-rolled Reanimated. 44 shape pieces, 25 in glyph mode |
| `components/BandPill.tsx`, `EmptyState.tsx`, `XpCounter.tsx` | (Task 2, Spec 1a). BandPill now only renders on nocatch result cards; scored bands use the ladder |

Playback goes through `lib/audio.ts` `playAssetAudio(source, onDone)` — mirrors `playBase64Audio`'s iOS earpiece mode-flip/keepAudioSessionActive/pause-before-remove logic for bundled assets. `practice/[id].tsx` result effect mirrors web: clip at result render (spoken-feedback pref gated), feedback races `FEEDBACK_AUDIO_TIMEOUT_MS` (8s), plays after `clip.finished`, token-guarded. Tests: `__tests__/practice-band-audio.test.tsx` (band-audio mocked at the module seam — jest can't transform mp3 requires) |

**Native-script fonts** (`constants/fonts.ts`): all 13 Noto script families are bundled via `@expo-google-fonts/*` packages — including Ol Chiki and Meetei Mayek (both bundled since the app's creation; an earlier audit note claiming they were missing was wrong). `SCRIPT_FONTS` maps backend `fontFamily` → loaded font. Meetei Mayek is mapped on **Android only** (Platform-gated since the fonts pass, July 28, 2026): on iOS the Expo-loaded font bypasses the OpenType shaper and mis-renders combining vowel marks, while iOS ships its own Noto Sans Meetei Mayek system fallback (since iOS 10) with correct shaping. Do not map it on iOS.

**Mascot poses (both platforms, same five PNGs):** `wave`, `cheer`, `thumbsup`, `thinking`, `tryagain`.

**Visual polish pass (July 29, 2026, mobile).** Loading: both auth gates (`app/index.tsx`, `app/(app)/_layout.tsx`) show a floating Mascot instead of a raw `ActivityIndicator`; word-match and listen-and-pick topic pickers show 6 `SkeletonCard` rows (64px, radius 16) and their phrase loads use `FunFactLoader`. Speed Round / Phrase Builder / Daily Quiz still show plain "Loading…" text (follow-up proposed). Empty states: shared `components/EmptyState.tsx` gained an optional `mascotPose` prop and a mount-only entrance (ZoomIn mascot pop + staggered FadeInDown text) guarded by `useAppearSkip()`; review passes `thumbsup`; friends' inline `EmptyFriends`/`EmptyLeaderboard` use the same inline pattern (test-asserted strings unchanged). Press feedback: `GlobeButton`, all three tappable `PlusUpsell` cards, and the chat skip button now use `PressableScale` (which supplies the light haptic — manual `hapticLight()` calls were removed to avoid doubles). Haptics: `hapticLight()` added to Google/Apple SSO button presses, the three account toggles, the theme `Segmented` control (only on actual change), and both reminder switches. All entrances use the `lib/entrance.ts` appear guards (Expo Go + reduced-motion safe).

**Silent mode** (`bolo.silentMode`) skips the coach's auto-played voice for each new phrase. Device-only, not synced. Does not mute feedback and does not affect recording. The web practice header (July 29, 2026) shows it and the spoken-feedback toggle as two matching labeled pills — PHRASE (Volume2/VolumeX) and FEEDBACK (Headphones/HeadphoneOff) — with ONE shared convention: teal filled = that audio plays, gray = off (they previously had opposite active states). Note the visual is inverted from the stored flag: PHRASE is teal when `silentMode` is **false** (`aria-pressed={!silentMode}`). Labels stay visible at all widths per user preference (progress bar has room).

**First-time language choice (B1 mobile parity, July 30, 2026).** `lib/language-step.ts`: in-memory module skip flag (session-scoped, NEVER AsyncStorage; `useLanguageStepSkipped()` is a `useSyncExternalStore` read so bootstrappers react to a skip; `resetLanguageStepSkipForTests()` simulates restart) + `useExplicitLanguageChoice()` — one PATCH `{activeLanguage, hasChosenLanguage: true}` merged into the account cache (web-helper mirror). `app/(app)/choose-language.tsx`: full-screen step, all languages, NO lock badging/dimming/paywall routing (aspirational pick), listening badge only for `speechCapability === 'unsupported'`, community footnote, Skip sets the session flag only. GATE REMOVED July 30, 2026 by product decision (same day, both platforms): `LanguageChoiceBootstrapper` deleted from `app/(app)/_layout.tsx` and `TourBootstrapper` no longer holds on the language step — fresh accounts land directly on home with the seeded default (Hindi) and the tour fires on first home load. The `choose-language` screen stays registered as a normal navigable route; the flag + one-PATCH helper infra is retained. The picker modal (`language.tsx`) now persists picks via the shared helper (`adoptLanguageLocally` on LanguageContext updates only the local mirror — going through `setActiveLang` too would double-PATCH; `setActiveLang` remains for the provider's internal seed/correction writes). Tests: `__tests__/language-choice-gate.test.tsx`, `__tests__/language-choice-step.test.tsx`. Build number unchanged (bump deferred to the map port).

**Mobile auth (July 28, 2026 fix).** Factor set: **password, email code, and SSO (Apple/Google)**. Factor selection is driven by Clerk's sign-in response — after `signIn.password()`, `signIn.status` and `signIn.supportedFirstFactors` decide the next step (a `needs_first_factor` response offering `email_code` auto-falls-over to the emailed-code step; web sign-ups are passwordless, so this is their only mobile path). `app/(auth)/sign-in.tsx` also has an explicit "Email me a sign-in code instead" entry (`signIn.emailCode.sendCode`/`verifyCode`). Shared error policy lives in `lib/authErrors.ts`: every auth operation ends in navigation, a user-visible error, or a code step; expected user-input errors (wrong password/code) show but do not go to Sentry; everything else is Sentry-captured with `authContext` tag; a flow that stops at a non-complete status surfaces **the status and offered factor strategies in the user-visible copy and the Sentry event** (never a generic error). Never pass emails/passwords/codes/factor objects (they carry `safeIdentifier`) into these helpers — strategy strings only.

**Journey map port (Spec D1b-M, July 30, 2026, headlines build 28).** Mobile port of the web `/journey` map — translation job, web is source of truth, zero api-server/web changes. `app/(app)/journey.tsx` (Stack screen outside `(tabs)`, registered in `app/(app)/_layout.tsx`): 6 per-zone `useListCategoryLessonGroups(zoneId, lang)` queries + `useListCategories`; serpentine track rendered as per-zone absolutely-positioned `react-native-svg` slices (viewBox `0 startY mapW sliceH`, 3-layer rail segments) inside a ScrollView — NOT one tall Svg (Android texture limits); stop markers/postcards/station cards are absolutely positioned RN views over the slices (a11y labels: "Stop N of M — StatusCopy"). Line/zone geo theming in `lib/journeyLines.ts` (22 lines, e.g. gu = Gujarat Express; fallback for unknown codes). Scenery (`components/journey/Scenery.tsx`) grayscales via palette-swap (COLORS/GRAYS), not filters (no SVG filters in rn-svg). Boarding-pass fittings in `components/journey/TicketParts.tsx` + `TrainEngine.tsx`; home hero pass `components/journey/JourneyPassCard.tsx` (driven by `lib/useJourneyProgress.ts`) sits above DailyQuizCard on home — ContinueCard intentionally kept below. Locked-stop dialog (RN Modal) has 3 kinds: progression, sentence (→ paywall), language showroom (teaser/exhausted → paywall with `{lang, reason}`); NO test-out on mobile (approved ruling). Showroom entry: locked rows in `language.tsx` adopt the language locally and open the map read-only (plain 402 summary → UpgradeRequiredScreen). To make that adoption survive, `contexts/LanguageContext.tsx` no longer auto-reverts a supported-but-locked active language to the first allowed one (mirrors the web D1b ruling that removed the same guard); only the unsupported-code fallback remains. Consequence: a free account can persist a locked activeLanguage (showroom, or post-downgrade) — journey/practice handle their own 402/403 states; since the pre-28 polish pass, home detects the summary 402 (`asUpgradeRequired`) and renders a showroom banner inside the stats gradient (Preview the journey / Unlock) instead of degraded zero stats, mirroring web home. Practice `?group=` scope: `practice/[id].tsx` accepts a `group` param, fetches via `useListLessonGroupPhrases` (must pass matching `queryKey` when using `enabled`), invalidates only that group key on completion, and renders a locked-stop card (not the error screen) on 403 `lesson_group_locked`. `react-native-svg` moved devDeps→deps. Tests: `__tests__/journey-map.test.tsx`, `journey-showroom.test.tsx`, `practice-group-locked.test.tsx`. NOTE: the new hooks forced `getListLessonGroupPhrasesQueryKey`/`useListLessonGroupPhrases`/`useListCategoryLessonGroups` stubs into ~38 existing test files' `@workspace/api-client-react` mock factories (inserted at factory top so file-specific keys still win), and the home test's rn-svg mock became a full passthrough set. **Build-28 full-screen ticket regression (fixed July 30, 2026, ships in build 29):** on a real iPhone (native only — Expo web looked perfect) both ticket surfaces grew to fill the viewport. Root cause: `TicketPerforationV` rendered `<Svg width={2} height="100%">` as a NORMAL-FLOW child of an indefinite-height stretch row — web resolves that percentage to auto, native Yoga measures the Svg and inflates strip → row → card unbounded. `TicketParts.tsx` now carries a sizing contract (header comment): no percentage-sized Svg in flow, ever; stripes + perforation Svgs render only after an onLayout measure, numerically sized, inside absolute wrappers. Belt caps: `JourneyPassCard` `maxHeight:240`, journey `headerTicket` `maxHeight:140` (both `overflow:'hidden'`; testIDs `journey-pass-card`/`journey-header-ticket`). Pinned by `__tests__/ticket-sizing.test.tsx` + a journey-map regression test (real Yoga layout is device-only; jest pins the bug's ingredients stay gone). `Scenery.tsx`'s percentage Svg is safe — absoluteFill inside the fixed 56px vista.

**Pre-28 mobile polish pass (July 30, 2026).** (1) Tab bar is a floating rounded pill: absolute-positioned, 14px side margins, `bottom: max(insets.bottom, 14)`, radius 32, height 74, 1px border, soft shadow (elevation 16 Android — parent elevation + the overflow-visible Bolo bubble verified on web only, not on a device). Active Home/Games/Progress icons sit in a brand-filled `TabIcon` pill (46×28, `primary` bg / `primaryForeground` icon). `TAB_BAR_CLEARANCE` in `components/Screen.tsx` raised to 124 web / 132 native. (2) The 5th slot (route name still `profile`) is `LanguageTabButton`: globe + uppercase active-lang code, pushes `/(app)/language`; the friend-request badge moved to Home's top-right gear button (`useListIncomingFriendRequests` now imported by home, and by every test mocking api-client that renders home). (3) `language.tsx` picker redesigned to match web: 2-col FlatList of rounded tiles, native script prominent (Nastaliq keeps lineHeight 52 + taller tile), full English name never truncated, single gold `MaterialCommunityIcons crown` corner glyph for locked (check-circle for active), header sub-line "Locked languages need All-Access…" when any lang is locked; locked-tile a11y label contract: "`{name}` — locked, preview its journey". (4) Customer-facing "Plus" copy swept to "All-Access" across mobile (paywall, PlusUpsell, subscription, family, voice, analytics, progress, chat, practice, games pill, billing-history `billingPlanLabel`); internal identifiers (`isPlus`, `PlusPill`, `plusOnly`, tier ids) intentionally unchanged. (5) Locked-adoption regression pinned in `__tests__/language-locked-adoption.test.tsx` (supported-but-locked survives; unsupported code still falls back). Screenshot harness: `qa/pre28-shots.mjs`. (6) Boarding-pass fixes (home hero + journey header): the eyebrow brand is per-language native-script "Bolo Rail" via `getRailBrand(code)` in `lib/journeyLines.ts` (20 script entries; sat/mni deliberately fall back to Latin "BOLO RAIL" — untrusted transliteration beats wrong glyphs), rendered in a nested `<Text>` with `nativeTextStyle` (the Latin UI font renders tofu for Indic scripts; `isTallCascadingScript` adds lineHeight room for Nastaliq — journey test fonts mocks must stub it). Stub layout made deterministic: the rotated ZoneStamp sits in a fixed centered slot (56×56 home / 52×52 journey header) so it can't straddle the perforation; the vertical line name is an ABSOLUTE rotated Text inside a measured slot with explicit `maxWidth` — react-native-web's Text base style has `maxWidth:'100%'` which otherwise clamps an explicit width to the 14px slot (one-glyph truncation), and a bare rotated Text only reserves its unrotated box (the original stamp/name collision). Verified via `qa/pass-shots.mjs` + `qa/pass-probe.mjs` (Devanagari + Gujarati headers, stamp/name rects). Cutout ruling (approved): punch/notch circles may ONLY straddle card edges as bites (perforation ends + edge midpoints) — never float inside the card. The web hero's floating `PunchHole` was deleted from mobile `TicketParts.tsx`, and the journey header's floating notch dot + 🎫 emoji (tofu without an emoji font) were dropped from the port; `qa/notch-probe.mjs` measures every background-colored circle in both cards and flags FULLY-INSIDE vs STRADDLES-EDGE.

**Build 30 batch 1: journey layout fixes (July 30, 2026).** (1) Journey header safe-area: `journey.tsx`'s main render keeps `padTop={false}` but the header now pads itself with `paddingTop: 10 + headerTopInset` where `headerTopInset = Platform.OS === 'web' ? 67 : insets.top` (matches Screen's own web chrome offset); loading/error branches use a plain `<Screen>` and need nothing. testID `journey-header`. ZoneStamp's name Text lost `numberOfLines` (wraps on spaces, can never ellipsize; testID `zone-stamp-name`), letterSpacing only at fontSize 7. Slots sized from the extent: `JourneyPassCard` stampSlot (`home-stamp-slot`) and the journey header stub slot (`header-stamp-slot`); the old fixed 56/52px slots clipped the rotated corners. **Build 30 batch 2: chat record trigger + reply audio (July 30, 2026).** Three device-reported build 29 chat defects fixed, owned files `(tabs)/_layout.tsx`, `chat.tsx`, `lib/audio.ts`. (1) Dead nav-bird hold-to-talk: `BoloTabButton` read only `accessibilityState?.selected`, but the installed `@react-navigation/bottom-tabs` v7 passes the selected flag to custom `tabBarButton` renderers as `aria-selected` on native and passes NO `accessibilityState`, so `focused` was always false on device — pressIn never fired the haptic or the registered start handler (press-scale styling is unconditional, hence "visual feedback but nothing happens"). Jest missed it because both tab suites mocked the renderer with `accessibilityState`; `nav-bar-hold-to-talk.test.tsx` now mocks the real v7 shape. The button accepts both shapes (`ariaSelected ?? accessibilityState?.selected ?? false`). (2) Barge-in orphaning: the `wasPlaying` branch of `handleStartRecording` now bumps `activeTurnRef` (previously only `wasProcessing`) — with progressive voice streaming the SSE turn is still open while phase is `playing`, and a late `reply` payload from the interrupted turn could flip the phase back to `playing` mid-recording. (3) Reply loudness/quality mismatch: all chat players (`playSquawk` in chat.tsx, `playBase64Audio`/`playStreamingAudio` in lib/audio.ts) now pass `keepAudioSessionActive: true` to `createAudioPlayer`. Without it, expo-audio schedules an AVAudioSession `setActive(false)` 0.1 s after ANY player finishes or pauses unless another registered player is strictly `.playing` — and a buffering AVPlayer (streamed reply, first bytes 300+ ms out) does NOT count as playing, so the squawk chirp finishing mid-buffer deactivated the session the playback-mode flip had just configured. The greeting is the only clip never preceded by another player's completion, which matches "greeting loud and clear, later replies quiet and degraded" exactly. TTS content was ruled out empirically (probe: buffered vs `stream_format:"audio"` synthesis of identical text, both 24 kHz/128k mono mp3, streamed ~3 dB LOUDER). Exact audible outcomes are device-verify-only. Tests: `__tests__/lib-audio-playback-mode.test.ts` (mode-flip ordering, playbackModeToken races, keepAudioSessionActive pins, against the real lib/audio) and nav-bar suite section 6 (barge-in through the registered wrapper, turn orphaning, squawk option pin). Intentional asymmetry left in place and documented: the on-screen mascot Pressable stays `disabled` during `playing` (no barge-in from the mascot; nav bird only).

## 7. Assets, libraries, and audio

- **Animation:** framer-motion on web, react-native-reanimated on mobile. No Rive, no Lottie, no canvas-confetti.
- **Mascot:** every surface (web, mobile app, video artifacts, legal-page header, hosted email images) renders the five canonical pose PNGs; on web `Mascot` animates them as whole images (crossfade/bob/tilt — see the mascot.tsx row). The Task 836 SVG rig was retired July 30, 2026 (non-canonical art) and `bolo-rig.tsx` must stay unreferenced. No Rive state machine exists.
- **Confetti:** hand-rolled on both platforms, not a library.
- **Sound cues:** a `playCue` layer exists on both platforms (Spec 1 v3) but **no cue audio files exist**. Mobile `CUE_SOURCES` is empty; web 404s silently. Cues are silent no-ops until assets ship.
- **Coach TTS playback already existed** and is separate from the cue layer.
- **Band result clips (Task 903, July 31, 2026):** six bundled mp3s per platform — `perfect, great, good, almost, retry, nocatch` — in web `public/sounds/bands/` and mobile `assets/sounds/bands/` (0.8–1.7s each). Generated once by `artifacts/api-server/scripts/genBandClips.ts` (gpt-4o-mini-tts, voice nova = the feedback voice, per-clip tone instructions; nocatch is deliberately NEUTRAL per the system-miss rule), loudness-normalized with ffmpeg loudnorm I=-16 (24kHz mono 48k). They speak English band names, so the set is voice-static, not per-language — regenerate with the script if the default TTS voice ever changes. Playback wiring: see the `band-audio.ts` rows in §5/§6 and `lib/feedbackTts.ts` in §4. Live-verified July 31, 2026 via `qa/task903-band-audio-probe.mjs`: band clip starts ~20ms after the pronunciation response (no synthesis wait), feedback sentence follows after the clip, server prewarm join observed as `hit: "pending-prewarm"`.

### Audio recording

**Web:** custom `useVoiceRecorder` hook at `lib/integrations-openai-ai-react/src/audio/useVoiceRecorder.ts`. Uses `MediaRecorder` and holds the `getUserMedia` `MediaStream` in `streamRef` with its own `AudioContext` and `AnalyserNode` computing RMS levels. **Microphone processing is explicit, not the browser's default** (August 11, 2026): all three acquisition paths — `prepare()`, the lazy fallback inside `startRecording`, and the post-stop background re-warm — request the exported `SPEECH_AUDIO_CONSTRAINTS` (`echoCancellation: true, noiseSuppression: false, autoGainControl: false`). Never reintroduce `{ audio: true }`; see §10ad. Pinned by `artifacts/gujarati-coach/src/test/use-voice-recorder-constraints.test.ts`. Since Spec D2 the analyser is **always** created in `startRecording` (silence detection reuses it via `readRms()`), and the hook exposes `getAmplitude()` — a pull-based 0..1 value (RMS x5, clamped). Callers sample it themselves (rAF loop); it never triggers React renders.

**Mobile:** `expo-audio`. `recorder.record()` and `stopAndReadRecording(recorder)`. Metering is enabled via `isMeteringEnabled: true` in `RECORDING_PRESET`. `RECORDING_PRESET` records 16 kHz mono at **96 kbps** (was 32 kbps until August 11, 2026; see §10ad). `bitRate` must stay a TOP-LEVEL preset field — expo-audio's `createRecordingOptions` spreads the common options first and the platform block second, so a `bitRate` added to the `ios` block would silently shadow it and leave iOS on the old rate. Pinned by `artifacts/bolo-mobile/__tests__/recording-preset.test.ts`, which drives expo-audio's real flattener. `useAudioRecorderState(recorder, 60)` (60ms in practice and review since Spec D2; was 250ms) exposes a **dBFS** `metering` value, also used for adaptive silence auto-stop — auto-stop timing is wall-clock based, so the faster poll does not change its behaviour.

> Metering gotchas: the value is dBFS (logarithmic, mostly negative), so map roughly -50..0 onto 0..1 with clamping — use `meteringToAmplitude()` in `lib/audio.ts`, never a second inline mapping. A linear read looks dead until someone shouts.

**Visualisation (Spec D2):** web `SoundWavePulse` (`src/lib/motion.tsx`) accepts an optional `amplitude?: MotionValue<number>`; with the prop it renders audio-driven bars, without it it keeps the old time-based loop. Mobile has `components/Waveform.tsx` (7 Reanimated bars off a `SharedValue`, reduced-motion fallback = 5-dot static level meter). Both practice/review screens scale the mascot 1.0-1.08 with live amplitude (MotionValue on web, SharedValue on mobile — never React state) and show a "We can't hear you" hint after >1.5s of near-silence. Reduced motion disables the mascot scale and swaps the waveform for the static meter (`prefersReducedMotion()` at the sampling sites).

## 7a2. Email sending via Resend (July 28, 2026)

Two distinct Resend mechanisms coexist:

1. **Replit Resend connector** (`@replit/connectors-sdk`, no API key in app secrets) — used by friend invites (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/inviteEmail.ts`) and family-plan invites (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/familyInviteEmail.ts`). Both call `connectors.proxy("resend", "/emails", ...)` — the proxy must be addressed by **connector name** (`"resend"`), not the `conn_…` connection id (the id form stopped resolving and returns 404 "no connection found"). The connector's key is send-only (GET `/emails` returns 401 `restricted_api_key`), so delivery status cannot be queried through it.
2. **Raw `resend` npm SDK with `RESEND_API_KEY`** — used by ElevenLabs quota alerts (`quotaAlertEmail.ts`; sender `ELEVENLABS_ALERT_FROM` ?? `Bolo! <onboarding@resend.dev>`) and the contact form (`routes/contact.ts` → `resendClient.ts`; sender `RESEND_FROM` ?? `noreply@boloapp.in`, which is NOT a Resend-verified domain and 403s — see debt table).

**Invite CTA + template (July 28, 2026):** CTA destination is `INVITE_CTA_URL` (dev+prod env = `https://bolo-india.app`; code default is the same web URL — flip the env var to the App Store link at iOS launch, no code change). Both invite templates carry a properly hidden preheader div and a hosted mascot header image (`https://bolo-india.app/mascot/mascot-wave.png` — hosted, not base64, because Gmail strips data URIs). Family invites' CTA remains the personal join link.

**Contact-form sender:** `RESEND_FROM` (dev+prod env = `hello@bolo-india.app`), no hardcoded fallback — `fromAddress()` throws if unset, caught by the best-effort send path (logs + `email_sent=false`).

**Invite sender address:** `INVITE_FROM_EMAIL` (server-side env var, both dev and production; no `VITE_*` involvement). There is deliberately **no hardcoded fallback** — a missing value throws at send time. Dev and production Replit env vars are set to `hello@bolo-india.app` (the bolo-india.app domain is Resend-verified: DKIM/MX/SPF green). Because it is a server-side env var (not a deployment secret and not a build-time `VITE_*` value), the production env var is read at runtime by the deployed api-server; the workspace-secrets-baked-into-Vite trap in §8 does not apply.

## 7b. Observability (Sentry + PostHog, July 28, 2026)

**Sentry (error reporting, all three apps).** Initialized ONLY when the DSN env var is present; without it every call is a no-op. Each init scrubs PII in `beforeSend`/`beforeBreadcrumb`: emails masked by regex, and keys like `transcript`, `audioBase64`, `nativeScript`, `romanized`, `english`, `email*` redacted. `tracesSampleRate: 0` (errors only), `sendDefaultPii: false`, Sentry user = id only.

| App | Init file | DSN env var | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| api-server | `src/lib/sentry.ts` (imported FIRST in `index.ts`) | `SENTRY_DSN` | `Sentry.setupExpressErrorHandler(app)` + a new global express error handler in `app.ts` (500 JSON, logs via pino). `@sentry/node` is in build.mjs `external`. Dev-only test route `GET /api/__sentry-test` (throws; absent when NODE_ENV=production) |
| gujarati-coach | `src/lib/sentry.ts`, called from `main.tsx` | `VITE_SENTRY_DSN` | Dev-only verification: open with `?sentry_test=1`. `setSentryUser` synced from Clerk in `App.tsx` `AnalyticsIdentitySync` |
| bolo-mobile | `lib/sentry.ts`, called at module load in `app/_layout.tsx`; root export is `Sentry.wrap(RootLayout)` | `EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_DSN` | app.json plugin is `["@sentry/react-native/expo", { organization: "lark-enterprises-llc", project: "bolo-mobile" }]`; source map upload at EAS build still needs `SENTRY_AUTH_TOKEN` as an EAS secret (see debt). Dev-only verification: `EXPO_PUBLIC_SENTRY_TEST=1` |

**PostHog (product analytics, both clients).** Event names live in ONE constants file per app — `src/lib/analyticsEvents.ts` (web) and `lib/analyticsEvents.ts` (mobile) — the deliberate, complete set: `sign_up_completed`, `language_selected`, `first_practice_session_started`, `first_phrase_attempted`, `session_completed`, `paywall_viewed`, `purchase_completed`. Wrappers in `src/lib/analytics.ts` / `lib/analytics.ts` (`track`, `trackOnce` — once per browser/install via localStorage/AsyncStorage `bolo.analytics.once.*`, `identifyUser` — Clerk user id only). Autocapture, pageviews, and session recording OFF. Init only when `VITE_POSTHOG_KEY` / `EXPO_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_KEY` present (`VITE_POSTHOG_HOST`/`EXPO_PUBLIC_POSTHOG_HOST` optional, default US cloud). **Payload rule: no phrase content, transcripts, audio, or email — user id and language code only** (plus numeric counts).

Hook sites: web `App.tsx` (sign-up via `user.createdAt` < 2 min, also identity sync), `account.tsx` `handleChangeLanguage`, `practice.tsx` (session start effect, evaluate call, summary transition), `upgrade.tsx` (`Paywall` mount, `?checkout=success`). Mobile `app/_layout.tsx` (`AnalyticsIdentitySync`, same createdAt rule — covers email/Google/Apple without touching auth screens), `language.tsx` `choose`, `practice/[id].tsx` (list-loaded effect, evaluate call, done transition), `paywall.tsx` (mount, `runPurchase` success).

**Correction to section 5:** web practice's summary state literal is `"summary"` (line ~791 `setState("summary")`), not `"done"` as previously written; `"done"` is mobile-only (`phase === 'done'`).

## 7c. D1a Slice 1 — lesson grouping data layer (July 28, 2026)

- NEW table `lesson_groups` (lib/db/src/schema/lessonGroups.ts): the journey-map "station" unit — ordered partitions of a (language, category)'s phrases into ~10-phrase chunks. Unique (language_code, category_id, position) — NOT the spec's (category_id, position), because categories are shared across languages. `title` null pending fare-zone/station naming.
- NEW nullable columns `phrases.lesson_group_id` (FK) and `phrases.lesson_group_position` (1-based within group).
- Migration: `0023_lesson_groups` (drizzle generated it as a duplicate `0022_` prefix — artifact of the healed journal; file + journal tag renamed to 0023 by hand, DDL untouched).
- Grouping backfill: `artifacts/api-server/src/scripts/backfillLessonGroups.ts`, run at startup after scoring v2 (advisory lock 727_003). Idempotent: skips any (language, category) that already has groups. Ordering tiebreak inside a stage block: `(sort_order, id)`. Stage blocks (phrase, then sentence) are never interleaved within a group; positions number phrase-stage groups first. Tail chunks of ≤4 merge into the previous group (sizes mostly 8-13; a stage block of ≤7 with no previous group stays small — observed mins 5-7 in gu/mni/brx).
- Result on migration day: 620 groups over 132 (language, category) pairs, 22 languages, median size 10, ZERO unassigned phrases; concatenation property (group order reproduces (sort_order, id) order per stage) verified for gu/hi/ta.
- NEW endpoints (both additive; openapi.yaml + regenerated clients): `GET /categories/{id}/lesson-groups/{lang}` → `{ lessonGroups: [{id, position, title, phraseCount, attemptedCount, masteredCount}], unassignedCount }` (progress derived at read time from attempts; no stored counters); `GET /lesson-groups/{id}/phrases` → same per-phrase shape as the category listing (Phrase schema), ordered by lesson_group_position, premium-filtered identically. Existing endpoints untouched.
- The existing `lessons` table is a per-(language, category) AI-content-cache record — a MISNOMER kept as-is (renaming is non-additive; deferred cleanup, see debt). The journey-map grouping is `lesson_groups`.
- Tests: `learning.lesson-groups.test.ts` (endpoints + partitionIds unit).
- Post-review hardening: migration `0024_phrase_group_position_unique` adds a UNIQUE index on `phrases (lesson_group_id, lesson_group_position)` (NULLs never conflict), guarding the concatenation invariant against future writers. Since July 29, 2026 the backfill runs AFTER the server listens (listen-first startup), so a replenisher write can in principle race the first-boot backfill window; both sides hold advisory locks and the unique index plus retry-then-fallback bounds the damage to a NULL-group insert that self-heals on a later backfill; a cross-scope composite FK (group and phrase agreeing on language/category) and a race regression test are deferred to Slice 2 (see debt).

### D1a Slice 2, lesson group unlock model (July 28, 2026)

- NEW tables (migration `0026_slice2_unlock_model`, generated as duplicate `0025_` prefix and renamed by hand pre-application; DDL reordered so the composite FK follows its target unique constraint): `lesson_group_progress` (composite PK user_id + lesson_group_id; only `tested_out` is persisted today, all other statuses are derived at read time) and `lesson_group_testouts` (append-only submission log: user, group, passed, created_at; keeps history for future rate limiting).
- Composite-FK scope hardening: `lesson_groups` gains UNIQUE (id, language_code, category_id); `phrases` gains composite FK (lesson_group_id, language_code, category_id) referencing it, MATCH SIMPLE so NULL-group rows stay legal. A phrase can no longer point at a group in a different language or category. This closes the Slice 1 "scope not DB-enforced" debt row.
- Unlock derivation (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/lessonGroupUnlock.ts`, pure): statuses are locked / unlocked / in_progress / completed / tested_out. First group always unlocked; a group unlocks when the previous one is completed or tested_out. Completed = at least 80% of the group's phrases at bestScore >= 80 (the attempts-based mastery signal; FSRS remains review-scheduler only). Completed takes precedence over tested_out. Monotonicity is enforced by a LATCH (post-review fix): replenishment grows a group's denominator with fresh phrases, which would dilute a completed ratio below the threshold, so the lesson-groups GET persists a `completed` progress row the first time completion is observed and the derivation honors persisted completed/tested_out rows regardless of the live ratio. Nothing ever re-locks.
- Entitlement precedence: all deny* gates run BEFORE unlock computation; a gated caller gets the 402 payload and never sees unlock state.
- Test-out endpoints (additive, in openapi.yaml + regenerated clients): `GET /lesson-groups/{id}/test-out` returns a fresh random sample (5 phrases, or all accessible if fewer) plus sampleSize and requiredCorrect = ceil(0.8 x n); `POST` validates server-signed evaluationTokens (user match, phrase-in-group, distinct, exact sample count), passes on requiredCorrect attempts at a full-credit band (`isFullCreditBand`, legacy `nailed`), records every submission, and upserts `tested_out` progress on pass. Retryable with a new sample. If every phrase in a group is premium-filtered away, the assessment itself 402s (feature_locked).
- `GET /categories/{id}/lesson-groups/{lang}` now returns an optional additive `status` per group.
- Replenisher insert-time assignment (strategy A, cap 14): new phrase-stage rows append to the LAST phrase-stage group while it is under 14 phrases; overflow creates a new phrase group at (last phrase position + 1) and shifts sentence-stage groups up by one, all in a single transaction (two-phase sign-flip position update to dodge the unique index). Retries once on unique violation, then falls back to the legacy NULL-group insert (surfaces via unassignedCount rather than failing the request). Progress keys on group id, never position, so shifts cannot orphan progress.
- Tests: `learning.lesson-groups-unlock.test.ts` (derivation units, endpoint statuses, test-out pass/fail/forgery/402 precedence, replenisher append + overflow shift + concurrent-writer race). Replenish test cleanups now delete `lesson_groups` (and the new suite deletes `lesson_generations`) because the replenisher creates groups at insert time.

| Token balance writers do not share a lock (pre-existing, surfaced August 11, 2026 by the Chai pack review) | `grantTokensDetailed` and `spendTokens` (`lib/tokenService.ts`) read the state row WITHOUT `FOR UPDATE`, then update the balance with an atomic SQL expression. The balance therefore stays arithmetically correct under concurrency, but two effects follow: a ledger row's `balanceAfter` audit column can record a stale value, and a spend that read a positive balance can land after a concurrent large `applyChaiAdjustment` reversal, taking the balance below the zero floor that function enforces. `unlockStop` already locks (it must, because it derives a budget); nothing else does. Fixing it properly means putting every writer behind the same row lock or adding a `balance >= 0` CHECK constraint — deliberately NOT done inside the Chai pack task, whose scope excluded existing sinks. Exposure today is a learner spending at the exact moment of a rare manual correction |

| Scoring jitter is larger than most effects anyone will try to measure (August 11, 2026, Noise bench) | The same clip, same condition, transcribed twice, moves the score 5.5 points on average and **changes the displayed band 18% of the time** — i.e. roughly one attempt in five would band differently on a re-run, purely from transcription variance. Not a regression introduced by anything; it is the standing noise floor of the transcript-centric path (±3.8 points on an 80-clip sample). No user-facing decision has been taken on it. Any future scoring or "accuracy" work must size its sample against this floor or it will measure luck |

Scripts: `generateSentenceTopupsRollout.ts` / `qaSentencesRollout.ts` / `pruneRolloutMajors.ts` (pnpm scripts `generate-sentences-rollout`, `qa-sentences-rollout`, `prune-rollout-majors`; the first two take `--langs`, plus `--model/--out/--categories` and `--model/--in/--out` for experiments). `sentenceCount(slug, lang)` now derives from the rollout file for non-gu languages; ALL curated-file seed/validation consumers MUST go through `curatedLessonsWithC1()` (mirrors `gujaratiLessonsWithC1`), and `generateSentences` accepts an optional `model` override. Two-check QA on all 5,418 generated rows: 12 languages landed at pilot-level major rates (8.9-20.2 percent), 4 elevated (as 30.6, doi 36.4, sa 37.6, mai 41.1) converged after one prune-and-regenerate round; final residual major rates 1.2-18.6 percent (report `qa-c1-rollout-report.json`, describes shipped rows only). WITHDRAWN: kok, ks, sat, brx, mni stay at the 8-sentence curated base; both gpt-5.4-mini and a full-size gpt-5.4 generation+judging experiment (`experiment-c1-full.json`, `qa-c1-experiment-report.json`, one category each) failed at 67-100 percent major rates, dominated by sentence-gloss meaning mismatches. These 5 overlap the degraded/unsupported STT tier; their path is Bhashini probe plus native-speaker validation, not more LLM generation. Dev-DB note: gu/greetings carries 8 pre-pilot runtime-replenished sentences (source NULL), 4 of which text-collide with pilot JSON entries, so gu shows 55 there and 254 total `generated_c1` instead of 258; append-only seeding, prod unaffected |
| Lesson-group sentence top-up pass (July 29, 2026) | `backfillLessonGroups.ts` gained a second, append-only pass: unassigned sentence-stage rows in already-grouped (language, category) pairs get NEW groups positioned after the pair's current max position; existing groups and membership are never touched (completed is latched). Runtime never inserts sentences, so every unassigned sentence row is seeder content. A batch at or below the merge threshold (4) waits for more content rather than forming a tiny group |
| Curated provenance NULL in pre-existing environments | Rows seeded before the `source` column exists keep NULL (the seeder only stamps rows it inserts). Only `generated_c1` is reliable as a filter; `curated` appears only on fresh-DB seeds. Do not treat `source IS NULL` as runtime-generated |
| Pronunciation/TTS routes skip the language gate | `POST /openai/pronunciation` and the TTS route in `openai.ts` never call `denyLockedLanguage`; only the chat route gates by language. A locked-language client that already holds phrase content can synthesize audio and get evaluations for it. Pre-existing, observed during the M1 teaser work (July 29, 2026), deliberately NOT changed then. If closed later, the gate must honor the teaser exception for teaser phrase ids |
| Feedback TTS prewarm skips the ElevenLabs provider (Task 903, July 31, 2026) | `prewarmFeedbackTts` no-ops when `TTS_PROVIDER === "elevenlabs"` because per-user voice preferences make the client's eventual cache key unpredictable at eval time. With ElevenLabs active (flag currently false), feedback synthesis pays the full round trip again — the band clip still plays instantly, only the follow-on sentence is slower. If the flag flips, extend the prewarm to resolve the caller's voice pref at eval time |

Count failures only from a complete captured log (`pnpm run test > file 2>&1`), never from the truncated workflow preview |

Parity is a deliberate later pass: port the meaning segment, fail-silent contract, and a device-persisted toggle to the mobile practice flow |

| TRAP: publish diff reads the committed migration chain (July 29, 2026) | The publish schema sync appears to compute its delta from the committed migration chain, not only the live dev database, so a committed-but-unapplied migration enters the publish delta. Never stage a migration by leaving it unapplied. Observed during the 0026 composite-FK ordering failure: with the re-add migration (0027) committed but unapplied, publish kept emitting `phrases_lesson_group_scope_fk` before its target unique constraint and failed; with 0027 removed from the tree the publish reported success. Support ticket filed for the FK-before-unique ordering bug. UPDATE July 29, 2026: trigger fallback executed (see the trigger-fallback row) — the FK is dropped from the committed schema (0029), so PUBLISHING IS UNBLOCKED pending the owner's generated-migrations panel pre-flight: the delta must contain NO `ADD CONSTRAINT phrases_lesson_group_scope_fk` line (expected: two lesson_group_* CREATEs, the unique constraint ADD, phrases.source ADD COLUMN). If an FK ADD CONSTRAINT still appears, STOP and rethink |
| Lesson-group scope enforced by TRIGGERS, not FK (July 29, 2026) | `phrases_lesson_group_scope_fk` dropped (migration 0029) as the fallback for the publish FK-before-unique engine bug; the same invariant (phrase's lesson_group must match its language_code and category_id; NULL lesson_group_id unconstrained) is enforced by `phrases_lesson_group_scope_trg` (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF the three scope columns) plus parent-side `lesson_groups_referenced_guard_trg` (BEFORE DELETE OR UPDATE of key columns while referenced). Both raise SQLSTATE 23503 so FK-shaped error handling still matches. DDL lives in migration 0030 AND an idempotent api-server startup guard (`ensureLessonGroupScopeTriggers`, runs BEFORE the lesson-group backfill, logs "created" vs "already present (no-op)" distinctly — the first prod boot's log line is part of post-publish verification). KNOWN GAP: triggers lack the FK's cross-row locks, so a concurrent insert-phrase/delete-group race can slip through under READ COMMITTED; no group-delete path exists today, and any future one must take explicit locking. Restore procedure for the declarative FK: docs/trigger-fallback-lesson-group-scope.md section 7 (re-add FK and drop triggers in ONE migration, remove the startup guard) |

| TRAP: Clerk expo one-shot `signIn.password()` is NOT complete-or-throw | `signIn.password()` (Clerk "future" hooks API; there is no separate `attemptFirstFactor`) can return without error at a non-complete status (`needs_first_factor` for passwordless accounts, `needs_second_factor`, etc.). Code that assumes error-or-complete swallows those states silently — this was the July 2026 production sign-in failure. Always branch on `signIn.status` + `supportedFirstFactors`, and surface unexpected statuses in the UI and Sentry (see §6 mobile auth) |
| ~~TRAP: mobile sign-up verification step was inescapable~~ | **Resolved (August 5, 2026).** iOS build 34: a typo email left the learner on the 6-digit code step with only "Verify & continue" and "Send a new code". The step is derived from server state and the pending sign-up lives on the Clerk **Client**, whose credential (`__clerk_client_jwt`) sits in the iOS Keychain via the Expo token cache, so force-quit, app deletion + reinstall, and sign-out all returned to it (sign-out returns early with no session, and with a session takes Clerk's `client.removeSessions()` branch, which preserves the client). `client.destroy()` is the only call that resets `client.signUp`. Fix: `artifacts/bolo-mobile/lib/abandonSignUp.ts` (`abandonSignUpAttempt`) plus "Use a different email" and "Back" controls rendered ONLY in the awaitingCode branch. The helper refuses when `clerk.session` exists, because destroying the client while signed in would be a silent sign-out. Coverage: `__tests__/sign-up-abandon-attempt.test.tsx` |
| TRAP: migration ghost-apply (general pattern) | drizzle migrate against the shared dev DB can record a migration's hash row in `__drizzle_migrations` WITHOUT executing its DDL. Detection: `SELECT to_regclass('public.<table>')` (or check the specific column/index) for each object the migration creates, compared against the hash rows present. Fix: apply the committed migration SQL by hand via psql; never hand-edit applied migration files |

Helper: `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/romanizeTranscript.ts` using `@indic-transliteration/sanscript` (script detected from the text's Unicode block, never the language code; Tamil MUST use the `tamil_extended` scheme — plain `tamil` emits garbage); IAST → ASCII strip → garbage guard (>15% unmapped glyphs → "") → card-style schwa deletion for hi/gu/mr/pa/bn/as/mai/doi/kok/brx (script-based fallback for Gujarati/Gurmukhi/Bengali scripts when no languageCode, e.g. client-target requests without phraseId). Coverage: 17 of 22 languages; ks/sd/ur (Perso-Arabic), sat (Ol Chiki), mni (Meetei Mayek) return "" and clients hide the line. All 5 eval `res.json` sites carry the field (nocatch/empty paths send ""); optional in openapi.yaml (mobile back-compat), never stored on attempts or in the evaluation token. Both result cards render it under the raw "We heard" line and hide it when empty OR when it equals the transcript case-insensitively (Latin passthrough would just repeat) |

Sandbox evidence + options analysis delivered in chat and in agent memory (pronunciation-scoring-signal.md): STT logprobs are a dead end; gpt-audio grading via the AI proxy separates quality but needs a 3-run median; Azure PA covers only hi/ta of 22; IndicMFA is the only full-coverage phoneme-level path. The grouping-and-gating ruling below still stands until the last two land. This is why the Build 35 hub redesign is web-only: with five games, the three-group shelves would render 1/2/2 (headers over one card read as a defect) and the five-gated/five-free split would gate ALL five mobile games, leaving a free user zero playable games. Owner ruling: mobile hub stays flat at 3 gated / 2 free until the missing five ship there, then the grouping and gating split port over |
| Luggage Match copy drifts three ways, and one line is false (Aug 5, 2026) | The roster (`lib/quick-games.ts`, shared) says "Pair each bag with its owner before the train leaves."; the web hub hardcodes "Pair native-script luggage tags with their English twins"; the in-game instruction is a third string. Worse, the roster blurb promises a timer — "before the train leaves" — for a game that is UNTIMED on both platforms, so the hub tile sets an expectation the game does not meet. Owner ruled the mobile port register the roster description as-is and bank the fix rather than rewrite roster copy mid-port. A fix should settle one description per game and drop the false urgency |

Build 35 required every icon to animate; owner ruled the five keep static icons rather than have art invented for them, and banked it as an art-pipeline item. Adding a vignette is additive: register a component under the game id and the hub animates it with a staggered phase automatically |
| Hub hero must stay a free game (Build 35, Aug 5, 2026) | `FEATURED_SLOT` points at the Vocabulary group so the hero resolves to Luggage Match (free, Beginner). This is a deliberate merchandising constraint, not an accident of ordering: all five vignette-bearing games are gated, so a locked hero over a hub whose every animated card is locked reads as a paywall menu. Re-pointing the slot at a group whose first card is gated will reintroduce that, and no test currently fails on it beyond the explicit non-gated hero assertion in `games-hub.test.tsx`. Reordering a group so a gated game lands first has the same effect |

## 9. Working rules for the agent

Paste this block into every spec.

1. Do not run test suites automatically after each edit. Make the full set of changes, run once, report all failures together, fix in one pass.
2. Verify every edit by reading the file back. Edits have silently failed to persist in this codebase.
3. Do not batch string injection across files. Read, edit, verify, move on. Whitespace differences cause silent misses.
4. Treat a failing test as a question, not an answer. Check whether the test or the code is right before changing either. Do not change a test to match the code without confirming the code matches the spec.
5. Never hand-edit generated or dist files. Change `openapi.yaml` and regenerate.
6. New API response fields are optional, always, for mobile back-compat.
7. Never decode `evaluationToken` on the client.
8. Never introduce a second date-bucketing or reduced-motion implementation.
9. Zero output from typecheck means zero errors. Do not re-run to confirm.
10. Mobile count-up chips use the ReText pattern (animated `TextInput`, `editable={false}`). Tests must assert via `accessibilityLabel` or `getByLabelText`, never `getByText`.
11. If a named file, component, or line does not match this document, STOP and ask. Then report the discrepancy so this document can be corrected.
12. Before submitting any schema-bearing publish, read the generated-migrations panel and confirm the pending delta is exactly what you expect. Migration-stage publish failures produce NO deployment logs, so the panel is the only pre-flight visibility.
13. The lesson-group scope trigger DDL lives in TWO places: migration 0030 and the api-server startup guard (which reads the migration file as its single source of truth). Never edit the trigger functions in one place only, and never reorder the boot sequence so the guard runs after the lesson-group backfill.
14. **Test mock pattern (mandatory).** Both platforms have a shared helper that provides idle-safe defaults for every `@workspace/api-client-react` hook. Web: `artifacts/gujarati-coach/src/test-helpers/api-client-mock.ts` (`apiClientMockDefaults`). Mobile: `artifacts/bolo-mobile/test-helpers/api-client-mock.ts` (`apiClientMockDefaults`). All test files MUST spread the helper and override only what they exercise. Adding a new hook to a screen requires only touching the helper -- no test file changes. The helper is typed via `satisfies` against the generated client so a missing entry is a typecheck error, not a silent runtime `undefined`. Never add a full-replacement mock factory without spreading `apiClientMockDefaults` first.
15. Untracking files that remain on disk causes the NEXT platform rebase to fail with DIRTY_WORKTREE, because the replayed earlier commit still tries to add those exact paths. Workaround: move the files outside the repo for the duration of the rebase, then move them back. Better: add the `.gitignore` entry BEFORE producing the files, which avoids the situation entirely.
16. Work reported as committed has three times failed to survive a platform rebase in a single session: a working rule written to this file, a `.gitignore` entry, and working rule 14 itself. After any rebase, VERIFY on disk that the change is actually present rather than trusting the prior report. grep the file; do not assume.
17. `markTaskComplete` commits the ENTIRE working tree, exactly like publish does — there is no staging step you control and no per-file opt-out at completion time. Any `.gitignore` entry must therefore exist BEFORE the work that produces ignorable files, not after. Establishing it afterwards is too late: on August 12, 2026 a Gujarati audit dry run downloaded 1.06 MB of production mp3s to `qa/` for listening, and completing the task swept every blob into history; the only forward-only remedy is `git rm -r --cached` plus the ignore line in a follow-up commit, which leaves the bytes in the earlier commit forever. Before generating scratch downloads, recordings, screenshots, or any other regenerable blob, add its directory to `.gitignore` in the same breath as creating it. This is the same ruling that already keeps `attached_assets/` and `.agents/` out of publish commits.

18. **Deliver the artifact first; the owner judges it by eye and by ear (owner ruling, August 12, 2026, stated for audio and extended to art the same day; the user-preferences section of `replit.md` carries both forms).** For anything the owner will perceive directly, meaning generated audio, mascot art, garment renders, video, or layout, produce the thing and SHOW it. Do not audit your own output against a second method to prove it is clean, and do not substitute your own written assessment for the artifact: no coverage/hem/shoulder grading, no "which reads better" commentary, no naive-versus-good comparison methodology. Measurement is the exception, not the default, and is warranted for ONE class of defect only: a flaw invisible at preview scale that becomes visible after compositing or export. Report those as a single number with no methodology (for the garment pipeline, the count of surviving magenta-ish pixels after keying, since a 1-2px fringe is invisible in the flat but composites as a coloured halo on Bolo's teal). If an artifact is too large to preview, say so and supply a resized version; never analyse it in place of showing it. **A challenged number must be re-measured by a WIDER test, never by re-running the same one:** when the owner disputed a zero fringe count, the answer came from widening the distance threshold and splitting the hits by whether they touch transparency, which separated the garment's own crimson piping (interior, blue ~96) from a backdrop blend (edge-adjacent, blue near 255).

### openai.ts merge-scramble recovery (July 31, 2026)

**What happened:** Task #952 (pilot capture: tee pronunciation audio to R2) auto-merged onto Task #948 (progression completion: Polish card + zone capstone chat). Both tasks modified `openai.ts` in overlapping regions. Git's 3-way merge garbled ~400 lines, mixing greeting-TTS context with phrase-TTS context. The merged file was syntactically valid TypeScript and typechecked clean (compatible types), so no gate caught it. Root cause of the gate gap: #952's task-agent typecheck ran against its isolated pre-merge branch; no post-merge boot or typecheck ran on the combined result.

**Recovery:** Restored from HEAD~1 (c21397a, Task #948 baseline) as the clean base, then applied exactly three legitimate #952 deltas: `import { teeAudioToPilot }`, first `teeAudioToPilot` call (fast-path STT branch), second call (LLM evaluation branch), plus `rawBuffer` hoist above the try block so both call sites can reach it.

**`buildUserPrompt` fix (parrotChat.ts):** Function had three spec gaps vs `docs/specs/progression-completion.md §4`: scenario block was placed before `Language:` (should be after), `History:` label was omitted, scenario title was omitted from the scenario block. All three fixed.

**Boot smoke test added:** `artifacts/api-server/src/test/server-boot.test.ts` — two tests: app module import + openai route module import. Any parse-level corruption in any route file surfaces here before the server is manually started.

**`@workspace/db` mock completeness rule:** `openai.pronunciation.language-hint.test.ts` mocks `@workspace/db` via `mock.module`. Its `namedExports` list must include every table that `openai.ts`'s import line references. When `zoneConversationStampsTable` was added to `openai.ts` (Task #948) the mock was not updated, causing 9 test failures. Fix: add the missing entry to the mock's namedExports. **Do not add workarounds to production files to accommodate stale test mocks.** Known mock gaps (tables in schema, absent from mock): `dailyQuizCompletionsTable`, `dailyQuizzesTable`, `lessonGroupProgressTable`, `lessonGroupTestoutsTable`, `phraseReportsTable`, `scriptTraceProgressTable`, `userItemMemoryTable`. Phantom entry (in mock, not in schema): `phraseScheduleTable`. Task #922's release slot is the correct home for a shared mock factory.
