### Tear hand-off overlay + gust exit (Task #905, July 31, 2026)

**Problem:** the boarding-pass tear's final beat (stub opacity fade, last ~100ms of `--tear-duration` 600ms) was cut when navigation at `--tear-nav-delay` (500ms) unmounted home; the body half simply vanished mid-recoil at the swap.

**Mechanism (web only, home.tsx + index.css):** in the SAME beat as `navigate("/journey")` inside the nav timer, `spawnTearHandoffOverlay(body, stub)` clones BOTH mid-tear halves into a `position:fixed` container appended to `document.body` (`[data-tear-overlay]`, `z-60`, `pointer-events:none`, `aria-hidden`; deliberately not React-owned — a portal would unmount with home). Each clone resumes its tear keyframes via inline `animation-delay: calc(-1 * var(--tear-nav-delay))` (inline outranks the class shorthand's implicit 0s), inside a `.animate-gust-away` wrapper that sweeps the piece off over the incoming journey route — stub first, body `--gust-stagger` later, each along its own tear trajectory via per-half inline `--gust-x`/`--gust-r` = ± the `:root` constants. No morph toward the journey header ticket. New `:root` tuning constants: `--gust-drift` 120px, `--gust-drop` 22px, `--gust-rotate` 9deg, `--gust-duration` 420ms, `--gust-stagger` 120ms, `--tear-overlay-cleanup` 900ms (measured from the hand-off; JS fallback constant 900ms).

**Cleanup:** removal fires on the FINAL wrapper's (`[data-gust-final]`, the body half) `animationend` filtered by `animationName === "gust-away"` — the clones' own tear-keyframe ends bubble through the same listener and must not remove the overlay early — plus an idempotent `--tear-overlay-cleanup` timeout fallback (backgrounded tabs/jsdom never fire animation events).

**Placement correctness:** clones are placed by layout-true offsets (`offsetParent` rect + `offsetLeft/Top`, sizes from `offsetWidth/Height`) so the current tear transform never skews the box; the ticket-breathe wrapper class is additionally gated on `!tearing` so no ancestor scale skews measurement at the hand-off. jsdom degrades to 0-rects harmlessly (no `offsetParent` there).

**Invariants preserved:** navigation timing unchanged (`--tear-nav-delay` still 500ms) and never blocked — overlay spawn sits in its own try/catch inside the timer with `navigate()` after it unconditionally; activation-path failure (`getComputedStyle` on documentElement throwing) still navigates immediately and now provably creates zero overlay nodes (the spawn reads `--tear-overlay-cleanup` BEFORE any DOM write); reduced motion = instant native Link nav, no tear, no gust; transforms/opacity only.

**Tests:** `home-boarding-pass.test.tsx` 8 frozen pins untouched + new describe "tear hand-off overlay (task 905)" (4 pins: spawn-at-navigation-only + final-gust-animationend removal with bubbling-tear-end immunity; timeout-fallback removal (real timers — jsdom's lack of animation events IS the fallback path); reduced-motion no-overlay; failure-path no-orphan). **Test hygiene trap:** any test that lets the tear reach navigation now spawns the overlay, whose jsdom removal is time-based (~900ms real timer) and whose body clone duplicates the pass CTA text — a file-level `afterEach` purges `[data-tear-overlay]` or later tests' `getByText` finds multiple matches. `qa/task899-tear-probe.mjs` MODE=after extended: overlay presence over /journey right after the slowed nav, gone after animation end, and gone ≤1.2s post-nav at stock timing (real-browser run itself is Task #960's job).

**Suite state after this work:** web 49 files / 340 tests all pass (336 baseline + 4 new). Typecheck clean; boot smokes 2/2 (standalone run). api canonical run: exit 1 with the pilot-capture "unlisted userId" env-induced fence PLUS one intermittent failure in `feedbackTts.test.ts` ("b64_FROM_PREWARM==" assertion) that passes 6/6 standalone — full-suite interference (prewarm cache/TTS-provider adjacent, see Task #953's scope), not caused by this web-only change.


### Practice prev/next navigation + journey rail comet (Task #973, July 31, 2026)

**Item 3, practice manual navigation (web only, practice.tsx):** learners can move to the previous or next phrase manually via two visually secondary chevron buttons absolutely positioned at the left/right edges of the parrot zone (`button-prev-phrase` / `button-next-phrase`, accessible names "Go to previous phrase" / "Go to next phrase"; zero layout shift for the record button and waveform). Navigation is FREE, never attempt-gated (product decision). `goToPhrase(target)` guards bounds and `recording`/`evaluating`, clears `result`/`evalError`/confetti/`ownRecording` plus `feedbackAudioPendingRef`, sets the index and ALWAYS lands in `idle` (never `playing_coach`; the playing_coach effect's cleanup pauses any in-flight coach audio on the state flip, so no playback, recording, or scoring side effects). Prev disabled on the first phrase, next on the last; both disabled while recording/evaluating (disabled chosen over hidden). Milestone toasts remain exclusive to auto-advance (`handleNext`), which is unchanged. Keyboard ArrowLeft/ArrowRight mirror the buttons (window listener; skipped for modifier keys, INPUT/TEXTAREA/contentEditable targets, and test-out). Test-out mode renders no controls (one take per phrase, forward only). Attempts and bestScore survive any visit order because `sessionResults` is keyed by phrase id.

**Accessible-name trap:** the nav buttons deliberately are NOT named "Next phrase"/"Previous phrase" because the result CTA is "Next phrase" and several suites query `/^Next( phrase)?$/`; `practice-speech-capability.test.tsx`'s compare-stage assertion was anchored from `/Next/i` for the same reason. Any future control whose accessible name contains "next" will trip the unanchored `/next/i` queries in `practice-testout.test.tsx` if it renders in testout mode.

**Item 4, journey rail comet (web only, journey.tsx + index.css + lib/motion.tsx):** the active-run pulse is now a single clearly visible traveling comet instead of many faint dots. Geometry constants moved to the shared named export `RAIL_PULSE` in `src/lib/motion.tsx` (`dotsPerSegment: 10`, `dotRadius: 4`; the old hardcoded `PULSE_DOTS_PER_SEG = 8` with `r=3` was the defect); composing work (e.g. Task #972 journey open sweep) and tests import it. Timing/opacity stay in the `:root` tuning block in index.css: `--rail-pulse-peak` raised 0.85 to 1, new `--rail-pulse-glow: 5px` feeds a `drop-shadow(0 0 var(--rail-pulse-glow) currentColor)` on `.rail-pulse-dot` (the circle's inline `color: line.accent` supplies currentColor), and the keyframes became sharp attack / slow decay (0% min, 4% peak, 22%-100% min; stop percentages are the comet shape and cannot be custom properties). Delay stagger unchanged, so the bright head plus fading tail travels toward the next stop on the same 3.4s cycle. Reduced motion unchanged: no dots rendered at all.

**Mobile parity loci (NOT inherited, follow-ups only):** bolo-mobile has its own journey pulse implementation in `artifacts/bolo-mobile/app/(app)/journey.tsx` (untouched; still the old treatment) and its own practice screen `artifacts/bolo-mobile/app/(app)/practice/[id].tsx` (no manual prev/next).

**Tests:** new `src/test/practice-phrase-navigation.test.tsx` (5 tests: edge disabling, idle-no-playback landing with audio on, attempt preservation across visit order + result-card clearing, keyboard arrows, testout hides controls; spreads `baseApiClientMock()`); `journey-rail-pulse.test.tsx` now imports `RAIL_PULSE` (density can never drift) plus a comet pin (radius from `RAIL_PULSE`, inline accent color present).

**Suite state after this work:** web 50 files / 346 tests all pass (340 pre-task baseline, which was 336 documented + 4 from the merged #954 station-resume tests, + 6 new). Typecheck clean. api-server untouched: canonical run 680 tests, 679 pass, 1 fail (the documented pilot-capture "unlisted userId" env fence). Items 1-2 of the four-item batch brief verified already shipped on main via #919 (home-badge-card tests green; desktop upsell polish remains #923).
### Completed-station resume ruling + plan-visibility mechanism (August 1, 2026, web only)

**Ruling (binding for mobile parity #966/#976 too):** the first-unmastered scan (`bestScore == null || bestScore < 80`) runs UNCONDITIONALLY for a station session; completion/tested-out status never short-circuits it; index 0 is the fallback ONLY when the scan finds nothing or no per-phrase data exists (a tested-out station's all-null rows make the scan itself land on 0). Entry route must not affect the result.

**Mechanism of the reported bug (diagnosed empirically, real-browser repro):** there was NO status short-circuit anywhere — the #954 derivation was already correct against its payload. The wrong-feeling start is a plan-visibility mismatch: the journey listing's `masteredCount`/`phraseCount` count ALL group rows including `premium` ones, while `GET /lesson-groups/:id/phrases` filters premium rows for Free callers. A Free learner who masters a station's free rows sees "Completed | 8/10 mastered" on the card, but their session payload holds only the 8 free rows — all mastered — so the scan correctly finds nothing and the review-visit fallback (phrase 1) fires. Deterministic for every Free user on any station carrying premium rows (the 80% completion ratio latches at exactly the free-row count). Ruled fix locus: loader derivation only — the card tally mismatch was reported and explicitly left unchanged.

**Change (practice.tsx auto-start, group branch):** restructured to express the ruling — the scan is teaser-gated only (teaser inertness unchanged), and its hit is applied whenever `firstBelowCredit >= 0` (previously `> 0`; behaviorally identical since index 0 equals the fallback, but the shape now pins "scan wins; 0 only on miss"). Test-out/polish/phraseIds/`?phrase=` branches untouched.

**Tests:** `practice-start-position.test.tsx` gained a second describe (3 pins): T,T,F,T,F starts at index 2 (regression pin for the dev report), unmastered-at-0 starts at 0 (scan/fallback agreement), all-null tested-out starts at phrase 1. All run on the journey revisit path (`/practice/:zone?group=<id>` — the only route into a station session, so entry-route parity is pinned by the URL shape itself). Existing #954 (6) and #973 suites pass unmodified.

**Mic-grant audit (same brief, Item 2, NO code change):** practice.tsx does NOT share chat's fixed grant-after-release hole. `startRecording()` has exactly one call site, synchronously after `beginHold(e.pointerId)` inside `handleBellyPointerDown` (the sole `onPointerDown`); the only keyboard listener is #973's arrow navigation. Positive hold-confirmation converts a hold that died during a slow grant into finish-and-send (practice's release semantic), never a stuck-open mic. The null-hold skip asymmetry vs chat is dead code given the single synchronous call site, not a hole.

**Suite state after this work:** web 50 files / 351 tests all pass (348 baseline + 3 new). Typecheck clean; boot smokes 2/2 (standalone). api canonical run: 682 tests, 681 pass, 1 fail — the documented pilot-capture "unlisted userId" env fence only.

### Games hub colorful 2-column redesign (Task #967, July 31, 2026, web only)

**What changed (`src/pages/games/index.tsx` only):** the hub grid is now `grid gap-3 min-[480px]:grid-cols-2` (2 columns from 480px, 1 column only below — previously `sm:grid-cols-2`/640px). Each game has a distinct color identity from a static `GAME_COLORS` map keyed by game id (`{ bg, border, iconBg, iconColor, lockedBg, lockedIconColor }` Tailwind class strings; sky=word-match, emerald=listen-and-pick, amber=phrase-builder, rose=speed-round, violet=bolo-quiz, plus a neutral `FALLBACK_COLOR` for unmapped ids). Locked (Plus) cards keep their hue washed out (`opacity-80 saturate-[0.85]` on the card + per-game `lockedBg`/`lockedIconColor` on the icon bubble) instead of the old gray `bg-muted`/`text-muted-foreground` overrides.

**Motion:** each card is wrapped (inside its wouter `Link`) in a `motion.div` with a staggered entrance (`opacity/y/scale` in with `springs.bouncy`, `delay: index * 0.06`) plus `whileHover={{ y: -2 }}` and `whileTap={{ scale: 0.96 }}`. Gesture transitions are declared ON the gesture targets (`transition: springs.snappy` inside `whileHover`/`whileTap`) — otherwise framer reuses the entrance `transition` including its stagger delay. All three collapse under `useReducedMotion()` (instant fade, no hover/tap transforms). **`card-lift` was intentionally REMOVED from the card:** its CSS hover-translate/active-scale would compose with the wrapper's framer transforms (double press ≈0.94 scale); the card keeps only non-transform hover affordances (`transition-[box-shadow,border-color,background-color] hover:shadow-md` + per-hue hover border). Transform feedback for these cards lives in framer only.

**Unchanged:** GAMES data, fail-closed `plusReady` gating, locked→`/upgrade` routing, preview vignettes + negative `--gv-delay` stagger, pills/lock/difficulty badges, `games-hub.test.tsx` (passes unmodified).

**Verification:** `qa/task967-shots.mjs` (same sign-in-token harness) checks at 320/480/640/1280px: titles unclipped, column count (1 at 320, 2 at ≥480), five distinct card bg colors, no horizontal overflow, and reduced-motion (cards opacity 1, zero running animations). All pass; shots in `qa/shots/task967/`. Web typecheck clean; web suite baseline at this HEAD: 3 files / 4 tests failing (practice-start-position, journey-rail-pulse ×2, practice-barge-in ×2) — stash-verified pre-existing, untouched by this change; games-hub 4/4 green. NOTE (pre-existing, not this task): `api-server` typecheck/build is broken at this HEAD by a committed syntax error in `src/routes/openai.ts` (~line 561, `Expected ")" but found "else"`, from the "Refactor OpenAI route handler and chat logic" commit) — the dev API workflow cannot start until whoever owns that refactor fixes it.


### Recorded tear SFX replaces synthesis (Task #984, August 1, 2026, web only)

**What changed (`src/lib/tearAudio.ts` internals only):** the Web Audio noise-synthesis burst (white noise + exp decay + 2.2kHz bandpass, read as a cymbal, not paper) is DELETED. `playTearSfx()` now plays the recorded clip `public/sounds/tear-sfx.m4a` (0.985s, mono, 44.1kHz AAC-LC, loudness-normalized, 13KB; copied from the attached asset) via `AudioBufferSourceNode` → `GainNode` at the named constant **`TEAR_SFX_GAIN = 0.4`**. Exported interface (`preloadTearAudio`, `playTearSfx`), home.tsx wiring, silent-failure contract, fire-and-forget guarantee, tear timing, haptics, and navigation are all byte-unchanged in behavior.

**Preload path:** `preloadTearAudio()` keeps the suspended-context pre-warm AND now fetches + `decodeAudioData`s the clip into a module-cached `AudioBuffer` at home mount (`${BASE_URL}sounds/tear-sfx.m4a`, same URL convention as sound.ts/band-audio.ts). A `loadStarted` latch dedups repeated mounts; a failed fetch/decode resets the latch so a later mount retries. **If the buffer is not ready (preload failed/never ran/tap raced the decode), `playTearSfx()` stays SILENT** — never a fetch-then-play that would lag the tap. Warm-context reuse and fresh-context self-close (`source.onended → ctx.close()`, only when the warm ctx was closed out from under us) preserved.

**Gain rationale (measured, not guessed):** asset peak −2.2 dBFS / RMS −21.0 dBFS vs the old synthesis at peak −16.5 / RMS −33.3 (reproduced offline with the same RBJ bandpass). Brief's starting 0.55 would land ~7–9 dB hotter than the old effect; 0.4 (−8 dB) → peak ≈ −10 dBFS, RMS ≈ −29 dBFS, ~4 dB more present than the old too-subtle burst without being startling.

**Tests/verification:** the 2 SFX pins in `home-boarding-pass.test.tsx` pass unchanged (module mocked there); no module-level jsdom test added (jsdom has no `decodeAudioData` — mock-heavy, skipped per brief). New real-browser probe `qa/task984-tear-sfx-probe.mjs` (sign-in-token harness): asserts exactly one fetch+decode at mount (decode 0.985s/mono/44.1k), tap starts a buffer source with the decoded clip at gain 0.4 with ZERO additional fetch, tear classes land, navigation reaches /journey. PASS.

**Suite state after this work:** web 50 files / 348 tests all pass. Typecheck clean (all packages — the openai.ts syntax error noted under #967 is fixed on this HEAD). Boot smokes 2/2 (standalone run). api canonical run: 682 tests, 681 pass / 1 fail — solely the documented pilot-capture "unlisted userId" env fence (`PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS` set workspace-wide), byte-identical by name; baseline unchanged. NOTE (pre-existing log noise, not a failure): several openai voice-preference tests print `TypeError: Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'warn')` from `openai.ts` (~line 401 `req.log.warn` in the TTS cache-read catch) — the test harness's stub req has no `.log`; tests still pass.

### Journey map India scenery + 2.5D depth pass (Task #985, August 1, 2026, web only)

**Depth-order tokens (`DEPTH_2_5D.layers` in `src/lib/motion.tsx`) — the shared 2.5D language for later surfaces (games hub, home):** scenery(0) < rail(1) < stationCard(4) < station(6) < train(7) < postcard(8) < overlay(50). Scenery and rail are SVG groups inside the map svg (paint order; the svg underlies all HTML overlays); the rest are the z-indexes of the absolutely positioned HTML layers in journey.tsx. The current-stop marker wrapper takes `train`, other markers `station`; postcards and the interchange diamond take `postcard`; dialogs are z-50 (`overlay`).

**One light source:** upper-left, so all shadows offset down-right. CSS side: `--depth-shadow-x/-y/-blur/-color` + composed `--depth-shadow` in the `:root` tuning block (index.css) with `.depth-shadow` (box shadow) and `.ground-contact-shadow` (floor ellipse under characters; used under the journey Mascot — the canonical PNG itself is untouched) utilities. SVG side: `SCENERY_GROUND_SHADOW` (ellipse dx +2, opacity 0.13) in journey-scenery.tsx. All marker/diamond/terminus inline ring boxShadows append `var(--depth-shadow)`; postcards and the active card use `.depth-shadow` (replacing the old inconsistent `shadow-sm`).

**Rail-bed thickness:** RailSegment draws the 15px sleeper-tie band once more in ink (#0f172a) at `DEPTH_2_5D.railBedOpacity` (0.18), translated down `railBedDy` (2.5px), BEFORE the colored strokes. Same `d` and dash rhythm — the bezier geometry the comet samples is byte-identical.

**Scenery set (`src/components/journey-scenery.tsx`):** 9 dimensional flat assets — tuktuk, cow (standing zebu), fruitCart, chaiStall (unmanned, per token-economy spec section 4.5; the Chaiwala character task adds the figure later), temple, banyan, marigolds (toran), cycleRickshaw, ghat (steps + boat). Style contract: 3/4 view, two visible faces, exactly ONE darker palette step per asset for the shaded right face, shared ground ellipse, anchored at ground y=0 drawing upward, max height `SCENERY_MAX_H` (40), half-widths in `SCENERY_HALF_W`. `TracksideDoodad` (generic tree/signal/hut set) is DELETED; `ZoneVista` and `Bunting` unchanged.

**Placement (deterministic):** `ZONE_SCENERY_THEMES` by zone index — 0-1 urban (tuktuk/chaiStall/banyan, cycleRickshaw/tuktuk/chaiStall), 2-3 market (fruitCart/cow/marigolds, cow/fruitCart/cycleRickshaw), 4-5 river/temple (temple/banyan/marigolds, ghat/temple/marigolds). `planZoneScenery(zoneIndex, stationCount)` → 1-3 elements (floor(n/3) capped), spread across in-zone rows at `floor((i+0.5)*n/count)`. Placement anchors: `SCENERY_PLACEMENT` (edgeX 42, groundDy 22) + station point side (free strip opposite the card), inside the SVG scenery group. Geometry constants exported as `SERPENTINE` from journey.tsx for the no-overlap tests.

**Parallax:** ONE scroll-linked `translate(0, scrollY * DEPTH_2_5D.parallaxFactor)` (0.03) set as a `transform` attribute on the scenery group via a passive window-scroll listener + rAF (window scroll is the map's scroll mechanism; sticky header, no overflow container). Entirely absent under reduced motion (attribute removed). The ref is read lazily inside the handlers because the map mounts after the loading state.

**Mobile parity spec inputs (#966-adjacent follow-up):** reuse the theme table, planZoneScenery, SCENERY_PLACEMENT, the layer tokens, and the one-shade-step asset contract; RN side needs its own SVG components and a scroll-position-driven transform on the scenery group.

**Deferred idle micro-motion candidates (explicitly out of scope v1):** chai-stall steam wisp above the kettle, cow ear/tail flick, marigold/bunting sway, ghat water ripple shimmer.

**Performance (real-browser probe `qa/task985-depth-probe.mjs`, shots in `qa/shots/task985/`):** before → after: document elements 2171 → 2074, map svg elements 673 → 574 (59 per-station doodads replaced by 17 zone-planned scenes outweigh the +66 rail-bed paths), scroll frame p50/p95 16.7/16.8ms unchanged, zero console errors. Layout/recalc counts vary run-to-run with scroll sampling; durations comparable (layout 24-41ms, style 71-98ms).

**Tests:** new `src/test/journey-scenery.test.tsx` (13 tests: theme progression + determinism, 1-3 spread rows, rendered per-zone kinds, no-overlap geometry vs rail band/markers/cards/row bounds at 320/360/390, reduced-motion static + no transform + comet absent, scroll applies the shared-factor translate, token ordering, scenery-below-rail structural order, HTML z-index tokens present).

**Suite state after this work:** web 51 files / 361 tests all pass (348 baseline + 13 new). api canonical 682 tests, 681 pass / 1 fail (the documented pilot-capture env fence, byte-identical by name); both boot smokes pass inside it. Typecheck clean.

### Games hub motion punch-up (Task #986, August 1, 2026, web only)

**Premise correction found in Step 0:** the brief assumed the GamePreview vignettes were not on the hub cards; they were already the icon-tile content (since #967; the lucide icon is only a defensive fallback). The real change is the ENERGY MODEL, not surfacing.

**Energy model (game-previews.css + game-previews.tsx):** every vignette duration is now `calc(<authored>s * var(--gv-tempo, 1))`. `.gv` defaults `--gv-tempo: 2.2` (slow ambient idle); `.group:hover .gv` / `.group:active .gv` restore 1 (full authored energy). Switching tempo mid-cycle jumps the loop phase — deliberate, reads as waking up. Locked cards pass `locked` into GamePreview → `gv--locked` = `animation-play-state: paused` (static mid-cycle frame at the card's negative `--gv-delay` offset), running only under `.group:hover` — look-but-locked (this REVERSES the #967 decision that locked cards idle-loop "to sell the game"; brief override). Off-screen: an IntersectionObserver per vignette (rootMargin 64px, guarded for jsdom) toggles `gv--offscreen` (paused). Reduced motion needs no JS: the global index.css 0.001ms `!important` rule beats the tempo var, so vignettes sit on their authored static base frames.

**Entrance (index.tsx):** cascade upgraded to `{opacity 0, y 28, scale 0.9}` → `springs.poppy` (preset already existed in lib/motion.tsx — no new preset added) with `delay: index * 0.07`; 5 cards settle in ~0.65s (under the 700ms budget). Header mascot: one whole-image bounce (`y [0,-9,0]`, `rotate [0,-7,4,0]`, 0.55s, delay 0.08) via a motion.div wrapper, once per mount, never looping — canonical mascot rule respected. Reduced motion: instant fade, still mascot.

**Press/hover theater:** whileHover `{y: -4, scale: 1.02}`; whileTap squash `scale 0.93` (snappy release springs back). Press bloom = per-game border glow: new `pressGlow` field on GameCOLORS entries (`motion-safe:active:shadow-[0_0_14px_2px_rgba(...)0.45]` in each game's hue; radial-from-press-point rejected as not cheap). Unlocked cards get `motion-safe:hover:saturate-[1.15]`; card transition list gained `filter`. Step-in on navigate: `enteredId` state flips the wrapper's animate target to `scale 1.05` (snappy) in the same click that lets Link navigate — navigation is NEVER delayed or intercepted; locked (upgrade-routed) cards and reduced motion skip it. Ambient restraint: nothing beyond the vignettes idles; pills/lock/difficulty static.

**CPU (qa/task986-cpu-probe.mjs, 8s idle @480px, Free user):** style recalc 358.6ms → 243.0ms (-32%), running grid animations 17 → 7 (three locked cards paused), script 79.5 → 91.7ms (observer overhead, noise-level), layout 0 → 0, frame p50 16.7ms unchanged. Behavior probe `qa/task986-behavior-probe.mjs`: idle 10.56s computed duration, hover wake to authored 4.8s/5.2s, locked paused-until-hover, offscreen pause + resume — all PASS. `qa/task967-shots.mjs` regression: all width/RM checks PASS unmodified.

**Tests:** new `src/test/games-hub-motion.test.tsx` (6 pins: vignette-per-card, locked mapping free user, no gv--locked for Plus, IO offscreen add/remove via stubbed IntersectionObserver, reduced-motion identical DOM, step-in click navigates same-tick via memoryLocation record). `games-hub.test.tsx` passes UNMODIFIED. Testing note: framer's `useReducedMotion` is mocked via partial module mock (journey-scenery pattern).

**Suite state after this work:** web 52 files / 370 tests all pass (364 certified combined baseline + 6 new). Typecheck clean; boot smokes 2/2; api canonical 682/681/1 (documented pilot-capture env fence only).

### Brief A: full web parity on mobile (August 1, 2026, bolo-mobile only)

**Scope:** eight parity items on `artifacts/bolo-mobile`; web and api untouched (verified by git diff). Exclusions per brief: mobile tear SFX, Android. Device verification deferred to Brief B (Task #991).

**Item 1, practice chevrons (#976/#973 parity):** free prev/next Pressables absolutely positioned at the mascot-row edges in `app/(app)/practice/[id].tsx` (testIDs `button-prev-phrase`/`button-next-phrase`, labels "Go to previous/next phrase" distinct from the post-attempt "Next phrase" CTA). Hidden in test-out; disabled at ends and during recording/evaluating. `goToPhrase` sets `suppressAutoPlayRef` so manual browsing fires no coach playback; the auto-play effect consumes-and-clears the flag. Milestone toasts stay exclusive to auto-advance.

**Item 2, rail comet + 2.5D depth (journey.tsx):** the dash-march `RailPulse` is replaced by the web comet: 10 dots per segment sampled on the exact rail beziers (control points at half the vertical gap), one `useLoopProgress(3400)` clock per Svg slice, per-dot opacity keyframes 0%/4%/22% with a soft halo circle standing in for the CSS drop-shadow glow. Rail-bed underside: the 15px tie band duplicated in ink `#0f172a` at opacity 0.18, translated down 2.5px, drawn before the colored strokes. Scenery parallax: `Animated.ScrollView` + `useAnimatedScrollHandler` drive ONE `translateY: scrollY * 0.03` on the scenery wrapper (translateY only; position/top in useAnimatedStyle is the Expo Go crash trap), pinned to 0 under reduced motion.

**Item 3, games hub rewrite:** `app/(app)/(tabs)/games/index.tsx` is now a 2-column FlatList with per-game hue tints (web pressGlow rgbas), entrance cascade (70ms stagger, poppy spring), press squash 0.93 + hue glow, one-shot mascot bounce (Mascot default `motion='float'` verified safe and reduced-motion aware), viewability-driven vignette pause, fully static under reduced motion. NEW `components/games/GamePreview.tsx`: reanimated port of the web `game-previews.css` vignettes, one sawtooth clock per vignette, phase offset `(index*1100)%cycle`, testID `game-preview-<id>`. Documented simplification: linear segment interpolation at the CSS keyframe percentages instead of authored easing curves. Push targets and fail-closed Plus gating preserved; `games-hub-gate.test.tsx` passes unmodified.

**Item 4, station resume (Task 966 parity):** `?skipMastered` removed from the practice screen; every group run scans unconditionally to the first phrase with `bestScore == null || bestScore < 80`, is teaser-inert, and only moves when the target index > 0. Leftover link sites still passing the now-ignored param (report-only, ticketed as Task #992): `(tabs)/index.tsx:230`, `category/[id].tsx:308,333`, `practice/daily.tsx:27`.

**Item 5, mic audit (report-only, no hole found):** in practice, `recorder.record()` has exactly one call site inside `startRecording`, which is only invoked from RecordButton onPressIn behind `phase === 'idle'`; onPressOut clears+stops, `finishingRef` dedups, barge-in blocks only during evaluating. Chat and review own separate recorders (out of scope).

**Item 6, splash:** NEW `assets/images/splash.png`, canonical mascot-wave composited at 48% frame height on cream `#fffdf0` 1024x1024; `app.json` splash.image repointed (icon untouched). Content-height fraction: 0.778 before (icon.png as splash) to 0.479 after.

**Item 7, trackside scenery (Task 985 port):** `components/journey/Scenery.tsx` ports the 9-asset dimensional set (tuktuk, cow, fruitCart, chaiStallTrackside, temple, banyan, marigolds, cycleRickshaw, ghat), `SCENERY_COLORS` + luminance-matched `SCENERY_GRAYS` (RN has no CSS grayscale, so gray = palette swap), `GroundShadow`, `ZONE_SCENERY_THEMES`, `planZoneScenery`, `SCENERY_PLACEMENT` (edgeX 42, groundDy 22). `TracksideDoodad` deleted. journey.tsx renders the planned scenery in per-slice Svgs inside the parallax layer, below the rail; locked showroom zones gray out.

**Item 8, recent-plays band pill (#978 parity):** home badge is now a pill showing `BAND_LABEL[normalizeBand(a.band, Number(a.score))]`, never the raw score. allTopBand robustness ruling: `lib/ui.ts normalizeBand` is already defensive for legacy/absent bands, so no code change was needed there.

**Tests:** mobile suite 69 suites / 437 tests green (baseline 68/422). New `games-hub-vignettes.test.tsx`; extended `practice-start-phrase.test.tsx` (5 group-resume pins + 4 chevron tests), `practice-testout.test.tsx` (chevrons hidden), `home-pull-refresh.test.tsx` (band pill; its partial `@/lib/ui` mock now spreads `jest.requireActual`, the initial run failed on the missing `scoreColor` export), `journey-map.test.tsx` (`rail-pulse-dot` counts replace `rail-pulse`, planZoneScenery pins, scenery-layer render). `jest-setup.js` reanimated mock gained `useAnimatedScrollHandler` and `cancelAnimation`.

**Suite state after this work:** mobile 69/437 green; web 52 files / 370 tests green (unchanged); typecheck clean; boot smokes 2/2 (standalone run); api canonical run exits 1 with ONLY the documented pilot-capture "unlisted userId" env fence visible, byte-identical by name, consistent with the certified 682/681/1 baseline (no api files touched by this work).

### Scoring integrity diagnosis — "Namasto" scored Perfect (August 1, 2026, read-only)

**Discriminator (owner-confirmed + server-confirmed):** the error is swallowed at recognition. Dev-DB attempt id 13864 (2026-08-01 11:58:36 UTC, pilot user, phrase 2 gu નમસ્તે/namaste): stored transcript is "નમસ્તે" — byte-identical to the target. Score 100, passed, band perfect, flags `latency_missing`. The evaluation token signs the same fields (transcript નમસ્તે, score 100, band perfect). Banding was honest on a false transcript.

**No audio ever reaches the evaluating LLM.** The eval path is: STT (gpt-4o-mini-transcribe, `language: <code>` + neutral prompt naming the language, target text deliberately omitted) → char-Levenshtein similarity (`pronunciationGuards.ts compareToTarget`) → either fast path (sim ≥ 0.93, pooled copy) or one text-only `gpt-5.4-mini` chat completion receiving language/target/romanized/english/transcript. `gpt-audio` exists only in TTS/chat-voice paths. The "polish on the ending" / "keep the last te crisp" copy in attempt 13864's feedback is LLM-generated coaching flavor from a perfect transcript (the rubric asks it to name one sound to work on); it heard nothing and detected nothing. There is no audio-derived signal in this path that could feed banding.

**Why bespoke LLM copy at sim 1.0 (not the fast-path pool):** sentence-stage sibling phrase 21492 "નમસ્તે." normalizes identically to target phrase 2, so the fast-path wrong-phrase-cap sibling check matches at sim 1.0 and forces the LLM detour on every perfect નમસ્તે attempt. Same pattern seen in prod logs for "કૃપા કરીને." (sim 1). Score outcome identical; only copy source differs.

**High-quality retry cannot catch normalized errors:** the gpt-4o-transcribe second pass fires only when the first transcript is empty or sim ≤ 0.40, and its tie-break keeps whichever transcript is CLOSER to the target. A confident-but-normalized false positive (sim 1.0) never gets a second pass; by construction the retry only ever moves transcripts toward the target.

**NEW BLOCKER FOUND — pilot capture has never uploaded a clip.** All four R2 env vars are set, so `teeAudioToPilot` runs for the allowlisted user, but every PutObject fails and is swallowed at WARN by design:
1. `R2_ACCOUNT_ID` secret holds the full endpoint URL (65 chars, `https://…`), while `r2Client.ts` builds `https://${R2_ACCOUNT_ID}.r2.cloudflarestorage.com` → DNS ENOTFOUND (reproduced exactly).
2. With a corrected endpoint, Cloudflare rejects `R2_ACCESS_KEY_ID`: "Credential access key has length 53, should be 32" — the secret is not an R2 S3 access key id.
Additionally `PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS` contains only the owner's DEV Clerk id, so prod/mobile attempts never tee regardless. No R2 clip exists for attempt 13864 (or any attempt). Credentials must be fixed and verified with a real upload before the labeled capture session, or every clip will be silently lost.

**No code changed in this diagnosis** (read-only per owner instruction; fix design follows the labeled capture set).

### Brief B diagnostics D1 + D2, and #994 pilot-capture logging (August 1, 2026)

**D1 — Web "No phrases found here" on station entry: SERVER-SIDE, HOTFIX-CLASS.** Reproduced live by minting a Clerk dev session for the affected account and calling the API raw:
- `GET /api/lesson-groups/121/phrases` (hi, category 1, position 2) → **HTTP 200 `[]`** while `GET /api/categories/1/lesson-groups/hi` simultaneously reports group 121 as `status: "unlocked", phraseCount: 10`.
- Mechanism: the premium split was derived BY INDEX at seed time, so free starter rows all live in each category's position-1 group — **every position-2+ group is 10/10 premium** (verified for gu and hi in dev DB). The phrases route filters premium rows for callers whose plan lacks `extendedLibrary` (`learning.ts` ~2150) and returns a legitimate 200 with an empty array. The listing's `phraseCount`/`status` are NOT plan-filtered, so the journey map boards a station the phrase route then serves empty.
- Client: `practice.tsx` renders "No phrases found here" ONLY on 200-with-zero-phrases; 402 → UpgradeScreen, other errors → LessonErrorScreen. No swallowed errors (Brief B Q2 answered).
- Exonerated: commit 4337839 (resume start-index only, runs after data load), W7 BandPill change (home recent-plays display only). The hole predates both: premium-by-index seeding + D1 station grouping.
- Critical account fact: the owner's dev web account (`user_3H9XG2UWijYqRUXv6Ls0geMmX4Z`) is currently **tier=free** (subscription fields all NULL) — not All-Access. On a true All-Access account neither route can go empty (filter skipped). The pass copy the owner saw ("Stop 2 · 0/10", non-showroom unlock states on the gu line) is consistent with the account having held a gu-allowed, non-extendedLibrary state at test time; today's state (free) makes gu teaser-gated (556 → 402 teaser 2/3, taste group 555 → 3 teaser rows). Tier history is not recoverable from current data.
- Q5: the phrasebook/category route applies the same premium filter, but every category holds its free starter rows, so it cannot go fully empty for Free — the group slicing is what concentrates premium rows into whole stations.
- Fix (not implemented; hotfix design): unlock derivation, listing counts, and phrase serving must agree on PLAN-VISIBLE rows — either mark plan-empty stations locked-with-upsell on the map, or make station unlock/serving plan-aware. Product decision needed on which.

**D2 — Mobile chat once-per-install auto-record: CONFIRMED, fix is respin scope.** `bolo-mobile/app/(app)/(tabs)/chat.tsx` lacks the web "positive hold-confirmation" fix. First-install flow: pressIn → `isPressingRef=true` → `prepareRecorder()` → iOS permission dialog suspends the gesture → on grant, `recorder.record()` (line ~500) starts with no finger down. Both branches submit garbage: (a) no touch-cancel delivered → recording runs headless until silence auto-stop → `handleStopRecording` SUBMITS the capture as a turn; (b) touch-cancel delivered → post-start guard calls `handleStopRecording`, which also submits (no discard path, no min-duration guard, lines ~515-548). Also: the idle pre-warm (line ~330) triggers the permission dialog on first chat-tab mount without any press — web pre-warms only when permission is already granted. Fix design: gate `record()` on a verifiably-still-down press at grant-resolve and discard (not submit) when it isn't; add a min-duration guard.
- Q2 (language hint): YES — client sends `languageCode` in the turn body; the chat route parses it, gates via `denyLockedLanguage`, and passes `languageName`+`languageCode` into the turn pipeline whose STT stage logs `[stt] … lang=<code>`. Same hint family as practice.
- Q3 (echo): no ducking — `handleStartRecording` hard-stops playback before the mic opens; mode flips are serialized through `lib/audio.ts` sessionChain.

**#994 pilot-capture logging (code change):** `lib/pilotCapture.ts` catch upgraded from swallowed `console.warn` to `console.error` with a running failure count and the LOST clip key; success now logs the clip key + running success count (doubles as E2E evidence in server logs). New export `getPilotCaptureCounters()` for tests/health checks. Response path still unaffected (never rethrows). Typecheck clean; pilot-capture test file 3/3 pass (must run with `PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS` unset — the shared env var otherwise breaks the file's empty-allowlist assertion; the suite script handles this). R2 secrets still malformed — corrected values requested from owner; E2E tee verification pending their arrival.

### S1 amendment: global honesty cap (32.1 respin confirmed amendment, August 1, 2026)

**Change:** the honesty cap is now unconditional on ALL scored paths. Previously `honestyCapApplies` required `targetSim.sim === 1.0 && !sttDisagreement`, leaving a residual gap where a near-match transcript (sim < 1.0) rated 95+ by the LLM could band "perfect". After the amendment: `Math.min(rawFastScore, 92)` on the fast path, `if (score > 92) score = 92` on the LLM path - both unconditional. `honestyCapApplies` is retained for copy pool selection only (the "our ears could verify" copy applies when transcript exactly equals the target AND passes agree; near-miss high scores use the standard copy pool).

**Tests updated:** `openai.pronunciation.honesty.test.ts` comment on "non-matching transcripts" updated (cap is global, 55 < 92 so outcome unchanged); new test added: "global honesty cap: near-match (sim < 1.0) with high LLM score -> capped at 92, cannot band perfect" (namast against namaste, llmScore=95, asserts score=92 band=great not perfect). `openai.pronunciation.fast-path.test.ts` unchanged (all 20 pass - the monotonicity test's loose `>= 86` assertion holds at 92). Honesty file 11/11, fast-path 20/20; typecheck clean.

**Baseline arithmetic clarification:** the 32.1 gate report cited "372 baseline + 3 R3 = 375". The +2 vs the 370 BRIEF-B certified baseline are R5 (home fade mask pin added to home-pull-refresh, +1) and R4 (tear-audio pin in phrasebook-door, +1), both code-complete in this session. 370 + 2 (R4/R5 pins) + 3 (R3 bolo-quiz-order-tiles) = 375 confirmed. Canonical api run definition: `env -u PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS node --import tsx --experimental-test-module-mocks --test 'src/**/*.test.ts'` from `artifacts/api-server`, exit 0, 699/699 pass. ### Capture mode (STANDING pilot tool, BRIEF 32.1 respin, August 2, 2026; made permanent by owner ruling August 2, 2026)

**Purpose:** the pilot calibration-corpus capture protocol made explicit in the web practice page, replacing the item-1 layout fix (superseded above). PERMANENT scaffolding (owner ruling post-Round-1, Aug 2, 2026): additional language capture rounds will run over time, so this is NOT removed after the first corpus. It stays dev-only, param-gated, and server-checked by allowlist exactly as built. The "TEMPORARY capture mode" comment tags in code remain as touch-point locators only; they no longer imply scheduled removal.

**Activation:** `?mode=capture` on web practice (`practice.tsx`), allowlisted users only. The client asks `GET /pilot-capture/eligibility` (new openapi op `getPilotCaptureEligibility`, backed by `isPilotCaptureUser()` = membership in `PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS`); without a server-confirmed yes the param is inert and the session is normal. Review/test-out modes never combine with capture.

**Protocol flow:** per phrase exactly 4 attempts in fixed order (module const `CAPTURE_STEPS`): native ("YOUR BEST PRONUNCIATION", with a listen-first note), american_accent, subtle_error (final vowel), wrong_attempt. A persistent amber banner (`data-testid="capture-banner"`) shows "attempt N of 4" + the expectation. After each evaluation: NO result card/score/band/cues/confetti — new `SessionState` `"capture_saved"` shows a checkmark card ("Attempt N saved", `data-testid="capture-saved"`) for 900ms, then auto-advances (next expectation, or `handleNext()` via `handleNextRef` after attempt 4). `createAttempt` is skipped entirely in capture mode: no attempt row, no XP, no lesson progress. The evaluation pipeline (STT, scoring, R2 tee) runs unchanged. Prev/next chevrons hidden (free navigation would desync the protocol). Coach autoplay at each new phrase is the existing behavior, unchanged.

**Labels in the sidecar:** `PronunciationInput` gains optional `captureLabel` (enum of the 4 labels) + `captureAttemptOfFour` (1-4); `openai.ts` passes them to both `teeAudioToPilot` call sites as `capture` opts; `pilotCapture.ts` writes `label`, `captureMode: true`, `attemptOfFour` into the sidecar JSON. Fields are inert for non-allowlisted users (tee returns early). Harvest reads labels directly; attempt-order reconstruction is now the fallback for pre-capture-mode clips only.

**Redo affordance:** banner button "Redo previous attempt" (idle only) calls `POST /pilot-capture/discard-last` (`discardLastCapture()`: re-PUTs the caller's most recent capture sidecar with `discarded: true`, tracked via an in-memory per-user Map — a server restart forgets it, acceptable for immediate fumbles), then steps the client back to the same phrase+expectation (cross-phrase capable via stored phraseId). Client steps back even on a server no-op; harvest prefers the later duplicate.

**Tests/codegen:** openapi + `pnpm --filter @workspace/api-spec run codegen` regenerated api-zod + api-client-react (new hooks `useGetPilotCaptureEligibility`, `useDiscardLastPilotCapture`; the shared web test mock base auto-derives them). `openai.pronunciation.pilot-capture.test.ts` is now 4 tests (+1: capture labels land in sidecar, discard rewrites same key, second discard no-ops; test 2 additionally pins that non-capture sidecars carry no capture fields) — canonical api count 699 -> 700. Web practice suites unchanged (26/26).

### #998 verify-time honesty clamp (August 2, 2026, post-session STEP 2)

**Escape it closes:** signed evaluation tokens outlive deploys by their 15-minute TTL, so a token signed by a pre-cap binary could be replayed against a capped one and its claims written verbatim (proven live Aug 1: attempt 14097 wrote 100/perfect nineteen minutes after the S1 cap shipped; NULL stt columns fingerprinted the pre-respin signer).

**Fix:** `evaluationToken.ts` exports `HONESTY_SCORE_CAP = 92`; `verifyEvaluation` now clamps any claim with score > cap to 92/'great' — the single choke point covering every token consumer (/attempts and test-out). `nocatch` passes through untouched (a system miss is not a pronunciation claim). New optional claim `audioJudged?: boolean` is EXEMPT from the clamp: scoring v2 groundwork — only an audio-aware judge that actually heard the clip may certify above-cap scores. The two literal 92s in `openai.ts` (fast-path Math.min and LLM-path cap) now reference the constant, so signing and verification can never drift.

**Tests:** new `evaluationToken.test.ts` with three pins (replayed 100/perfect token verifies to 92/'great'; nocatch passthrough; audioJudged above-cap verbatim). `attempts.test.ts` badge tests now sign `audioJudged: true` tokens — a raw 100 clamps, so the audio-judged path is the only way perfect_100 awards until v2, and this doubles as end-to-end exemption coverage. Canonical count arithmetic corrected: 699 (32.1 gate) + 1 (honesty near-match pin) + 1 (pilot-capture labels test) = 701 baseline; + 3 pins = **704** certified this run (the capture-mode entry's "700" missed the honesty pin).

**Data repair:** attempt 14097 only: UPDATE to score=92, band='great' (xp untouched); verified by readback. Older pre-cap history intentionally untouched.

### Pilot corpus harvest + promotion-gate calibration (August 2, 2026, post-session STEPs 4-5)

**Harvest (`qa/harvest-pilot-corpus.mjs`, standing tool):** reads every sidecar under `pilot-clips/` in R2 and writes `qa/pilot-results/manifest.json` (gitignored; clips referenced by R2 key only). Explicit capture-mode sidecar labels are authoritative; `discarded: true` sidecars are excluded; duplicate (user, phrase, attemptOfFour) slots keep the later timestamp. Order reconstruction (position -> native/american_accent/subtle_error/wrong_attempt) applies ONLY to pre-capture-mode clips in exact quads; other group sizes go to needsReview. Owner amendments applied: owner-yahoo clips before 2026-08-02T02:10Z discarded as owner_warmup; the three fumbled quads (kruti818 hi maaf kijiye; pikaboyliam hi dhanyavaad + maaf kijiye) discarded — they surfaced as 2-member groups, not the predicted 3, so discard matching is by (user, language, normalized romanization), never by group size. Every clip's container is sniffed (EBML magic -> webm); all 237 corpus clips are webm under .m4a keys. Round-1 result: 237 labeled (197 explicit, 40 order-reconstructed), 17 needsReview groups, 15 discarded, 0 missing.

**Calibration (`qa/calibrate-promotion-gate.mjs`, standing tool):** per clip 3 parallel gpt-audio calls via the AI-integrations proxy (never the direct key), spec-2-verbatim rubric prompt, 5s timeout, median-of-3 (2-of-3 = rounded mean), spread = max-min. Raw results in `qa/pilot-results/calibration/results.jsonl` (gitignored) — append-only, last-record-wins, failed records retryable, so the run is resume-safe and idempotent; `--report` re-analyzes without API calls. Empirical constraints baked in (all discovered this run): input_audio accepts ONLY wav/mp3 (webm 400s, contra the spec signature) so clips are ffmpeg-converted to 16kHz mono mp3; the proxy 429s under burst (clip-concurrency 2 + 3s/8s/15s backoff-on-429); gpt-audio intermittently refuses short clips as "no audio" — 0.4s silence padding rescued 10 of 14, 4 clips (1.7%) remained permanently unscoreable across formats and ~18 attempts each.

**Round-1 verdict (233/237 scored): calibration gate FAILS on the current rubric.** Native promotion (>= 93) is far under the 80% bar in all three languages (gu 20%, hi 11%, mr 0%) — the judge lands genuine native attempts in the 82-92 band. Subtle-error floor breaches: gu 10 below 55 (cap 1), hi 3. wrong_attempt < 55 holds in hi/mr, 4 gu violations (incl. a 92 on single-syllable "ha"). american_accent containment: 3 gu violations (all monosyllables) + 1 hi. Spread <= 30 nearly holds (3 violations). Monosyllables (ha/na) are the dominant failure locus on both tails. Rubric adjustment proposals went to the owner as text; NO re-run without explicit owner release (standing rule).
### Calibration round 2 (August 2, 2026, owner CALIBRATION RULING; single authorized re-run)

**Ruling applied (all three in the spec + harness):** (1) rubric prompt gains the line "If the speaker sounds like a fluent native, score 95-100; do not reserve scores above 92 for flawlessness" — fixed at the source in spec section 2, promotion cut stays 93; (2) BINDING v2 design ruling recorded in spec section 2: monosyllabic phrases (ha/na/any single-syllable target) are excluded from gate criteria AND from production judge promotion entirely (text-path result stands, one phoneme cannot evidence Perfect); (3) criteria restructured — BINDING: no wrong >= 68 nor promoting, no subtle promotes, no american promotes, native >= 80% at 93, spread <= 30; ADVISORY non-gating: wrong >= 55 count (target 0), subtle < 55 proportion (target <= 25%). Unscoreable clips are excluded from denominators. The harness (`qa/calibrate-promotion-gate.mjs`) implements all of this; monosyllable detection = single romanized word with <= 1 vowel group.

**Refusal fix that ended the "permanently unscoreable" class:** refusals are clip+conversion specific, not clip-permanent. Uniform 0.4s padding (baked into the fresh `/tmp/pilot-corpus-mp3-padded` cache) rescued round-1's refusers but CAUSED refusals on other clips that had scored fine unpadded; a manual fallback ladder (0.4s pad -> unpadded -> 1.0s pad, overwriting the cached mp3 between harness passes) scored **237/237** — round 1's four "permanent" refusals all yielded. Round-1 raw results archived at `qa/pilot-results/calibration/results-round1.jsonl` (gitignored).

**Round-2 verdict: gate still FAILS, and the rubric line did NOT lift the 92 ceiling.** Native medians still cluster 85-88 (median-of-medians gu 87 / hi 85 / mr 87, vs 87/85/85 in round 1); native promotion at 93 is gu 8%, hi 7%, mr 0%, and even at a simulated 88 cut only ~30% (gu 8/25, hi 8/29, mr 1/3). The restructured safety criteria all PASS everywhere (wrong containment, subtle containment, spread except one hi clip at 32); new violations: exactly one american_accent promotion per large language (gu owner "kem chho?" 95, hi owner "namaste" 94 — both owner clips, plausibly too-good accent imitations). Monosyllable exclusion removed 22 gu clips and with them all round-1 monosyllable violations. No third run without a new ruling; the 88-cut tradeoff data went to the owner as text.

### Chunk 2 Stage A: cross-script transcript normalization + allowlisted nocatch diagnostics (August 2, 2026)

**A1, the bridge.** `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/crossScript.ts` is the owner-supplied module, integrated verbatim (zero rewrites). Single integration point: `compareToTarget` in `pronunciationGuards.ts` now wraps the unchanged pre-existing comparison (renamed internally to `rawCompareToTarget`) and, when the raw result is incomparable OR comparable with sim < 0.93 (Ruling 2), calls `normalizeForComparison` and takes max(raw, bridged). Rescue-only contract: a comparable raw result can only be raised, never lowered; a raw-incomparable pair becomes comparable only when bridgedSim >= 0.45 (deliberately the same evidential bar as guard 1b's Latin rule), otherwise it stays incomparable (nocatch) with bridge metadata attached. All three scoring consumers (fast path, `applyScoreGuards`, dual-pass effectiveSim via `chooseConservativeTranscript`) flow through `compareToTarget`, so all inherit the bridge from the one call site. Sibling wrong-phrase comparisons NEVER bridge (`{ noBridge: true }` at the guards `otherPhrases` loop and the fast-path sibling loop): a bridged sibling hit could only add wrong-phrase caps, a penalty, violating rescue-only. `PhoneticComparison` gained optional `bridge` metadata (`bridged`, `rawComparable`, `rawSim`, `bridgedSim`, both comparable strings); its presence means the bridge was attempted. A `normalizeForComparison` throw is caught and the raw result kept (transliteration failure must never break scoring). Rescues log with the `[xscript]` prefix. Accepted behavior change: the formerly canonical false nocatch (Devanagari transcript of phonetically identical Gujarati speech) now bridges at sim 1.0 and scores via the near-match floor; the stale pins in `pronunciationGuards.test.ts` were updated to the new contract and `crossScript.test.ts` pins the module plus integration rules (all sims empirically measured before pinning).

**Perso-Arabic honest limit (Ruling 3), verbatim from the module:** "Perso-Arabic (ur, sd, ks) is NOT reliably transliterable by sanscript; for those, normalization falls back to a consonant-skeleton comparison (below) rather than pretending." A both-sides-Perso-Arabic pair uses the skeleton check; a mixed Perso-Arabic/Indic pair returns `bridged: false` and keeps the raw incomparable result, so it still resolves nocatch (cause `script_mismatch`). No ur transliteration table was added.

**A2, nocatch diagnostics.** `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/nocatchDiagnostics.ts` exports `writeNocatchDiagnostic`, which uploads one JSON sidecar to R2 at `nocatch-diagnostics/<languageCode>/<uuid>.json` when an allowlisted account's attempt finalizes as nocatch. Allowlist reuses the PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS mechanism (`isPilotCaptureUser`); the three route call sites in `openai.ts` gate inline BEFORE any payload assembly, and the lib re-checks internally as defense in depth. Fire-and-forget after `res.json()`, fail-open (upload failures are logged with `[nocatch-diag]` and swallowed; the function never throws). The similarity field is named `similarityValues`, never "confidence" (this pipeline has no STT confidence). Cause taxonomy (Ruling 5, extended by #1067): `empty_audio_or_silence`, `undecodable_audio` (transcription API rejected the recording as corrupt/unsupported; Aug 12, 2026), `dual_pass_uncorroborated`, `script_mismatch`, `latin_low_sim`, `unsupported_language`, `no_match_after_bridge` (bridge attempted, bridged true, similarity still below the floor). Call sites: the unsupported-language exit writes cause `unsupported_language` with null transcripts; the empty-transcript exit splits on `sttChosenEmptyWithEvidence` (`dual_pass_uncorroborated` vs `empty_audio_or_silence`); the guard-nocatch exit classifies `latin_low_sim` when the comparison was comparable, else `no_match_after_bridge` vs `script_mismatch` by bridge outcome. Sidecars carry both raw STT pass transcripts, the chosen transcript, normalized forms via the now-exported `normalizeForAgreement`, the `bridged` boolean, and both comparable strings when a bridge was attempted. No analysis code exists; sidecars are write-only.

**Canonical api-server suite baseline (Ruling 6): 704/15.** 703 passing plus the single pilot-capture unlisted-userId env-trap failure, which is known-environmental in this workspace (PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS set via `.replit`). See the debt row; it is not to be fixed as part of Chunk 2A and never counts as regression.

### Chunk 4A: zone test-out engine, cross-zone gate (dark), attempt-time hardening (August 2, 2026)

**Zone test-out engine.** New table `zone_testouts` (migration `0036_calm_blue_shield.sql`, `lib/db/src/schema/zoneTestouts.ts`): append-only submission log keyed (user_id, language_code, category_id, passed, created_at) with a lookup index on (user_id, language_code, category_id, created_at). FKs to users and categories. Two new endpoints in `learning.ts`:

- `GET /zones/:categoryId/test-out/:lang` — builds a `ZoneTestoutContext` (all phrase-stage and sentence-stage lesson groups in the category, each filtered by plan visibility), picks one phrase per station up to `ZONE_TESTOUT_SAMPLE_CAP = 10`, and returns the sample with `sampleSize` and `requiredCorrect = ceil(0.8 * sampleSize)`. Phrase objects include `lessonGroupId` alongside the standard `serializePhrase` shape so the client can display station provenance. Rate-gate runs before token work: non-authoritative SELECT count first; authoritative recheck plus insert are inside a single transaction behind `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(userId||':zone_ratelimit'), hashtext(categoryId||':'||languageCode))`. Cap: `ZONE_TESTOUT_MAX_PER_WINDOW = 3` per rolling `ZONE_TESTOUT_WINDOW_MS = 3600000` ms.
- `POST /zones/:categoryId/test-out` — validates server-signed evaluationTokens (user match, phrase visible in the zone, no duplicate phrases, no duplicate stations, exact sampleSize count), grades on `isFullCreditBand` count vs `requiredCorrect`, inserts the log row, and on pass upserts `tested_out` for every member group (preserving `completed` via `CASE WHEN ... = 'completed' THEN 'completed' ELSE 'tested_out' END`). Advisory-lock pattern is the same as the GET rate-limit. Returns `{ passed, correctCount, requiredCorrect, sampleSize, status }`.

**Cross-zone gate (dark).** `CROSS_ZONE_GATE_ENABLED` in `artifacts/api-server/src/lib/featureFlags.ts` is a module-private `let` initialized from `process.env.CROSS_ZONE_GATE_ENABLED === "true"` and exported via a getter alias. A test-only `__setCrossZoneGateForTests(value)` setter follows the existing `__resetTeaserCacheForTests` convention (never callable in production without source access). When the flag is on:

- `deriveAndLatchUnlock` (in `lessonGroupAccess.ts`) short-circuits to return all groups as `locked` when `zoneGateAllows()` returns false for the caller.
- `loadTestoutGroup` (stop-level test-out preamble) returns 403 `zone_locked` when the caller's zone is gated.
- `loadZoneTestout` (zone GET/POST preamble) returns 403 `zone_locked` when the caller's zone is gated.

`zoneGateAllows(userId, categoryId, languageCode)` in `lessonGroupAccess.ts`: finds the zone with the next-lower `sortOrder` (the immediate predecessor zone). Zone 1 (no predecessor) is always eligible. For zones 2+, the predecessor must have ALL its lesson groups in `completed`, `tested_out`, or meeting live-completion criteria (`isZoneComplete`). `isZoneComplete` is a pure helper in `lessonGroupUnlock.ts`: a zone is complete when every group in (categoryId, languageCode) has a persisted `completed` or `tested_out` progress row.

**Attempt-time hardening.** Inside `POST /attempts`, after `verifyEvaluation` and `denyLockedLanguage`, a new guard rejects first-ever attempts on phrases whose lesson group is sequentially locked. Precondition chain: `langAccess.state === "allowed"` AND `claims.phraseId != null` AND phrase row exists AND `phraseRow.lessonGroupId != null` AND `phraseRow.languageCode === claims.languageCode` (per-language scope; cross-language token minting is an open question tracked outside this commit). If all conditions hold and no prior attempt exists for (userId, phraseId), `getUnlockedGroupIds` is called and a locked group returns 403 `lesson_group_locked`. Retakes (prior attempt exists) are always allowed regardless of lock state.

**openapi.yaml additions:** `ZoneTestoutInput` request schema; `GET /zones/{categoryId}/test-out/{lang}` and `POST /zones/{categoryId}/test-out` path entries (separate path objects to avoid orval `Params` collision on path+query). Codegen regenerated: `lib/api-client-react` and `lib/api-zod` (including new type files `zoneTestoutInput.ts` and `zoneTestoutInputAttemptsItem.ts`).

**Tests:** `artifacts/api-server/src/routes/learning.zone-testout.test.ts` (32 new tests, 15 suites total unchanged). Coverage: zone GET station sampling and sampleSize cap, plan-visibility gating (premium phrases, sentence stations), rate-limit 429 shape and retake counting, zone POST pass/fail grading and latch writes, zone POST station-distinctness and token-validity rejection, cross-zone gate flag on/off for groups listing and test-out routes, gate exemption for zone 1, attempt-time hardening first-ever vs retake. FK cleanup in the test's `after()` uses raw `pool.query` for all language-FK tables (xp_ledger, user_ability, badges, chat_turns, game_sessions, phrase_reports, daily_quiz_completions, daily_quizzes, lesson_generations) before the Drizzle-managed phrase/group/lesson/category/language deletes.

**Suite state after Chunk 4A:** api 752 tests / 15 suites, 751 pass / 1 fail (pilot-capture env trap, unchanged). Typecheck clean across all workspaces. Both boot smokes pass (inside the canonical run). Code-review subagent finding: rate-limit SELECT-then-INSERT was not atomic; resolved with the advisory-lock pattern described above.

### Chunk 4B: zone test-out web entry + verdict (August 2, 2026)

Web only (`artifacts/gujarati-coach`); api-server, bolo-mobile, lib/, schema, and openapi.yaml untouched. Parameterization of the existing Build 31 test-out flow, not a fork.

**practice.tsx zone scope.** `/practice/:id?mode=testout&scope=zone` (no `group` param) runs the exact same test-out session as the stop-level flow. `isTestout` is now the umbrella of `isGroupTestout` (existing `?group=` path, unchanged) and new `isZoneTestout`. Data source swaps at one seam: `activeTestoutQuery = isZoneTestout ? useGetZoneTestout(id, activeLang) : useGetLessonGroupTestout(groupId)` (identical `{ phrases, sampleSize, requiredCorrect }` envelope, same 402 upgrade handling). Submit swaps in `submitTestoutRun`: zone scope posts `{ languageCode, attempts }` via `useSubmitZoneTestout`; the verdict screen reads `activeTestoutSubmit` (verdict states byte-identical to stop-level). `backHref` for zone scope is `/journey`. Pass invalidation: exact `getListCategoryLessonGroupsQueryKey(id, activeLang)` plus predicate prefix sweep of keys matching `/api/lesson-groups/*/phrases` (the sample does not carry member group ids). 403 `{ error: "zone_locked" }` from either call (detected by `isZoneLockedError`): GET renders `LessonErrorScreen` with the copy "Finish the previous zone first, or test out of it." (new optional `message` prop on `LessonErrorScreen`, default copy unchanged); POST renders the verdict error state with the same copy, resubmit hidden, back-navigation to `/journey`.

**journey.tsx entries.** (1) Progression-lock dialog second action `link-test-out-zone` below the stop-level link: "Test out of this whole zone" / "One phrase from each stop. Pass to unlock everything here.", href `/practice/{zoneId}?mode=testout&scope=zone`. (2) `ZonePostcard` optional `testOutHref` prop rendering a "Test out of this zone" link (`link-zone-test-out-{zoneIndex}`), passed only when the zone is gate-locked: `stations.length > 0 && every status === "locked" && !some planLocked`. Dormant pre-flip by construction (every zone's first stop is unlocked pre-flip; showroom zones carry planLocked stops), activates when `CROSS_ZONE_GATE_ENABLED` flips.

**Suite state after Chunk 4B:** web 412 tests / 58 files, all pass (`journey-testout-dialog.test.tsx` gained the zone-action test). Web typecheck clean. ### Chunk 5A: Chai token economy server, earn-only slice (August 2, 2026)

Server-only (`artifacts/api-server`, `lib/db`). No client changes in this chunk. The UI spend vignette is a separate follow-on task.

**New DB tables (lib/db/src/schema/).**
- `tokenLedgerTable` (`token_ledger`): append-only, columns `id`, `userId` (FK→users ON DELETE CASCADE), `delta` (integer, pos=earn neg=spend 0=consume), `balanceAfter`, `reason` (`TokenReason` union), `refId` (arbitrary string), `createdAt`. Unique index on `(userId, reason, refId)` — idempotency rides this index; every grant/spend is a silent no-op on duplicate.
- `userTokenStateTable` (`user_token_state`): one row per user, columns `userId` (PK via FK→users ON DELETE CASCADE), `balance`, `stationPausesEquipped`, `expressMultiplierExpiresAt` (nullable), `updatedAt`. Insert-or-ignore on first use.
- Both tables use `ON DELETE CASCADE` on the users FK (migration 0038 fixed the original NO ACTION in 0037) to let test teardown delete the test user without FK violations.

**Constants and types (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/tokenEconomy.ts`).**
- `TOKEN_EARN_STREAK_DAY = 1`, `TOKEN_EARN_ZONE_COMPLETE = 5`, `TOKEN_EARN_EXPRESS_STAMP = 3`, `TOKEN_EARN_QUIZ = 2`, `TOKEN_ALLOWANCE_ALL_ACCESS_MONTHLY = 30`.
- `STATION_PAUSE_COST = 10`, `STATION_PAUSE_MAX_EQUIPPED = 2`, `EXPRESS_MULTIPLIER_COST = 20`, `EXPRESS_MULTIPLIER_MINUTES = 30`.
- `EXPRESS_MULTIPLIER_FACTOR = 2` (doubles XP during the window).
- `TokenReason` union of all earn/spend/consume reason strings.
- `SpendItem = "station_pause" | "express_multiplier"`.

**Token service (`artifacts/api-server/src/lib/tokenService.ts`).**
- `grantTokens(userId, reason, refId, amount)`: idempotent via ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING on the ledger unique index.
- `spendTokens(userId, item, refId)`: throws `InsufficientTokensError` (409) or `SpendConflictError` (409, codes `pause_max_equipped` | `multiplier_active`). Spend 409 is NEVER 402 — that status is reserved for the UpgradeRequired entitlement envelope.
- `consumePausesForGap(userId, missedDates)`: consumes equipped pauses to bridge missed streak days. Partial coverage is a no-op (all-or-nothing: can't cover N dates with fewer than N pauses). Returns `Set<string>` of dates covered (including previously covered).
- `getOrCreateTokenState(userId)`: read-or-create, idempotent.
- `listPausedDayKeys(userId)`: returns `Set<string>` of YYYY-MM-DD keys from `station_pause_consumed` rows, for streak derivation.

**Token routes (`artifacts/api-server/src/routes/tokens.ts`).**
- `GET /tokens`: returns `{ balance, stationPausesEquipped, expressMultiplierExpiresAt }`.
- `POST /tokens/spend`: body `{ item, refId }` (body schema exported as `SpendTokensBody`, deviation from spec's `TokensSpendBody` — imported correctly in the route). Returns spend result or 409 with `{ error: "insufficient_tokens"|"pause_max_equipped"|"multiplier_active", ... }`.
- Both routes require authentication (Clerk session).
- `maybeGrantAllowance(req)` is exported for use in other routes (currently unused; monthly allowance hook is wired for Plus users via the attempts path).

**Earn hook wiring.**
- HOOK 1a/1b: `POST /attempts` — `getOrCreateTokenState` in the Promise.all; `effectiveXp` uses `EXPRESS_MULTIPLIER_FACTOR` when multiplier is active (used for XP ledger write and XP state update).
- HOOK 1c: `POST /attempts` — streak-day grant (`earn_streak_day`, refId = local YYYY-MM-DD) in side-effects block.
- HOOK 1d: `POST /attempts` — `consumePausesForGap` after side-effects, bridges 1-2 missed days between attempts.
- HOOK 2a: `POST /zones/:categoryId/test-out` pass path — fire-and-forget `earn_zone_complete` grant.
- HOOK 2b+3: `POST /lesson-groups/:id/test-out` pass path (in `learning.ts`) — fire-and-forget express stamp + zone-complete check.
- HOOK 2c: `lessonGroupAccess.ts` — after zone-complete latch write, fire-and-forget `earn_zone_complete` grant.
- HOOK 4: `games.ts` — fire-and-forget `earn_quiz` after quiz completion insert.
- HOOK 6: `GET /progress/summary` — `listPausedDayKeys` added to the big Promise.all; passed to `computeProgressMetrics` and `computeSpeakingStreakDays`.

**Streak integration.** `computeProgressMetrics`, `computeStreakDays`, `computeSpeakingStreakDays`, `computeExtendedProgressMetrics` all gained optional `pausedDayKeys?: Set<string>` param. `badgeAward.ts` calls `listPausedDayKeys` and passes it to `computeExtendedProgressMetrics`.

**OpenAPI additions.** `GET /tokens`, `POST /tokens/spend`, schemas `TokenBalance`, `SpendTokensBody`, `SpendTokensResult` added to `lib/api-spec/openapi.yaml`.

**Migrations.** `0037_common_sentry.sql` — DDL for both tables (original, ON DELETE NO ACTION). `0038_token_fk_cascade.sql` — drops and recreates both FKs with ON DELETE CASCADE. Rationale: test teardown deletes the test user via Drizzle cascade; NO ACTION FKs violate when token rows exist.

**Test suite (`artifacts/api-server/src/test/tokenEconomy.test.ts`).** 19 tests (2 suites added, one existing extended): grantTokens idempotency, spendTokens insufficient + pause_max_equipped + multiplier_active + duplicate refId, consumePausesForGap gap coverage + refusal + idempotency, allowance monthly, streak-day grant, zone-complete grant, express stamp, GET /tokens shape, multiplier XP doubling, pausedDayKeys streak bridging, listPausedDayKeys coverage. Tests self-provision a dedicated test user and clean up via `after()`.

**dbMock.ts additions.** `tokenLedgerTable: {}`, `userTokenStateTable: {}`, `insertTokenLedgerSchema: {}`, `insertUserTokenStateSchema: {}` added to satisfy the `satisfies DbValueExports` guard.

**Suite state after Chunk 5A:** api 786 tests / 30 suites, 785 pass / 1 fail (pilot-capture env trap, audited skip — fires only when PILOT_CAPTURE_USER_IDS is set in .replit). Typecheck clean. Code-review subagent: PASS (atomicity, idempotency, 409-not-402, no negative balances). Both boot smokes pass.

### Chunk 6A: game-session context + once-ever Chai grants (August 3, 2026, api-server only)

**Scope:** `artifacts/api-server`, `lib/api-spec` + generated client output, `docs/CODEBASE-FACTS.md`. Zero schema changes, zero migrations, zero web/mobile file touches.

**tokenEconomy.ts additions.** Two new constants: `SIGNAL_FIRST_CLEAR_CHAI = 1` and `CLOSEOUT_FIRST_CHAI = 2` (comment: "ruled Aug 2; once-ever per identity via the ledger index"). `TokenReason` union gains `"earn_signal_first_clear"` and `"earn_closeout_first"` — type-level change only; the `token_ledger.reason` column is `text` so no DDL required.

**GameSessionBody extension.** Two optional fields added to the inline zod schema in `routes/learning.ts`: `context: z.enum(["hub","signal","closeout"]).optional()` and `contextRef: z.string().regex(/^gap-[0-9]+$/).optional()`. Post-parse validation enforces: `contextRef` is required when `context === "signal"` and forbidden otherwise; both violations return 400 "Invalid game session payload". Absent context is byte-identical to today in every respect.

**Grant logic.** After the session insert + XP write succeed (the 422 path grants nothing): signal context calls `grantTokens(userId, "earn_signal_first_clear", \`${languageCode}:${categoryId}:${contextRef}\`, 1)`; closeout calls `grantTokens(userId, "earn_closeout_first", \`${languageCode}:${categoryId}\`, 2)`. Idempotency is the ledger's unique `(user_id, reason, ref_id)` index — replays are silent no-ops. Hub and absent context fire no grant. Errors are caught and logged (warn); response proceeds regardless.

**chaiGranted response field.** The 201 body gains `chaiGranted` (optional integer) via `...(chaiGranted !== undefined && { chaiGranted })`. Absent-context responses carry no `chaiGranted` key at all. Presence comes from the `granted` boolean returned by `grantTokensDetailed`, which reports whether THIS call inserted the ledger row; no extra query needed. The original `stateBefore`/`stateAfter` balance compare was retired (Aug 5, 2026) because a concurrent unrelated grant (streak day, monthly allowance) landing between the two reads would be mis-attributed to the session and over-report Chai. Both grant paths on the route use the flag, matching the attempt-response Chai receipt precedent.

**OpenAPI + codegen.** `GameSessionInput` gains `context` (enum) and `contextRef` (pattern) as optional fields. `GameSessionResult` gains optional `chaiGranted` integer. Codegen ran cleanly; generated client files updated: `lib/api-client-react/src/generated/api.schemas.ts`, `lib/api-zod/src/generated/api.ts`, `lib/api-zod/src/generated/types/gameSessionInput.ts`, `lib/api-zod/src/generated/types/gameSessionResult.ts`, `lib/api-zod/src/generated/types/index.ts`.

**New test file `artifacts/api-server/src/test/gameSessions.test.ts`.** 14 tests / 4 suites: absent/hub produce zero grant ledger rows; signal first-clear grants 1 Chai + ledger row with composed refId; signal replay leaves balance and row count unchanged; closeout grants 2 Chai once, replay no-ops; contextRef zod regex rejects bad patterns; post-parse check rejects contextRef with non-signal context and signal without contextRef. ### Tiny-UI batch 3: app-wide toast repair, Chai cell affordance (August 3, 2026, web only)

**Scope.** `artifacts/gujarati-coach` only: `src/components/ui/toaster.tsx`, `src/pages/home.tsx`, `src/test/home-stats-banner.test.tsx`, plus this doc.

**Item 1 root cause and the one-Toaster rule.** Every toast on web was silently broken because `src/components/ui/toaster.tsx` was an empty stub (`return null`) with the comment "Adding an empty Toaster component since we import it in App.tsx": `toast()` dispatched into the use-toast store, but the mounted outlet rendered nothing, so no toast ever painted anywhere. The fix is the standard shadcn Toaster implementation in that same file (useToast subscription rendering ToastProvider, Toast, ToastTitle, ToastDescription, ToastClose, ToastViewport from `ui/toast.tsx`). RULE: keep exactly ONE Toaster, mounted once in App.tsx inside the provider tree that wraps AppRouter (every route, authenticated or not, renders it). Never mount a second Toaster and never introduce a parallel toast system; `ui/sonner.tsx` exists but is unmounted and unused, and must stay that way unless a deliberate migration replaces the whole system. A dispatch-to-paint probe test verified the repair end to end and was deleted (session rule: no new committed test files).

**Items 2 and 3 required no code change.** Task 919 already shipped both: the shared `UpgradeCard` title/badge row is a wrapping flex row (`flex-wrap`, pill `shrink-0 whitespace-nowrap`, title `min-w-0`) so the All-Access pill yields to the next line before overlapping the title, and the home latest-badge card is a real wouter `Link` to `/progress`. Both are pinned by `src/test/home-badge-card.test.tsx`.

**Item 4 (Chai stat cell affordance).** `StatCell` in `home.tsx`: when `onClick` is present the cell (already a real `motion.button` with `type="button"` since the wallet polish pass) now also gets `whileTap` scale feedback reusing the page's existing `PASS_PRESS_SCALE` constant, gated on `useReducedMotion`, and the label gains a trailing `ChevronRight` glyph at `opacity-60` (lucide, same set as the row icons) inside an inline-flex span. Static cells render the label exactly as before and never get tap feedback or the chevron.

**Vite dev-server note.** Implementing the real Toaster made Vite optimize `@radix-ui/react-toast` mid-session; the optimize reload crashed the dev workflow once (`@replit/vite-plugin-runtime-error-modal` ENOENT on `/@react-refresh`) and one stale-chunk "Invalid hook call" appeared in the browser console. A workflow restart healed both; they are one-time dep-optimization artifacts, not code errors.

### Splash v2: layered motion boot with ready-signal hold (August 3, 2026)

**Web (artifacts/gujarati-coach).** brand-splash.tsx rebuilt. All splash art enters ONLY through the exported `SPLASH_V2_ASSETS` map (paths only, five slots: carriage, boloWingRaised, boloWingLowered, steamPuffA, steamPuffB); the owner swaps final art by editing paths, zero code changes. Current values are placeholders: `public/splash/carriage.svg` (hand-authored simple shapes, brand train palette, a TRUE transparent center window aperture cut with an evenodd path at viewBox fractions x 36.667 percent, y 24.286 percent, w 26.667 percent, h 34.286 percent, mirrored in the component's `WINDOW` constant), `public/splash/steam-a.svg` and `steam-b.svg` (white circle puffs), and the canonical mascot PNGs `mascot-cheer.png` (wing raised frame) and `mascot-wave.png` (wing lowered frame), whole images per the canonical mascot rule. Composition layers: steam puffs on offset rise loops, Bolo composited into the window by the app layer inside an overflow-hidden clip box (canon: Bolo is never baked into scenes), carriage img on top so the aperture frames the bird. Wing frames alternate on a steps() swap (`--splash2-flap-cycle`). Bolo's size follows the composition; this supersedes the old fixed mascot clamp treatment and closes the banked 78 percent debt item by design.

**Ready-signal hold.** useBrandSplash now HOLDS until home's real readiness signal (`useListCategories` isLoading flipping false, the same first-paint condition as before) and releases with the existing 260ms opacity fade over the already-painted skeleton/content (no blank flash). There is NO fixed-duration primary timer anymore; `--splash-max-hold` (8000ms, :root tuning block) is a failsafe cap so a stuck signal can never trap the user. Reduced motion now MOUNTS the fully composed STATIC frame (no animate-splash2-* classes, single wing frame) instead of skipping; play-once latch, route qualification, warm-cache skip, and the error-boundary fall-through are unchanged. Contract pinned in src/test/home-brand-splash.test.tsx.

**Boot gap (item 4).** Web: index.html carries a boot `<style>` painting the document background with the splash backdrop gradient (literal HSL values of from-primary via hsl(220,70,52) to-secondary) so the first paint can never white-flash; a `body:has(> #root:empty)` rule keeps the gradient visible until React mounts, and body paints its normal background afterwards. RULE: if the overlay gradient in brand-splash.tsx changes, update index.html's boot style in the same commit. Native (bolo-mobile): the old `splash.png` had the cream #fffdf0 background BAKED IN (1024x1024 RGB, no alpha) while the app's first frame paints colors.background #F8FAFC, which is the native boot gap. app.json's splash block now uses the transparent `./assets/images/mascot/mascot-wave.png` over backgroundColor #F8FAFC (resizeMode contain) so the native-to-app handoff is color-seamless in light mode. Native behavior is config-asserted only, unverifiable in this workspace; device verification rides the store parity pass. Known residuals: dark-mode users still get a light native splash (static Expo config) and a light web boot gradient; the old assets/images/splash.png remains on disk, unreferenced.
